### [1] [From Static Lindblad Stabilizability to Robust Physical Control Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02362)
*Yibin Wang,Ruifeng Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对不完美控制下的耗散量子动力学，给出可检验的稳定化判据，覆盖脉冲、空闲区间与背景动力学，并给出收敛、误差与物理代价的界；进一步以有限库搜索在四能级级联模型中构造出显式制备时间界的控制序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散动力学（dissipative dynamics）可在不依赖可靠有限控制序列的情况下稳定量子目标态，但实际控制存在校准与计时等不完美性，因此需要可检验的条件来判断此类稳定化在误差下是否仍然成立。

Method: 采用目标偏离的公共 Lyapunov 度量（Lyapunov measure），要求在给定不确定集上、计入实现误差后收缩界（contraction bound）一致小于 1；分析同时包含脉冲、空闲区间与所有并行作用的背景动力学，并比较“保持目标子空间（target subspace）”的合格路径以获得长时间误差界；方法还支持有限库搜索（finite-library search）。

Result: 若所有允许路径在每次操作中均保持目标子空间，则重复循环可从任意初态收敛到该子空间，即使误差逐周期变化；在有界泄漏（bounded leakage）下给出显式长时间误差界；两个单量子比特反例说明稳定的平均值或由幺正共轭（unitary conjugation）得到的稳定生成元并不保证路径条件；对含有限脉冲、校准与计时误差、退相位（dephasing）与有界泵浦（bounded pumping）的四能级级联模型，搜索得到带显式制备时间界的序列，保证任意初态在制备结束时及后续循环中与目标的迹距离（trace distance）至多为 0.1。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了在不完美控制下认证耗散稳定化的可检验框架，将路径保持条件与收缩界、泄漏界及物理代价联系起来，并通过有限库搜索给出可构造的、具明确性能保证的控制序列。

Abstract: Dissipative dynamics can stabilize a quantum target without providing a reliable sequence of finite control operations. We derive checkable conditions for implementing such stabilization with imperfect controls, with bounds on convergence, errors during operations, and physical cost. The analysis includes pulses, idle intervals, and all simultaneously acting background dynamics. Using a common Lyapunov measure of deviation from the target, we require a contraction bound that remains uniformly below one over the prescribed uncertainty set after accounting for implementation errors. If all allowed paths preserve the target subspace throughout each operation, repeated cycles converge to that subspace from every initial state, even when errors vary between cycles. Comparison with these certified target-preserving paths gives an explicit long-time error bound under bounded leakage. Two single-qubit counterexamples show why a stabilizing average or a stabilizing generator obtained by unitary conjugation does not ensure these path conditions. The certification approach also supports a finite-library search. For a specified four-level cascade model with finite pulses, calibration and timing errors, dephasing, and bounded pumping, this search yields a sequence with an explicit preparation-time bound. For every initial state, it guarantees trace distance at most $0.1$ from the target at the end of preparation and throughout subsequent cycles.

</details>


### [2] [Quantum Error-Corrected Memories Keep Proper Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02786)
*Sebastian P. Kish,Simon J. Devitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明量子纠错（quantum error correction）不会抹除相对论固有时（proper time）差异：两个沿不同世界线、由局域参考系控制的纠错量子存储器的局域纠错历史可精确分解，固有时差在比对时表现为关系相位旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究量子纠错（quantum error correction）对相对论固有时（proper time）的影响，并连接容错量子信息（fault-tolerant quantum information）与相对论时钟，进而为保存固有时信息及长相干时间测试提供平台。

Method: 方法为构造两个沿不同世界线（worldlines）运动、由局域参考系（local references）控制的纠错量子存储器，分析其局域纠错历史与固有时差的关系，并考察在局域参考操作及编码所允许的离散相位下的关系演化。

Result: 结果显示：局域纠错历史可精确地按固有时差分解；在局域参考操作中该相位旋转对任何局域纠错症状（error syndrome）均不可见；在编码允许的离散相位处，最终关系演化精确表现为确定性逻辑门，包括在π/2偏移处的 S 型 Clifford 门（$S$-type Clifford gate）。

Conclusion: 结论是量子纠错不会抹除时间膨胀（time dilation），而是将其保留为关系相位；受保护的量子存储器（protected quantum memories）可作为保存固有时信息并进行涉及相对论固有时的量子演化长相干测试的平台。

Abstract: What does quantum error correction do to relativistic proper time? We show that when two error-corrected quantum memories follow distinct worldlines and are controlled by local references, their local correction histories factor exactly from the proper-time difference between them. Error correction therefore does not erase time dilation: when the memories are compared, the accumulated difference appears as a relational phase rotation. During locally referenced operation this rotation is invisible to every local error syndrome; at discrete phases admitted by a code, the final relational evolution instead returns exactly as a deterministic logical gate, including an $S$-type Clifford gate at a $π/2$ excursion. These results connect fault-tolerant quantum information with relativistic clocks and establish protected quantum memories as a platform for preserving proper-time information and for long-coherence tests of quantum evolution involving relativistic proper time.

</details>


### [3] [Strict non-tightness of the level-3 quantum bootstrap for a natural three-dimensional Hamiltonian, at an excited level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03362)
*Daniel Keren,Ye Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在三维耦合双势阱（double well）哈密顿量上构造了一个显式反例：一个满足第3层级本征态自举（level-3 eigenstate bootstrap）全部约束、矩矩阵（moment matrix）严格正定、却对某非负多项式赋负值的线性泛函，证明第3层级松弛（relaxation）存在无法由任何量子态实现的内部点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自举（bootstrap）方法通过矩矩阵正定性与多项式非负性约束逼近量子本征态，但层级松弛的内部点是否都对应真实量子态尚不明确。该研究旨在检验层级松弛的紧性（tightness），即是否存在满足全部约束却非物理的伪解。

Method: 针对三维耦合双势阱 H=½|p|²+2Σ(q_i²−1)²−Σ_{i<j}q_i²q_j²，在基态与第一激发能级上构造六次多项式空间上的线性泛函，验证其满足第3层级本征态自举的全部约束并具严格正定矩矩阵；所有代数步骤以精确有理数证书给出，唯一数值部分（本征函数的谱包络）采用严格球算术（ball arithmetic）完成。

Result: 所构造的泛函虽满足第3层级全部约束且矩矩阵严格正定，却对某非负多项式赋予负值，因而属于第3层级松弛的内部点，但不能由任何量子态实现。

Conclusion: 该反例源于一个各层级普遍成立的事实：本征态行（eigenstate rows）始终无法触及最高次动量矩（top-degree momentum moments）。在第3层级且严格可行点存在的情况下，这意味着矩矩阵的相应分块不携带关于哪些能量允许存在可行解的信息，表明层级松弛在有限层级下不具备紧性。

Abstract: For the coupled three-dimensional double well $H=\tfrac12|p|^2+2\sum_i(q_i^2-1)^2-\sum_{i<j}q_i^2q_j^2$ we exhibit, at the first excited energy level (and also at the ground state), a linear functional on polynomials of degree $\le 6$ that satisfies every constraint of the standard level-3 eigenstate bootstrap, has a \emph{strictly} positive definite moment matrix, and nevertheless assigns a negative value to a nonnegative polynomial. It is therefore an interior point of the level-3 relaxation that is realized by no quantum state. The construction reflects a fact that holds at every level: the eigenstate rows never reach the top-degree momentum moments. At level 3, where strictly feasible points exist, this means that the corresponding block of the moment matrix carries no information about which energies admit one. Every algebraic ingredient is an exact rational certificate, and the only numerical ingredients (spectral enclosures of an eigenfunction) are carried out in rigorous ball arithmetic.

</details>


### [4] [Geometric Aspects of Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03446)
*Lucio De Simone,Lorenzo Capra,Arthur Vesperini,Leonardo Rossi,Loris Di Cairano,Roberto Franzosi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作从几何视角研究多量子比特态的纠缠，基于投影希尔伯特空间上由 Fubini–Study 度规（Fubini–Study metric）诱导的黎曼结构（Riemannian structure），提出"纠缠距离"（entanglement distance, ED）这一几何度量，并在两量子比特纯态情形下证明其与 concurrence 及纠缠熵（entropy of entanglement）的单调函数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠（quantum entanglement）是量子信息论的基础资源，但其一般性的刻画与量化仍具挑战，多体系统（multipartite systems）尤为困难。本文旨在借助几何工具为纠缠度量提供统一的几何解释。

Method: 在多量子比特量子态投影希尔伯特空间（projective Hilbert space）上，利用 Fubini–Study 度规的局域酉不变性（local-unitary invariance），将纠缠刻画为局域操作所生成的平方 Fubini–Study 距离之和在局域意义上无法最小化的阻碍，由此定义纠缠距离（ED）。

Result: 给出了纯多量子比特态下 ED 的性质，并分析其在局域操作与经典通信（LOCC）下的行为；显示 ED 在限定条件下可重现已有纠缠度量：对两量子比特纯态，ED 分别为 concurrence 与纠缠熵的单调函数。

Conclusion: ED 为 bipartite 标准纠缠度量提供了清晰的几何解释，同时表明此类对应关系在两量子比特情形之外存在局限。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is a fundamental resource in quantum information theory, yet its general characterization and quantification remain challenging, especially in multipartite systems. In this work we investigate entanglement from a geometric perspective, focusing on the Riemannian structure induced by the Fubini--Study metric on the projective Hilbert space of multi-qubit quantum states. By exploiting the local-unitary invariance of this metric, we derive the entanglement distance (ED), a geometric measure that quantifies entanglement as an obstruction to locally minimizing the sum of squared Fubini--Study distances generated by local operations.
  We analyze the properties of ED for pure multi-qubit states and discuss its behavior under local operations and classical communication. In particular, we show that ED reproduces established entanglement measures in well-defined and restricted settings. For pure states of two qubits, ED reduces to an exact monotone function of the concurrence and, independently, to an explicit monotone function of the entropy of entanglement. These results provide a clear geometric interpretation of standard bipartite entanglement measures within the present framework, while highlighting the limitations of such correspondences beyond the two-qubit case.

</details>


### [5] [Discriminating Lindbladian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03629)
*Robert Salzmann,Ludovico Lami,Martin B. Plenio,Susana F. Huelga*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由 Lindblad 生成元驱动的量子马尔可夫动力学（quantum Markovian dynamics）的判别极限，引入总暴露时间与时间分辨率（temporal resolution）两个资源维度，用 Stein 指数刻画最优类型-II 错误指数，并借助量子 Zeno 协议（quantum Zeno protocol）证明时间分辨率是可作为判别资源的物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子假设检验（quantum hypothesis testing）此前主要针对量子态与量子信道展开，而由 Lindblad 生成元描述的连续时间马尔可夫动力学判别问题尚缺乏对总暴露时间与时间分辨率作用的系统刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，明确该场景下的基本极限及其代数判定条件。

Method: 允许自适应地选择演化时长，并允许在演化之间施加任意瞬时控制（instantaneous controls）。将最优类型-II 错误指数（Stein exponent）刻画为量子相对熵（quantum relative entropy）的最大瞬时增长率，并基于 Lindblad 生成元给出其有限性的代数判据。通过量子 Zeno 协议（quantum Zeno protocol）构造达到最优的判别方案，以此分析时间分辨率对错误概率的影响。

Result: 指数无穷当且仅当在足够大的有限总时间下、对任意正的类型-I 容忍度，类型-II 错误概率可趋于零。部分动力学存在有限协议使类型-II 错误为零，另一部分则要求时间控制不断细化：后者在固定足够大总时间下，随允许的最小演化时长（分辨率时间）趋于零而错误消失，但在任意固定正分辨率时间下有正下界。当零假设为单位半群（identity semigroup）时，无穷渐近错误指数有更强结论——在固定有限总暴露时间内，类型-II 错误可任意小且类型-I 错误严格为零，这在对应经典情形中不可能实现。量子比特上单位半群与广义振幅阻尼（generalised amplitude damping）的判别给出了三种情形的实例。

Conclusion: 时间分辨率（temporal resolution）是量子动力学判别中的一种可用资源：在某些情形下，只有让最小演化时长趋于零才能实现类型-II 错误的完全消除。此外，量子判别在单位半群情形下表现出超越经典对应物的能力，即有限总时间内同时实现类型-I 错误严格为零与类型-II 错误任意小。

Abstract: The fundamental task of quantum hypothesis testing, which consists in discriminating quantum objects, has been extensively studied for quantum states and channels. Here, we investigate the fundamental limits of discriminating an arbitrary pair of quantum Markovian dynamics generated by Lindbladians, focusing on the roles of total exposure time and temporal resolution. We allow adaptive choices of evolution durations and arbitrary instantaneous controls between them. We characterise the optimal type-II error exponent (Stein exponent) as the largest instantaneous increase of quantum relative entropy, and give an algebraic criterion for its finiteness in terms of the Lindbladians. Remarkably, an infinite exponent is equivalent to a vanishing type-II error probability at sufficiently large finite total time, for every positive type-I tolerance. While some dynamics admit a finite protocol with zero type-II error, others require increasingly fine temporal control. For the latter, the error vanishes as the minimum allowed evolution duration, or resolution time, tends to zero at fixed sufficiently large total time, but remains bounded away from zero at every fixed positive resolution time. We achieve this using quantum Zeno protocols, establishing temporal resolution as a resource for discrimination. When the null hypothesis is the identity semigroup, an infinite asymptotic error exponent has a stronger consequence: at a fixed finite total exposure time, the type-II error can be made arbitrarily small while keeping the type-I error exactly zero. This is impossible in the corresponding classical setting. Discriminating the identity semigroup from generalised amplitude damping on a qubit provides examples of all three regimes: a finite asymptotic error exponent, zero type-II error attainable at positive resolution time, and vanishing type-II error requiring the resolution time to tend to zero.

</details>


### [6] [Modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality for 1D non-commuting Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03683)
*Ángela Capel,David Pérez-García,Matteo Scandi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在非对易哈密顿量（non-commuting Hamiltonian）情形下证明了修正的对数Sobolev不等式（modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality, MLSI），首次在非对易区域（non-commuting regime）给出此类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对一维有限自旋链上的有限程（finite-range）非对易哈密顿量，研究热浴动力学（heat-bath dynamics）等准局部（quasi-local）Gibbs采样器的混合速度，此前MLSI在非对易情形下尚无证明。

Method: 证明结合了相对熵的弱准因子化（weak quasi-factorisation of relative entropy）、关联衰减估计（decay-of-correlation estimates）、一致条件局部间隙（uniform conditional local gap），以及条件熵与二次能量间的缓冲比较（buffered comparison）；所得有界熵亏损（bounded entropy defect）借助全局间隙（global gap）消除。

Result: 对热浴动力学、准局部Gibbs采样器及正则化热浴动力学（regularised heat-bath dynamics）证明了常数为Ω(1/log n)的MLSI，进而给出相对熵与迹范数下趋向Gibbs态的快速混合，混合时间为O(log n·log(n/ε))。相互作用可空间非均匀（spatially inhomogeneous）。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是非对易区域中MLSI的首个证明，为相关采样器的快速混合提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Given a finite-range, non-commuting Hamiltonian on a 1D chain of finite spins, we prove a modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality with constant $Ω(1/\log n)$ for the heat-bath dynamics, a quasi-local Gibbs sampler, and for the regularised heat-bath dynamics. This implies rapid mixing of both samplers towards the Gibbs state in relative entropy and trace norm, with mixing times of order $\mathcal{O}(\log n\cdot \log(n/ε))$. The interactions may be spatially inhomogeneous. The proof combines weak quasi-factorisation of relative entropy, decay-of-correlation estimates, a uniform conditional local gap, and a buffered comparison between conditional entropy and quadratic energy. The resulting bounded entropy defect is removed using the global gap. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first proof of MLSI in the non-commuting regime.

</details>


### [7] [How I Learned to Stop Worrying and Love the Redfield Equation: Completely-Positive Resummation of Non-Markovian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03676)
*Greg Kaplanek,Jason Pollack*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了 Redfield 方程（含时系数，TCL2）与对同一二阶微扰映射的 Magnus 指数重求和（完全正定保迹，CPTP），并构造 CPTP 预解重求和，说明重求和非唯一性；给出弱耦合判据以保证 Redfield 演化即使非马尔可夫仍接近 CPTP 映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Redfield 方程是开放量子系统（open quantum system）动力学最简单的微观微扰描述之一，但其近似地位微妙：微扰论按系统-环境耦合逐阶确定约化动力学，而求解 Redfield 方程得到的特定重求和并不保证完全正定性（complete positivity）。因此需要厘清其可靠性条件。

Method: 将 Redfield 演化与同一二阶微扰映射的指数化（Magnus）重求和进行对比，后者在广泛物理条件下为 CPTP；同时构造一种简单的 CPTP 预解（resolvent）重求和以展示重求和的非唯一性。

Result: Redfield 与 Magnus 方案在二阶一致，但在更高阶通过时间排序（time-ordering）修正而不同。给出一个弱耦合判据，保证 Redfield 演化即使非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）仍接近 CPTP 映射；并在有限维（qubit）系统的两个例子中演示该判据。

Conclusion: 结果阐明了 Redfield 演化在何种情况下、以及为何即使高度非 Lindblad 型（non-Lindbladian）仍可保持可靠性。

Abstract: The Redfield equation with time-dependent coefficients (or TCL2 equation) gives one of the simplest perturbative descriptions of open quantum system dynamics derived from a microscopic theory. Its status as an approximation is nevertheless subtle: while perturbation theory fixes the reduced dynamics order-by-order in the system-environment coupling, solving the Redfield equation yields a particular resummation that need not preserve complete positivity. Here we compare Redfield evolution with an exponentiated (Magnus) resummation of the same second-order perturbative map, which is completely positive and trace preserving (CPTP) for a broad class of physical conditions. We also construct a simple CPTP resolvent resummation, illustrating the non-uniqueness of resummations of the same perturbative map. The Redfield and Magnus prescriptions agree to second order but differ through time-ordering corrections at higher orders. We exhibit a weak-coupling criterion which guarantees that Redfield evolution, even when non-Markovian, remains close to a CPTP map. We illustrate the criterion in two examples, focusing on finite-dimensional (qubit) systems. Our results clarify when, and why, Redfield evolution can remain reliable even when it is highly non-Lindbladian.

</details>


### [8] [Exact Recovery for Non-Abelian Surface Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03677)
*Alison Warman,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn,Sakura Schafer-Nameki*

Main category: quant-ph

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Fermionic non-Gaussianity is a key resource for universal quantum computation, and its behavior in quantum many-body systems has attracted growing interest. These results provide a computational tool that quantitatively evaluates many-body fermionic non-Gaussianity by accounting for higher-order correlations.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the physical requirements to reach the ultimate limits of ground-state metrology -- i.e., the estimation of an unknown parameter $θ$ of an $N$-body Hamiltonian via measurements on its ground state -- using many-body probes featuring either short-range or long-range interactions. In contrast, we find that all-to-all interacting models can achieve optimality even in gapped regimes. Furthermore, reaching the dynamical bound is tightly related to the ground-state preparation time; we demonstrate that its optimal, inverse-gap profile can be obtained via local adiabatic protocols. By analyzing paradigmatic systems -- the transverse-field Ising chain, the XXZ chain, a squeezing Hamiltonian, and the two-mode Bose-Hubbard model -- we identify specific many-body probes capable of approaching both limits. Overall, our results provide a theoretical basis for the design of (local) many-body ground-state sensors operating at the ultimate limits of quantum precision.

Abstract: Correlation functions are central to understanding the behavior of quantum systems, with their spectra giving direct access to quasiparticle and collective excitations. Result: 混合酉信道满足该不等式，一般酉信道则不满足；到$\mathbb{M}_d$任意酉$C^*$-子代数的迹保持条件期望（trace-preserving conditional expectation）是Erdős信道，且在相同Choi秩信道中局部孤立；Choi秩2的Erdős信道有显式形式（至多相差酉等价）；与经典情形不同，Erdős信道的酉等价类不可数；量子比特情形下恰有四个酉等价类，由恒等信道与Pauli信道的均匀平均代表。

Abstract: Unital quantum channels are the noncommutative analogues of doubly stochastic matrices. In 1959, Marcus and Ree showed that the squared Frobenius norm of a doubly stochastic matrix is at most its maximal diagonal sum. We prove a quantum version of this inequality for mixed unitary channels on $\mathbb{M}_d$ and give an example showing that it fails for general unital channels. As in the classical case, we call the channels attaining equality as Erdős channels.
  We show that the trace-preserving conditional expectation onto any unital $C^*$-subalgebra of $\mathbb{M}_d$ is an Erdős channel. It is also locally isolated among channels of the same Choi rank. Next, we introduce quantum RCDS channels. These are the noncommutative analogue of doubly stochastic matrices whose supported permutations all have the same diagonal sum. We prove a structure theorem for them and use it to characterize the quantum RCDS Erdős channels. We give an explicit form, up to unitary equivalence, for the Erdős channels of Choi rank $2$, and further show that there are uncountably many unitary equivalence classes of Erdős channels unlike the classical case. We also characterize the Erdős channels among mixed Weyl-unitary channels of any Choi rank.

Finally, restricting to the diagonal channels, our results recover the Marcus--Ree inequality, the structure of RCDS doubly stochastic matrices, and the characterization of Erdős.

As special Kähler geometries, domestic geometries carry formal brane amplitudes: when the domestic geometry describes the supersymmetric low-energy limit of a consistent quantum theory of gravity, its formal brane amplitudes have the right properties to be actual branes. We also derive a sharp lower bound on the net energy \(E_{\rm net}\), with a coefficient larger by a factor of two than the value quoted by Abdolrahimi and Page [Phys. Rev. D 92, 083005 (2015)] and, in the QNEC-saturating sector, we obtain a sharp lower bound on the negative energy \(E_-\). The constants are sharp as infima over smooth conformal vacua. We determine the entropy profiles that minimize \(E_+\) for general QNEC states and the one that minimizes \(E_-\) in the saturating sector. Finally, for an excursion containing an infinite sequence of small sub-excursions within a finite duration, we derive a necessary condition for the total positive energy to remain finite, which is stronger than requiring the entropy to have finite total variation.

Conclusion: 论文勾勒了 de Sitter 空间可能纳入该图景的路径，其中观测者（observers）扮演自然角色。整体而言，该工作将粘合一致性提升为量子引力理论背后的普适体原理。

Abstract: We argue that BF theory with $SO(d+1,1)$ gauge group provides a SymTFT ''skeleton'' for building AdS$_{d+1}$/CFT$_d$ pairs via gluing consistency as the bootstrap principle, loosely analogous to how compact Chern-Simons theory is a TQFT skeleton for rational CFTs. This is a non-perturbative $bulk$ framework for quantum gravity. The bootstrap is formulated as presentation independence of physical amplitudes within a universal, defect-closed BF sewing calculus, which includes Wilson lines, codimension-two monodromy defects, and their junctions. The flat BF connection encodes the conformal geometry of the boundary, while Wilson-line path integrals construct candidate quantum-gravity microstates and charge sectors. When the bootstrapped spectrum has a stress tensor, Ward identities naturally connect it to the $B$-field, a relation we call generalized simplicity. In large-$N$, large-gap regimes admitting a gravitational EFT, this motivates the introduction of an effective notion of the $B$-field. In the Einstein limit, this becomes the ''simplicity'' relation used in spin-foam models. Conventional spin-foam models reach gravity by constraining BF theory. We suggest instead that BF theory is the Platonic sewing skeleton, from which theories are to be bootstrapped. Despite the topological nature of the skeleton, perturbative local bulk fields can emerge via Wilson-towers of multi-trace primaries, reproducing their one-loop determinants. We note that the BF Fourier kernel is a suggestive higher-dimensional analogue of the modular-$S$ structure. Because the bulk gluing principle in itself does not require a boundary description, the framework is conceptually broader than AdS/CFT. We sketch how de Sitter space may fit into this picture, with a natural role for observers. Our approach elevates gluing consistency to a universal bulk principle underlying theories of quantum gravity.

</details>

*Arindam Bhattacharjee,Muktajyoti Saha,M. V. S. Saketh*

TL;DR: 本文研究近极值（near extremality）四维渐近平坦 Reissner-Nördstrom 黑洞的准正规模（quasinormal modes）与潮汐响应（tidal responses），并考察近视界量子修正对这些可观测量的影响。基于近视界有效 Schwarzian 作用量，计算黑洞与标量粒子的 $2 \to 2$ 散射振幅，得到准正规模谱与静态 Love 数，发现其与经典预期不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极值黑洞的近视界动力学通常由有效 Schwarzian 作用量（Schwarzian action）与边界规范模（boundary gauge modes）描述，但其对潮汐响应等可观测量的量子修正尚未充分探讨。本文旨在揭示近视界量子效应对这些可观测量的非平凡改变。

Method: 将有效 Schwarzian 理论与视界外的无质量标量场（massless scalar field）耦合，利用该构造计算黑洞与标量粒子的 $2 \to 2$ 散射过程。通过散射振幅在复频率上的极点提取准正规模谱，并由散射振幅的弹性极限（elastic limit）评估静态 Love 数。

Result: 在领头阶恢复了近极值下预测的零阻尼模（zero damped modes）。静态 Love 数与经典预期相反，表现为非零。极接近极值时行为是解析的，但当近极值能量足够大时，出现对能量的对数依赖（logarithmic dependence），且该依赖无法通过重整化方案（renormalisation scheme）的选择消除。

Conclusion: 近视界量子效应非平凡地改变了近极值黑洞的潮汐响应，体现为静态 Love 数的非零值及对数能量依赖，表明量子修正对这些可观测量具有不可忽略的物理效应。

Abstract: In this paper we discuss the quasinormal modes and the tidal responses of a four-dimensional asymptotically flat Reissner-Nördstrom black hole near extremality and investigate the nontrivial near-horizon quantum corrections of these observables. The near-horizon dynamics of near extremal black holes are generally governed by an effective Schwarzian action coupled to boundary gauge modes. We use this description and couple this effective theory with a massless scalar field outside. Using this setup we compute $2 \to 2$ scattering process of a black hole and a scalar particle. The poles of the scattering amplitude at complex frequencies give us the quasinormal mode spectrum and we recover the predicted zero damped modes near extremality at leading order. Next we evaluate the static Love numbers from the elastic limit of the scattering amplitude. We show that the static Love numbers are non-zero in contrast to the classical expectations. Very close to extremality, the behaviour is analytic, but as the near-extremal energy becomes sufficiently large, we find logarithmic dependence on the energy. This cannot be removed by the choice of renormalisation scheme. Thus near-horizon quantum effects non-trivially change the tidal response of near-extremal black holes.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 引入活性会产生新型形态斑图（morphological patterns），且这些斑图对具体的迁移规则敏感；活性显著改变入侵波特征，提高其传播速度，但超过某一阈值后，过强的活性会破坏入侵过程的波状特性。二维扩展则分析了导致斑图形成的不稳定性（instabilities）。

Conclusion: 活性与增殖的耦合可产生受迁移规则调控的新颖斑图与波传播行为；活性对入侵过程的影响存在非单调性——适度提高传播速度，过度则破坏波状入侵。该最小模型为理解增殖型活性群体中的斑图形成与不稳定性提供了框架。

Abstract: Selective advantage leads to the proliferation of a species into an ecosystem. On the other hand, self-propulsion can lead to dynamic clustering of individuals. To analyze the intricate effects produced by such competing processes, we introduce a minimal model for a proliferating active population, where run-and-tumble migration is combined with a stochastic birth-death process. Beginning with the microscopic dynamics of the particles, we derive the corresponding hydrodynamic equations and validate them through numerical simulations. We demonstrate that the inclusion of activity leads to the emergence of novel morphological patterns, which are sensitive to the specific rules governing migration. Furthermore, we show that activity significantly alters the characteristics of invasion waves, increasing their propagation speed. However, beyond a threshold, excessive activity disrupts the wave-like nature of the invasion process. We also extend our study to two dimensions and analyze the instabilities that lead to pattern formation in such systems.

</details>

Abstract: Hard Magnetic Soft Materials, i.e., elastomers seeded with hard magnetic particles, convert magnetic energy into complex motion and have become central to the design of untethered soft robots, biomedical devices, and, in general, for enabling fast, wireless actuation. Their behavior depends critically on how the surrounding soft matrix deforms and transmits torque to each magnetized inclusion, yet there is no consensus on the correct mapping between macroscopic strain and microscopic motion. Competing models predict conflicting particle rotations and magnetic responses under identical loads. Here, we construct counterexamples that show the key shortcomings in these models, and then establish a first-principles framework that leads to an optimal deformation mapping. The resulting closed-form law unifies conflicting models and shows that they are, in fact, simply different limits of our general approach. Our work permits the rational design of magnetically actuated soft devices and a unified view of deformation in magnetic soft matter, and broadly, in similar architectured materials.

</details>

Abstract: We recently proposed a framework for the steady mass flux of particles through circular apertures [R.~S.~Sharma \textit{et al.}, Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{136}, 068204 (2026)]. Here, we combine experiments and discrete-element simulations of spherical particles discharging through triangular and rectangular apertures to test this framework beyond circular openings. Despite changes in aperture shape, we observe a similar free-fall flux, with a modified relevant transport length. Deviations due to confinement reflect packing modifications, which are explained by geometry. A boundary layer dilation imposed by the aperture edge is shown to be a mechanism for the decrease of flux across various aperture shapes. By integrating the local packing deficit over the opening, we identify a hydraulic diameter $D_h=4A/P$, where $A$ and $P$ are the aperture area and perimeter, respectively, as the leading confinement length. This length also provides a reference scale, $\sqrt{gD_h}$, for the mean outlet velocity when the aperture is much larger than an individual particle. The same local packing model predicts the field in a lens-shaped aperture without further fitting, while the normalized discharge rates follow a common trend across the tested shapes.

</details>


### [47] [triangulax: Differentiable Simulations with Triangular Meshes across Soft Matter Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02566)
*Samuel Silliman,Qiwei Yu,Nikolas Claussen*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理与生物系统（如泡沫、膜、弹性壳、组织片）可数学描述为二维表面，这些表面承载反应扩散等复杂动力学并发生剧烈变形，因此需要能够处理强变形并支持多物理耦合与参数拟合的模拟工具。

Result: 该方法在膜力学与膜上浓度场耦合的模型中进行了应用，并与精确解及现有软件进行了验证；同时在膜力学、顶点模型（vertex models）以及形状变形弹性体片的逆向设计等多种问题上展示了 triangulax 的能力。

Abstract: Many physical and biological systems -foams, membranes, elastic shells, or tissue sheets- can be mathematically described as 2D surfaces. These surfaces can host complex dynamics, like reaction-diffusion systems, and undergo drastic deformations. Here, we present triangulax, an open-source Python library for simulations and geometry processing that discretizes surfaces using triangular meshes available at https://github.com/nikolas-claussen/triangulax. It combines two features. First, discrete differential geometry provides coordinate-free discretization, which allows simulating strongly deforming surfaces. Second, the library is built on the machine-learning framework JAX, so mesh-based energies and entire simulations can be differentiated automatically (including simulations with stochastic forces or topological modifications). This simplifies multi-physics simulations by computing forces automatically and allows fitting model parameters to experimental data or design objectives. We implement an algorithm to preserve mesh quality in strongly deforming surfaces and apply it to a model of membrane mechanics coupled to an on-membrane concentration field. We validate our approach against exact solutions and existing software, and demonstrate triangulax across a range of problems, including membrane mechanics, vertex models, and inverse design of a shape-morphing elastomer sheet.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在刻画分子有限尺寸在近外电荷的非线性区域中所起的作用，尤其是平均场电势的非单调性与极性溶剂化对外电荷符号的依赖性，从而检验宏观电动力学中有效电荷对称性是否成立。

Method: 采用平均场（mean-field）框架，处理具有刚性电荷分布的线性分子经库仑耦合产生的相互作用；分析限于外电荷附近的非线性（nonlinear）区域，并在此区域内通过非局域响应（nonlocal response）刻画有限分子尺寸的效应。

Result: 结果显示两点：其一，平均场电势呈非单调（non-monotonic）行为，即同号电荷之间可出现吸引；其二，外电荷诱导的极化（polarization）及其溶剂化结构（solvation structure）依赖于该电荷的符号，违背了宏观电动力学的有效电荷对称性（C-symmetry）。文中识别了若干此类效应的情景，并用自由能（free energy）与熵差（entropy differences）加以表征，同时与不对称电解质（asymmetric electrolytes）中由非线性泊松-玻尔兹曼方程（nonlinear Poisson-Boltzmann equations）描述的类似效应进行比较。

Conclusion: 在近外电荷的非线性区域中，分子有限尺寸带来的非局域响应会破坏平均场电势的单调性与宏观有效电荷对称性，其溶剂化效应具有电荷符号依赖性，且与不对称电解质中非线性泊松-玻尔兹曼描述的现象存在可比性。

Abstract: We study an equilibrium system, where linear molecules with a rigid charge distribution interact via the mean-field generated by the Coulomb coupling. We focus on the nonlinear regime, which is valid close to external charges immersed in the system. The finite molecular size in this regime leads to a nonlocal response at short distances. First, we show that the mean-field potential is non-monotonic, i.e., like charges can attract each other. Second, we show that the polarization induced by an external charge (and hence its solvation structure) depends on the sign of this charge, violating the effective C-symmetry of the macroscopic electrodynamics. We identify several scenarios of this effect, characterize it with the free energy and entropy differences, and compare it with analogous effects in asymmetric electrolytes described by nonlinear Poisson-Boltzmann equations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未缠结聚合物熔体的普适流变性质尚需在保持 Rouse 松弛谱的前提下得到统一描述。该研究将拉伸从各 Rouse 模式的显式状态中外置，以建立兼具取向动力学的本构模型。

Conclusion: 拉伸-取向分解为未缠结聚合物熔体流变行为提供了统一的本构描述，并揭示传统 Cox-Merz 规则未涵盖的弹性贡献。

Abstract: Universal rheological properties of unentangled polymer melts are investigated through the stretch-orientation decomposition of polymer dynamics. By externalizing stretch from the explicit state of each Rouse mode, we construct a constitutive model with orientational kinetics that preserves the Rouse relaxation spectrum. The resulting model yields the observed steady-shear scalings of the shear viscosity and first normal-stress coefficient, predicts a finite second normal-stress coefficient, and approximately reproduces the Cox-Merz and Gleissle-Osaki rules. The derived steady-oscillatory shear correspondence further identifies an elastic contribution, associated with the first normal stress difference, that is absent from the conventional Cox-Merz rule.

</details>

*Shailesh Sathe,Apratim Chatterji*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用复制中新片杆菌染色体的珠-簧聚合物模型（bead-spring polymer model），将瞬时的 ParA-ParB 接触建模为产生 DNA 环与有效熵排斥（effective entropic repulsion）的相互作用，并纳入 ParA 在新极附近的浓度梯度（ParA concentration gradient）以及焓贡献（enthalpic contributions）；进一步加入最小的挤出（extrusion）模型以恢复染色体臂的线性排布。

Result: 该熵排斥驱动染色体分离并产生与实验一致的 oriC 轨迹；该最小框架可定量再现多次实验中不同标记基因座（tagged loci）与复制体（replisomes）的时间依赖空间组织；加入最小挤出模型后，可恢复由 Hi-C 图特征对角线推断的染色体臂线性排布。

Conclusion: 由瞬时 ParA-ParB 结合产生的拓扑修饰引发的有效熵相互作用，是驱动新月柄杆菌染色体分离与时空组织的核心机制；纳入 ToMo 聚合物物理的最小框架即可定量描述实验观测的染色体动态与 Hi-C 特征。

Abstract: We identify an entropy-mediated mechanism underlying the spatiotemporal organization of the chromosome in the bacterial cell Caulobacter crescentus. Effective entropic interactions between replicating DNA segments emerge from their topological modifications of daughter DNA polymers caused by transient ParA-ParB binding. Enthalpic contributions arising from the ParABS system further influence their dynamics. In C. crescentus, chromosome replication and segregation occur concurrently before cell division. The origin of replication oriC of the mother chromosome of C. crescentus is tethered to the old cell pole. Replication generates two daughter oriCs, one of which remains anchored at the old pole. The other oriC is actively transported toward the new pole by the ParABS system of proteins even as replication of the rest of the chain is in progress. How ParABS generates a directed force to transport a daughter oriC to the opposite pole during chromosome replication remains unclear. Furthermore, the two daughter chromosomes get neatly segregated into two halves of the cell. In a bead-spring polymer model of the replicating C. crescentus chromosome, we model transient ParA-ParB contacts that generate DNA loops and an effective entropic repulsion. This repulsion drives chromosome segregation and produces oriC trajectories consistent with experiments, aided by the ParA concentration gradient near the new pole. This minimal framework, incorporating the physics of topologically modified (ToMo) polymers, allows us to quantitatively reproduce the time-dependent spatial organization of different tagged loci and replisomes observed in multiple experiments. Incorporating a minimal model of extrusion also allows us to recover the linear arrangement of the chromosome arms, as is inferred from the characteristic diagonals of Hi-C maps.

</details>


### [51] [Discontinuous buckling from cytoplasmic mechanics in vertex models of epithelia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03239)
*Chandraniva Guha Ray,Pierre A. Haas*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 在最小顶点模型（vertex model）中引入代表细胞质结构受限代价的细胞质贡献后，单层上皮在压缩下的屈曲转变（buckling transition）在中等细胞质模量区间变为不连续，并可由六阶朗道理论（Landau theory）的三临界点（tricritical point）予以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上皮顶点模型主要刻画细胞皮层（cell cortex）与细胞黏附（cell adhesion），而基本忽略了被皮层包裹的细胞质中其他亚细胞结构。该研究旨在揭示这些结构对上皮单层力学行为的影响。

Method: 构建上皮单层的最小顶点模型（minimal vertex model），在单细胞能量中额外加入细胞质贡献项，用以表示细胞质聚合物受限等代价；并通过该模型连续极限下的渐近求解（asymptotic solution）分析屈曲问题。

Result: 模拟显示，在细胞质模量（cytoplasmic modulus）的中间取值范围内，单层受压屈曲转变变为不连续（discontinuous）。连续极限渐近解表明该回归（reentrant）行为对应六阶朗道理论（sixth-order Landau theory）的三临界点。

Conclusion: 超越皮层与细胞黏附的细胞尺度力学（cell-scale mechanics）能够甚至在定性层面改变组织尺度（tissue-scale）的涌现行为。

Abstract: The cell cortex and cell adhesion contribute to the mechanics of epithelial cells and hence tissues. Vertex models of epithelia represent the cell cortex and cell adhesion, but these models mostly ignore the other subcellular structures that compose the cytoplasm surrounded by the cortex. Here, we reveal the mechanical consequences of these structures in a minimal vertex model of an epithelial monolayer, in which the single-cell energy includes a cytoplasmic contribution that represents, e.g., the cost of confinement of cytosolic polymers. Strikingly, our simulations show that the buckling transition of the monolayer under compression becomes discontinuous in an intermediate range of values of the associated cytoplasmic modulus. By asymptotic solution of this buckling problem in the continuum limit of the model, we explain this reentrant behaviour in terms of the tricritical points of a sixth-order Landau theory. Our results thus underline how cell-scale mechanics beyond the cortex and cell adhesion can change emergent, tissue-scale behaviour even qualitatively.

</details>


### [52] [TUR Violation, Minimal Power Fluctuation, and Enhanced Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03366)
*F. Adersh,M. Sahoo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由惯性带电活性粒子构成的“活性磁回转器”（active magneto gyrator），其在二维非对称抛物势、双热浴温差及垂直磁场下运行，展现出对功率-效率权衡、热力学不确定性关系（TUR）及卡诺极限（Carnot limit）的显著违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动布朗回转器（passive Brownian gyrator）受功率-效率权衡严格限制，且TUR约束使其在高效率时功率波动剧烈，接近卡诺界（Carnot bound）时无法使用；因此需要探索能突破这些限制的活性系统。

Method: 构建模型：惯性带电活性粒子（inertial charged active particle）被限制在二维非对称抛物势（two-dimensional asymmetric parabolic potential）中，耦合两个温度不同的热浴（thermal baths），并施加垂直于运动平面的磁场（magnetic field）。在此基础上分析其功率、效率与波动特性。

Result: 活性磁回转器出现对功率-效率权衡规则的显著违反，存在输出功率与效率随负载强度（load strength）同时增加的区间；可在高效率下保持最小功率波动，规避TUR约束；效率甚至可超越卡诺极限并接近100%，且不损害稳定性。

Conclusion: 活性磁回转器可作为设计超越卡诺效率（transcend Carnot efficiency）的随机热机（stochastic heat engines）的可行方案。

Abstract: We investigate the performance characteristics of an active magneto gyrator that consists of an inertial charged active particle confined in a two-dimensional asymmetric parabolic potential, coupled to two thermal baths maintained at two different temperatures and in the presence of a magnetic field applied perpendicular to the plane of motion. In contrast to the passive Brownian gyrator, where the power-efficiency trade-off imposes strict operational limits, the active magneto gyrator exhibits striking violations of this rule, allowing regimes in which both output power and efficiency increase simultaneously with load strength. Moreover, while the Thermodynamic Uncertainty Relation (TUR) predicts that highly efficient engines necessarily produce strongly fluctuating power, rendering the Brownian gyrator unusable as the efficiency approaches the Carnot bound, the active magneto gyrator circumvents this constraint. Specifically, it achieves high efficiency with minimal power fluctuations, thus defying the TUR. Remarkably, the efficiency can even surpass the Carnot limit and approach 100\%, without compromising stability. These results highlight the active magneto gyrator as a promising setup for designing stochastic heat engines that can transcend Carnot efficiency.

</details>

### [53] [A solution to the quantum Yang--Baxter equation associated with a universal two-valued algebraic group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02354)
*Victor M. Buchstaber*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该文由万有对称2-代数二值群的结合性条件构造量子Yang–Baxter方程（quantum Yang–Baxter equation）的谱参数解，其乘法律受系数约束$4k_8-k_4^2+k_2k_6=0$，该关系恰与三维WDVV方程一致；由此在每个含循环向量场的三维Frobenius流形上对应一个谱二值群，并给出9×9正则酉R-矩阵及可积链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用代数结合性条件（而非直接求解）来生成量子Yang–Baxter方程的谱参数解，并揭示其与三维WDVV方程、Frobenius流形（Frobenius manifold）及谱二值群（two-valued group）之间的深层对应关系，从而为可积系统提供统一的代数几何来源。

Method: 方法上，首先以万有对称2-代数二值群的乘法结合性条件出发，构造满足系数关系$4k_8-k_4^2+k_2k_6=0$的谱参数解；随后对任意含单位的代数，用乘法与单位构造双参数谱$R$-算子，并证明其Yang–Baxter缺陷正比于由结合子（associator）张成的显式张量；最后针对切Frobenius代数（tangent Frobenius algebra）导出9×9 R-矩阵，以双椭圆曲线（bielliptic curve）的Abel坐标为谱参数。

Result: 主要结果为：每个带循环向量场的三维Frobenius流形都对应一个谱二值群，其有限分支点为乘法算子特征值；对切Frobenius代数得到正则、酉的9×9 R-矩阵，该矩阵恰在WDVV点满足量子Yang–Baxter方程，谱参数为双椭圆曲线的Abel坐标（其椭圆商即谱曲线），并给出相应的可积链；文中对$A_3$ Frobenius流形作了显式展开。

Conclusion: 结论指出，代数结合性条件可系统导出量子Yang–Baxter方程的谱参数解，Frobenius流形的WDVV结构与谱二值群、可积链之间存在精确对应，且该框架对$A_3$情形可显式实现，为后续推广到更高维或更一般Frobenius流形提供了代数基础。

Abstract: We construct spectral-parameter solutions of the quantum Yang--Baxter equation from the associativity condition of the universal symmetric $2$-algebraic two-valued group, whose multiplication law depends on four coefficients $k_2,k_4,k_6,k_8$ subject to the single relation $4k_8-k_4^2+k_2k_6=0$ -- which coincides with the three-dimensional WDVV equation for Dubrovin's normalised potential. Every three-dimensional Frobenius manifold with a cyclic vector field carries such a spectral two-valued group, whose finite branch points are the eigenvalues of the multiplication operator. For an arbitrary unital algebra we show that a two-parameter spectral $R$-operator built from multiplication and unit has Yang--Baxter defect proportional to an explicit tensor built from the associator; for tangent Frobenius algebras this yields a regular, unitary $9\times9$ $R$-matrix satisfying the quantum Yang--Baxter equation exactly at WDVV points, with spectral parameter the Abel coordinate of a bielliptic curve whose elliptic quotient is the spectral curve, together with an associated integrable chain. The $A_3$ Frobenius manifold is worked out explicitly.

</details>

Abstract: An extension with temporal modulation is presented of the canonical 2 + 1-dimensional solitonic Dym equation as originally introduced by Konopelchenko and Dubrovsky. Application is made of admitted Painleve' II symmetry reduction to derive exact solution to a class of associated 2 + 1-dimensional nonlinear moving boundary problems of generalized Stefan-type.

</details>

Method: 采用交互面版本的十九顶点模型推导新的量子自旋-1链；在各项同性极限下将其与三条不同边界扭曲的 Babujian-Taktahjan 自旋链耦合相对应，并利用模型间的可积结构计算热力学极限下的基态短距离关联函数。

Result: 得到一个新的量子自旋-1链，并给出了各项同性模型在热力学极限下基态短距离关联函数的计算结果。

Conclusion: 十九顶点模型的交互面版本与量子自旋-1链之间存在可积对应关系，该对应关系可用于解析地获取模型的基态短距离关联函数。

In the isotropic limit, the associated spin chain can be seen as three coupled Babujian-Taktahjan spin chains with different boundary twists. </details>

### [56] [Fluctuation-induced magnetoresistance in graphene Hall bars at charge neutrality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02326)
*Anna Grigoreva,Alex Levchenko,Eleftherios Kirkinis,Anton Andreev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在流体力学（hydrodynamic）近似下，针对石墨烯（graphene）电荷中性点附近的电子液体输运，发展了由热涨落（thermal fluctuations）驱动、耦合电荷输运与流体流动从而产生正磁阻（magnetoresistance, MR）的定量理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $T\sim 50-100\ K$ 温区，石墨烯器件中电子-电子碰撞速率可能超过由无序和电子-声子散射导致的动量弛豫（momentum relaxation）速率，使电子液体运动可用流体力学方程描述。此情形下电荷中性点处电流与流体流动解耦，电导率等于电子液体的内禀电导率（intrinsic conductivity）$\sigma_0$，而近期研究表明热涨落诱导的电荷输运与流体流动耦合会增强宏观电导率并产生强正磁阻。本文即针对该涨落机制的磁阻建立定量理论。

Method: 在流体力学近似框架下，围绕电荷中性点发展涨落机制磁阻的定量理论，推导石墨烯霍尔条（Hall bars）中磁阻的解析表达式；同时考察霍尔条几何构型及其相对电流取向的影响，并纳入动量弛豫对磁阻的作用。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料量子电动力学（materials QED）的核心目标之一，是利用工程化腔中的亚波长电磁约束（subwavelength electromagnetic confinement）来定制光-物质相互作用，因此需要可调控的杂化平台。

By magnetically tuning graphene inter-Landau-level transitions (ILTs) into resonance with hBN phonons, we witness broadband, mode-selective strong coupling in the mid-infrared. ### [61] [Extracting the anyon charge from shot noise in complex fractional quantum Hall edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02818)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点接触散粒噪声（shot noise）测量可探测Laughlin分数量子霍尔态中的准粒子电荷，但在多通道边缘中，隧穿噪声与模间平衡化（intermode equilibration）和加热产生的噪声共存，导致测量解释变得复杂，需加以区分。

Method: 构建包含隧穿分配噪声与平衡化诱导加热两类贡献的一般框架，推导四端量子点接触（four-terminal QPC）几何下漏极自相关（auto-correlation）与交叉相关（cross-correlation）的表达式，并应用于ν=2/3 FQH边缘。

Result: 平衡化诱导加热对自相关与交叉相关的影响在参数依赖上不同于本征分配噪声（partition noise）；据此提出两相关量的特定组合，可强烈抑制加热贡献并完整恢复隧穿准粒子电荷。

Conclusion: 该工作为复杂FQH边缘的散粒噪声测量提供了系统性的解释框架，并可推广至更一般的反向传播边缘结构（counterpropagating edge structures）。

Abstract: Shot-noise measurements with a quantum point contact provide a direct probe of the quasiparticle charge in Laughlin fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states. We develop a general framework that incorporates both these contributions and apply it to the paradigmatic $ν=2/3$ FQH edge. We derive expressions for the drain auto- and cross-correlations in a four-terminal QPC geometry and show that equilibration-induced heating affects the auto- and cross-correlations in parametrically different way than the intrinsic partition noise. This distinction permits us to propose a specific combination of the two correlations which strongly suppresses the heating contribution and fully recovers the tunneling quasiparticle charge. Our results thereby provide a systematic framework for interpreting shot-noise measurements in complex FQH edges and can be further extended to more general counterpropagating edge structures.

</details>


### [62] [Topological insulators on complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02888)
*Sunkyu Yu,Xianji Piao,Namkyoo Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究证明真实复杂网络（complex networks）本身可承载拓扑绝缘相（topological insulating phases），并揭示小世界（small-world）网络拓扑绝缘体具有优越鲁棒性与可重构性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有拓扑绝缘体（topological insulators）研究多局限于周期晶格（periodic lattices）及短程跃迁（short-range hopping），虽已拓展至非欧几何、无序结构与长程跃迁，但与复杂网络的结合仍存在空白，故需探索复杂网络能否直接承载拓扑绝缘相。

Method: 提出面向多重拓扑目标的非冲突设计框架（nonconflicting design framework），在高阶 Hofstadter 晶格（Hofstadter lattices）以及规则网络、小世界网络（small-world networks）与随机网络中实现拓扑绝缘体，并采用图论（graph-theoretic）框架进行分析。

Result: 该推广揭示网络特有特征：可达拓扑特征范围扩展；出现由度（degree）驱动的从 Hofstadter 型向 Haldane 型 Chern 绝缘体（Haldane-type Chern insulators）的转变；小世界拓扑绝缘体展现出更优鲁棒性与可重构性。

Conclusion: 图论框架将网络复杂性（network complexity）确立为实现高容量与可重构拓扑绝缘体的资源。

Abstract: The landscape of topological insulators has expanded beyond its traditional domain of periodic lattices with short-range hopping. Related studies have explored topological phenomena under non-Euclidean geometries, disordered structures, and long-range hopping, progressively narrowing the gap between topological phases of matter and complex networks. Here we demonstrate that genuine complex networks, far beyond periodic lattices and their conventional variants, can themselves host topological insulating phases. By developing a nonconflicting design framework for multiple topological objectives, we realize topological insulators in high-degree Hofstadter lattices and across regular, small-world, and random networks. This generalization uncovers distinct network-specific features, including an expanded range of attainable topological characteristics, a degree-dependent transition from Hofstadter-type to Haldane-type Chern insulators, and, most notably, the superior robustness and reconfigurability of small-world topological insulators. Our graph-theoretic framework establishes network complexity as a resource for realizing high-capacity and reconfigurable topological insulators.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用低能两带描述与随机相近似（random-phase approximation, RPA），计算密度响应、态密度、德鲁德权重（Drude weights）、介电函数及低掺杂下的等离激元模式；同时考虑无倾斜体系以及面内与轴向能带倾斜。

Result: 尽管 VNR 与 PTNR 的哈密顿量、赝自旋织构和贝里曲率（Berry-curvature）分布不同，二者线性化色散却一致；涡旋织构使能带交叠因子与量子度规产生环向角依赖，进而修饰电荷响应，并影响不同波矢方向下的介电屏蔽与集体模式。

Conclusion: 结果表明，赝自旋织构在能带色散之外，对三维节线环半金属的集体电荷动力学具有重要的调控作用。

Abstract: We investigate collective charge excitations in three-dimensional vortex nodal-ring semimetals (VNRs), whose pseudospin texture winds around the nodal ring and differs qualitatively from that of the conventional $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric nodal-ring semimetal (PTNR). While plasmon modes in PTNRs have been studied in a companion work, arXiv:2609.35373. Here, we focus on how a VNR's pseudospin texture changes the response compared with the former. Within a low-energy two-band description and the random-phase approximation, we analyse the density response, density-of-states, Drude weights, dielectric function, and plasmon modes at low doping. We consider the untilted system together with in-plane and axial band-tilts. Although the VNR and PTNR have distinct Hamiltonians, pseudospin textures, and Berry-curvature distributions, their linearised energy dispersions coincide. The vortex texture, however, introduces a toroidal-angle dependence in the band-overlap factor and quantum metric, thereby modifying the charge response. We examine how these effects enter the dielectric screening and collective modes for different wavevector orientations. Our results clarify how pseudospin texture, beyond the band dispersion alone, influences collective charge dynamics in three-dimensional nodal-ring semimetals.

</details>


### [64] [Evaluating Chern Topology in Discretized Brillouin Zones Using Bargmann Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03104)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统陈数评估通常依赖参数空间上的连续积分或动力学演化。该工作旨在构建一种可直接在离散化参数空间上运行、且同时保留局域几何信息的有限态描述框架。

Method: 框架以规范不变（gauge-invariant）的态重叠为基础，利用三阶 Bargmann 不变量在离散参数空间上直接计算陈数；并通过相位导出的密度（phase-derived density）与互补的振幅密度（amplitude density）保留局域几何信息及拓扑性质变化的特征。方法还包含针对孤立复合子空间的秩-2（rank-2）推广。

Result: 该构造被应用于 Qi–Wu–Zhang 模型、1/3 与 1/4 通量 Hofstadter 模型、SSH 模型与 Rice–Mele 模型，并涵盖闭环（closed-loop）与参数空间拓扑的应用。结果显示该框架能够从有限态数据中同时组织陈拓扑与局域几何信息。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种紧凑的、基于量子态的描述方式，可在不依赖动力学演化的情况下统一处理陈拓扑与局域几何信息。

Abstract: We develop a finite-state geometric framework based on third-order Bargmann invariants for evaluating Chern numbers and characterizing local quantum-state geometry. Using gauge-invariant state overlaps, the framework evaluates the Chern number directly on discretized parameter spaces while retaining local geometric information through the phase-derived density and the complementary amplitude density, as well as signatures associated with changes in topological character. We apply the construction to the Qi--Wu--Zhang, $1/3$- and $1/4$-flux Hofstadter, SSH, and Rice--Mele models, including a rank-$2$ extension for isolated composite subspaces and applications to closed-loop and parameter-space topology. The resulting framework organizes Chern topology and local geometric information directly from finite-state data, providing a compact state-based description without relying on dynamical evolution.

</details>


### [65] [Beyond Pure Dephasing: Quantum Error Correction in Single Molecules Requires Multiple Spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03318)
*Matteo Mezzadri,Silvia Macedonio,Luca Lepori,Emilio Macaluso,Francesco Albarelli,Richard E. P. Winpenny,Alessandro Chiesa,Stefano Carretta1*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出以多自旋分子（multi-spin molecules）作为容错量子计算（fault-tolerant quantum computing）的可行架构，并引入一种混合编码纠错协议，可同时处理对角与非对角误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算需容错能力，而分子自旋具备误差层级分明、可在合成层面工程化多自旋结构等特性，故被提出作为实现容错的候选平台。同时，非谐系统在非对角误差下相干性损失的问题阻碍了单自旋层面的纠错。

Method: 提出一种处理对角误差与非对角误差（分别对应退相位 dephasing 与弛豫 relaxation）的纠错协议，基于混合编码（hybrid encoding）：将抗退相位单元（dephasing-tolerant units）与实现多量子比特码的多自旋分子相结合，前者抑制主要纯退相位误差，后者处理残余非对角误差。

Result: 借助第一级编码对退相位的巨大抑制，数值结果表明该策略即使每个逻辑单元仅含有限数量自旋亦具备潜在性能。

Conclusion: 多自旋分子可成为容错量子计算的可行架构，混合编码方案能有效应对对角与非对角误差，并在自旋数有限的条件下展现良好潜力。

Abstract: We propose multi-spin molecules as a viable architecture for fault-tolerant quantum computing. To this aim, we introduce a correction protocol handling both diagonal and off-diagonal errors, typically associated with dephasing and relaxation. The scheme is based on a hybrid encoding which combines dephasing-tolerant units suppressing the leading pure dephasing error into a multi-spin molecule implementing a multi-qubit code for residual off-diagonal errors. Our proposal leverages peculiar properties of molecular spins, i.e. the strong hierarchy between different errors and the possibility to engineer multi-spin molecules at the synthetic level. Moreover, it addresses the important issue of the loss of coherences in anharmonic systems subject to off-diagonal errors, which hampers their correction at the single spin level. Thanks to the huge suppression of dephasing by the first-level code, we numerically demonstrate the potential performance of this strategy even with a limited number of spins per logical unit.

</details>

### [66] [The causal diamond of relativistic hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02416)
*L. Gavassino*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文指出通过泰勒展开无隙色散关系 ω(k) 在 k=0 处判断流体力学收敛性的传统做法可能在任意接近流体力学极限处失效，并提出在壳外（off-shell）直接对 constitutive relations（本构关系）进行梯度展开的新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上以泰勒展开 ω(k) 围绕 k=0 的方法考察流体力学（hydrodynamics）收敛性，但该诊断可能在任意接近流体力学极限处失效：声模可在任意小波数处碰撞，使 ω(k) 非解析，而密度与流（fluxes）之间的关系在该处仍解析。

Method: 作者改为在壳外（off-shell，即存在外力时）于 constitutive relations（本构关系）层面直接构造梯度展开（gradient expansion）。针对具有弛豫谱的因果理论（causal theories with relaxational spectra），建立了收敛区域 |iω|+w|ik|<1/τ_g，其中 w 为最大信息速度、1/τ_g 为非流体力学能隙（nonhydrodynamic gap）；在洛伦兹型（Lorentzian）{iω,ik} 平面上该区域为因果菱形（causal diamond）。

Result: 在 {iω,ik} 平面上收敛区为因果菱形，其边界标记了首次可能遭遇非流体力学模的位置；超出该区域后，解析延拓（analytic continuation）可通过重求和本构关系（resummed constitutive relations）的奇点揭示非流体力学谱。

Conclusion: 流体力学对流体力学之外的自由度是有感知的，但该信息仅在壳外（off-shell）才完全可及；这一结论表明判断流体力学收敛性需脱离传统在壳色散关系诊断。

Abstract: The convergence of hydrodynamics is commonly investigated by Taylor-expanding the gapless dispersion relations $ω(k)$ around $k=0$. We show that this diagnostic can fail arbitrarily close to the hydrodynamic limit: sound modes can collide at arbitrarily small wave number, rendering $ω(k)$ nonanalytic, while the relationship between densities and fluxes remains analytic there. We instead formulate the gradient expansion off-shell (i.e. in the presence of external forces), directly at the level of the constitutive relations. For causal theories with relaxational spectra, we establish convergence for $|iω|+w|ik|<1/τ_g$, where $w$ is the maximal information speed and $1/τ_g$ the nonhydrodynamic gap. On the naturally Lorentzian $\{iω,ik\}$ plane, this region is a causal diamond, whose boundary marks the first possible encounter with nonhydrodynamic modes. Beyond it, analytic continuation reveals the nonhydrodynamic spectrum through singularities of the resummed constitutive relations. Thus, hydrodynamics is aware of the degrees of freedom beyond hydrodynamics, but this information becomes fully accessible only off-shell.

</details>


### [67] [Ab initio Green's function theory of superfluid neutron matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03507)
*F. Marino,W. G. Jiang,C. Barbieri,G. Colò*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文采用非微扰 Gorkov 代数图解构造方法，对均匀中子物质的状态方程与单粒子谱函数进行统一从头描述，首次量化超流配对能隙对多体截断的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星内壳层低密度区的中子物质呈超流态，影响星体冷却等宏观现象；但现有研究受限于不可控的多体近似（many-body approximations），缺乏精确的定量理解。

Method: 使用非微扰的 Gorkov 代数图解构造方法（Gorkov algebraic diagrammatic construction method），对均匀中子物质从稀薄区到饱和密度进行统一从头计算，同时处理动力学关联与配对关联。

Result: 在激发谱中识别出超流的清晰微观特征，并基于现实手性哈密顿量（chiral Hamiltonians）量化配对能隙，首次估计其对多体截断（many-body truncation）的敏感性。

Conclusion: 对动力学与配对关联的稳健处理，为中子星相关区域内的电弱过程（electroweak processes）研究开辟了路径。

Abstract: At the low densities encountered in the inner crust of neutron stars, neutron matter becomes superfluid, directly influencing macroscopic phenomena such as star cooling. However, a precise quantitative understanding of this system has been hindered by uncontrolled many-body approximations. In this letter, we exploit the nonperturbative Gorkov algebraic diagrammatic construction method to provide a unified ab initio description of the equation of state and the single-particle spectral functions of homogeneous neutron matter, spanning from the dilute regime to saturation density. We identify clear microscopic signatures of superfluidity in the excitation spectrum and quantify the pairing gaps associated with realistic chiral Hamiltonians, estimating for the first time their sensitivity to the many-body truncation. Our robust handling of both dynamical and pairing correlations paves the way for exploring electroweak processes across the regimes relevant to neutron stars.

</details>
