### [68] [Thickness-Controlled Ferroelectric-to-Ionic Transport in CuCrP2S6 at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02380)
*Subhashree Chatterjee,Abhishek Bajgain,Rabindra Basnet,Ramesh C. Budhani*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在超薄范德华（van der Waals, vdW）化合物 CuCrP2S6（CCPS）中，铁电性（ferroelectricity）、光电导（photoconductivity）与离子输运（ionic transport）在室温下共存，但铁电性与离子迁移可能因共享同一原子来源（铜离子）而相互拮抗。研究通过静电与光辅助界面能带调控，系统考察厚度依赖的铁电、离子迁移与忆阻行为，识别出约 40 nm 为关键厚度阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCPS 在室温下同时具备铁电性、光电导与离子输运，对多功能电子、光子与神经形态（neuromorphic）应用具有潜力。但当铁电性与离子输运共享同一原子起源（如 CCPS 中的铜离子）时，二者可能呈现拮抗关系，这一根本性矛盾（dichotomy）尚待厘清。

Method: 采用压响应显微镜（piezoresponse microscopy）、开尔文探针（Kelvin probe）与导电原子力显微镜（conductive atomic force microscopy），结合光激发输运测量，并通过静电与光辅助界面能带调控（interfacial band tuning），系统研究 CCPS 二极管中铁电性、离子迁移与忆阻行为的厚度依赖演化。

Result: 研究识别出不同的厚度区间：厚度小于约 40 nm 的 CCPS 薄片表现出稳健且可逆的铁电（FE）翻转；更厚的样品则可翻转极化被抑制，电导由 Cu+ 迁移、丝状忆阻翻转（filamentary memristive switching）与整流反转（rectification reversal）主导。光照通过光生载流子进一步提升离子迁移率，在非铁电薄片中驱动光辅助离子输运，在铁电薄片中产生光铁离子耦合响应（photoferroionic response）。

Conclusion: 上述结果阐明了铁电性如何从根本上改变范德华铁离子（ferroionic）体系中的离子输运动力学，为 CCPS 在多功能电子、光子与神经形态器件中的应用提供了厚度与光调控的设计依据。

Abstract: Ambient-temperature coexistence of ferroelectricity, photoconductivity, and ionic transport in ultra-thin van der Waals (vdW) compound CuCrP2S6 (CCPS) offers significant potential for multifunctional electronic, photonic, and neuromorphic applications. However, ferroelectricity and ionic transport can become antagonistic when sharing a common atomic origin, like the copper ions in CCPS. We address this fundamental dichotomy by systematically investigating the thickness-dependent evolution of ferroelectricity, ionic migration, and memristive behavior in CCPS diodes via electrostatic and photo-assisted interfacial band tuning. Using piezoresponse, Kelvin probe, and conductive atomic force microscopy, alongside photoexcited transport measurements, we identify distinct thickness regimes. CCPS flakes thinner than ~ 40 nm exhibit robust, reversible ferroelectric (FE) switching. Conversely, thicker samples display suppressed switchable polarization, with electrical conduction dominated by Cu+ migration, filamentary memristive switching, and rectification reversal. Optical illumination further enhances ionic mobility via photogenerated carriers, driving photo-assisted ionic transport in non-FE flakes and a coupled photoferroionic response in FE flakes. These findings clarify how ferroelectricity fundamentally alters ionic transport dynamics in vdW ferroionic systems.

</details>


### [69] [Distinguishing sodium-ion penetration and sustained transport in realistic hard carbon nanostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02411)
*Carolina Cruz-Cardona,Andreea Hedes,Patrick Rowe,Anderson Arboleda-Lamus,Raul Arenal,Zhenyu Guo,Heather Au,Magda M. Titirici,Celine Merlet,Carla de Tomas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合机器学习生成的多孔硬碳（hard carbon）结构与显式电解液的分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟，系统考察碳密度（0.5–2.0 g cm⁻³）对钠离子存储与输运的影响，指出Na⁺的持续渗透由静电驱动力、溶剂化环境与孔道可及性/限域效应共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬碳是有前景的钠离子储能负极，但其异质微结构与钠输运之间的关系尚不清晰，缺乏对微结构影响电化学响应与Na⁺输运的系统性认识。

Method: 将机器学习生成的硬碳结构与显式1 M NaPF6/碳酸乙烯酯-碳酸二甲酯（ethylene carbonate/dimethyl carbonate）电解液的分子动力学模拟相结合，考察0.5至2.0 g cm⁻³密度范围内的钠存储与输运；通过配位分析、三维轨迹分析和平均力势（potential of mean force）分析刻画离子行为。

Result: 钠摄取随施加电位增加而增大，但随碳密度显著下降；最致密结构中电极仍可充电，但Na⁺摄取极小。配位分析显示施加电位下Na⁺与碳骨架作用增强，同时保持部分溶剂化。三维轨迹显示输运路径异质且以迂曲为主，较开放的HC-C1结构支持更广渗透与更持久位移；最大渗透深度与保留位移并不等价，离子可短暂深入致密结构后返回电解液界面。平均力势分析表明深度渗透对应多种投影自由能变化，无法用单一能量判据描述。

Conclusion: Na⁺的存储与输运受静电驱动力、电解液溶剂化、孔道可及性与限域之间相互作用的支配，碳微结构是决定离子能否持续渗透的关键因素。

Abstract: Hard carbons are promising anodes for sodium-ion energy storage, yet the relationship between their heterogeneous microstructure and sodium transport remains poorly understood. Here, we combine machine-learning-generated hard-carbon structures with molecular dynamics simulations of explicit 1 M NaPF6 in ethylene carbonate/dimethyl carbonate electrolyte to investigate sodium storage and transport across carbon structures with densities from 0.5 to 2.0 g cm-3. Increasing carbon density produces less accessible pore networks and greater graphitic local ordering, allowing the influence of microstructure on electrochemical response and Na+ transport to be examined systematically. Sodium uptake increases with applied potential but decreases strongly with carbon density, while electrode charging persists even in the densest structure, where Na+ uptake remains very small. Coordination analysis shows increasing interaction with the carbon framework under applied potential, while Na+ retains a partially solvated environment. Three-dimensional trajectory analysis reveals heterogeneous, predominantly tortuous transport pathways, with the more open HC-C1 structure supporting broader penetration and greater sustained displacement. Maximum penetration depth and retained displacement are not equivalent: ions can transiently reach deep regions of denser carbon structures before returning towards the electrolyte interface. Potential-of-mean-force analysis further shows that deep penetration occurs across a range of projected free-energy changes and cannot be described by a single energetic criterion. These results show that Na+ storage and transport are governed by the interplay between electrostatic driving force, electrolyte solvation, pore accessibility and confinement, highlighting carbon microstructure as a key determinant of sustained ionic penetration.

</details>


### [70] [LATHE: LAnguage-driven Toolkit for Hypothesis-based crystal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02671)
*Qianyu Zheng,Shuyi Jia,Victor Fung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: LATHE 是一个几何工具包（geometric toolkit），将七类晶体学性质表达为可微目标函数（differentiable objectives），以实现晶体结构的直接几何编辑；其通过模型上下文协议（Model Context Protocol, MCP）服务器嵌入闭环多智能体系统（multi-agent system），在单性质基准上接近完全满足约束，并在带隙逆向设计案例中十次运行有九次达到目标容差窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操控晶体结构以实现目标几何性质或对称性约束是长期挑战。现有计算框架存在不足：大语言模型（LLM）空间感知能力差且 token 空间粒度粗糙；扩散（diffusion）与基于梯度（gradient-based）的结构生成方法通常只能从头（de novo）生成完整结构，难以在任意约束下对给定输入进行细粒度编辑。

Method: 提出 LATHE 几何工具包，将键长、键角、二面角、配位环境、晶格参数、晶胞体积与空间群共七类晶体学性质表达为可微目标，从而支持结构的直接几何编辑。为展示其在材料设计中的用途，进一步通过 MCP 服务器暴露 LATHE，并将其嵌入闭环多智能体系统，将自然语言假设转化为几何修改。

Result: 在单性质基准（single-property benchmarks）中，LATHE 对全部七类性质均达到近乎完美的约束满足（constraint satisfaction），同时使优化后结构接近局部能量极小值（local energy minima）。配备 LATHE 的智能体将超过 87.5% 的自然语言提示转化为有效可执行配置并忠实完成。在带隙逆向设计（band-gap inverse-design）案例中，多智能体环路在十次独立运行中有九次达到目标容差窗口，典型代价为十三个假设—评估循环（hypothesis-evaluation cycles）。

Conclusion: 该工作衔接了自然语言假设生成与基于物理的梯度结构编辑，为可解释、闭环的计算材料发现（computational materials discovery）建立了范式，并可立即应用于广泛的函数材料设计任务。

Abstract: Manipulating crystal structures towards targeted geometric properties or symmetry constraints is a longstanding challenge. Existing computational frameworks fall short: LLMs have poor spatial awareness and operate at coarse granularity in the token space, while diffusion- and gradient-based structure generation approaches generally produce complete structures \textit{de novo} rather allowing for fine-grained editing of a given input under arbitrary constraints. We present LATHE, a geometric toolkit that closes this gap by expressing seven classes of crystallographic properties --- bond length, bond angle, dihedral angle, coordination environment, lattice parameters, cell volume, and space group, as differentiable objectives to enable direct geometric editing of crystal structures. To demonstrate its usage in materials design, we further expose LATHE through a Model Context Protocol server and embed it in a closed-loop multi-agent system which translates hypotheses intogeometric modifications in natural language towards a given design objective. Across single-property benchmarks, LATHE attains near-perfect constraint satisfaction on all seven property types while keeping optimized structures close to local energy minima. The LATHE-equipped agent translates over 87.5\% of natural-language prompts into valid executable configurations and faithfully completes them. In a band-gap inverse-design case study, the multi-agent loop reaches the target tolerance window in nine of ten independent runs at a typical cost of thirteen hypothesis-evaluation cycles. By bridging natural-language hypothesis generation and physically grounded gradient-based structural editing, this work establishes a paradigm for interpretable, closed-loop computational materials discovery that is immediately applicable to a broad class of functional material design tasks.

</details>


### [71] [Temperature--Composition Mapping and Growth-History Dependence of rBN-Related Raman Responses in BN Grown on Fe--B under Flowing N$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02693)
*Donghoi Kim,Ayoung Cho,Kyeongho Park,Young Duck Kim,Chinkyo Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过空间分辨拉曼（Raman）mapping，考察了流动N₂下Fe–B源上生成的BN产物，在1250–1400 °C、1–10 wt% B范围内，rBN相关拉曼特征与生长条件及生长历史的关系，并建立定量描述框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定rBN（related BN，即与rBN相关的BN结构）的拉曼特征如何依赖于生长条件与生长历史，并厘清低频响应与主带非对称性之间的关联。

Method: 在1250–1400 °C、名义B含量1–10 wt%条件下，于流动N₂中于Fe–B源上生长BN，覆盖44种生长条件进行空间分辨拉曼mapping；定义两个独立观测量——750–835 cm⁻¹积分低频响应A₇₅₀₋₈₃₅，以及扣除对称拟合核心后远高波数残余积分T_high^far；并在1350 °C开展生长时长实验；辅以粉末X射线衍射（powder X-ray diffraction）与二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation）。

Result: 存在一个受限的温度–成分区域，其中A₇₅₀₋₈₃₅与~1367 cm⁻¹ BN主带总响应A₁₃₆₇^total均强烈发育；A₇₅₀₋₈₃₅与T_high^far在全数据集上高度对应；A₇₅₀₋₈₃₅/A₁₃₆₇^total随生长条件系统性变化，并随1350 °C高温N₂停留时间改变。

Conclusion: 结果界定了有利于强rBN相关拉曼响应的工艺窗口（processing window），并提出了追踪rBN相关BN结构低频特征与带非对称性特征的定量框架。

Abstract: BN products formed on Fe--B sources under flowing N$_2$ were investigated primarily at 1250--1400~$^\circ$C over nominal B contents of 1--10~wt\% to determine how rBN-related Raman character depends on growth condition and history. Spatially resolved Raman mapping across 44 growth conditions identified a restricted temperature--composition region in which the integrated rBN-related low-frequency response over 750--835~cm$^{-1}$, $A_{750-835}$, is strongly developed together with the integrated total response of the main $\sim$1367~cm$^{-1}$ BN band, $A_{1367}^{\rm{total}}$. In addition, the asymmetric high-wavenumber extension of the $\sim$1367~cm$^{-1}$ band was quantified by subtracting a symmetric fitted core and integrating the far high-wavenumber residual, $T_{\rm{high}}^{\rm{far}}$. The independently defined $A_{750-835}$ and $T_{\rm{high}}^{\rm{far}}$ responses exhibit a strong correspondence across the full dataset, supporting their use as complementary rBN-related Raman observables. In contrast, $A_{750-835}/A_{1367}^{\rm{total}}$ varies systematically among growth conditions, indicating that the relative prominence of the low-frequency response changes with temperature and composition rather than tracking the overall BN response through a fixed proportionality. Growth-duration experiments at 1350~$^\circ$C further show that this ratio also depends on high-temperature N$_2$ dwell time, establishing growth history as an additional kinetic variable. Powder X-ray diffraction on selected products independently confirms the presence of rBN-related stacking, while second-harmonic generation provides complementary symmetry-sensitive evidence. These results define a processing window favorable for strong rBN-related Raman response and introduce a quantitative framework for tracking both the low-frequency and band-asymmetry signatures associated with rBN-related BN structure.

</details>


### [72] [Emergent Noncoplanar Spin Chirality through Magnetic and Nonmagnetic Substitution in the Kagome Antiferromagnet Mn$_3$Sn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02794)
*Anupam Barik,Achintya Low,Susanta Ghosh,Kapildeb Dolui,Setti Thirupathaiah*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过在kagome反铁磁体Mn$_3$Sn的Mn位进行磁性(Cr)与非磁性(Cu)替代，分别以"交换增强"和"交换稀释"两种路径调控实空间Berry曲率，实现拓扑霍尔效应(THE)的可控工程化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中心对称磁体（centrosymmetric magnets）中控制非共面自旋织构（noncoplanar spin textures）是实现涌现Berry相位现象（emergent Berry-phase phenomena）的核心难题，摘要据此将化学替代作为突破口。

Method: 以kagome反铁磁体（kagome antiferromagnet）Mn$_3$Sn为对象，在Mn位分别引入磁性Cr替代与非磁性Cu替代，通过输运测量考察自旋手性（spin chirality）、自旋倾角（spin canting）、自旋重取向转变（spin-reorientation transition）及载流子类型（carrier type）的变化。

Result: Cr替代增强低温自旋倾角，稳定致密非共面织构并产生显著低温拓扑霍尔效应（topological Hall effect, THE）；Cu替代驱动伴随易轴旋转（easy-axis rotation）的自旋重取向转变，在150--208 K温区形成竞争磁各向异性稳定非共面织构的窗口并产生大THE，同时伴随载流子类型交叉（carrier-type crossover）指示电子结构重构。

Conclusion: 交换增强（exchange enhancement）与交换稀释（exchange dilution）是调控实空间Berry曲率（real-space Berry curvature）的两种根本不同路径，化学替代可作为kagome反铁磁体中工程化拓扑输运（topological transport）的有效策略。

Abstract: Controlling noncoplanar spin textures in centrosymmetric magnets remains a central challenge for realizing emergent Berry-phase phenomena. Here, we demonstrate that magnetic (Cr) and nonmagnetic (Cu) substitution at the Mn site provide distinct routes to engineer spin chirality and topological transport in the kagome antiferromagnet Mn$_3$Sn. Cr substitution enhances low-temperature spin canting, stabilizing a dense noncoplanar texture that yields a pronounced topological Hall effect (THE) at low temperatures. In contrast, Cu substitution drives a spin-reorientation transition accompanied by an easy-axis rotation, creating a broad temperature window (150--208 K) where competing magnetic anisotropies stabilize noncoplanar spin textures and generate a large THE. A concurrent carrier-type crossover signals an underlying electronic-structure reconstruction. These results reveal that exchange enhancement and exchange dilution represent two fundamentally different pathways for tuning real-space Berry curvature, establishing chemical substitution as a powerful strategy for engineering topological transport in kagome antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [73] [Orientation refinement for nanobeam precession electron diffraction patterns using spot intensity profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02871)
*Arthur Després,Muriel Vérion,Edgar F. Rauch*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种针对纳米束旋进电子衍射（precession electron diffraction）图样的高角分辨晶体取向测量方法，可用于四维扫描旋进电子衍射（4D scanning precession electron diffraction），通过模拟并关联衍射斑点内部强度变化实现取向测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自动化晶体取向映射（automated crystal orientation mapping, ACOM）的角分辨率有限，且在晶带轴（zone axis）附近存在系统误差，需要更高精度的取向测量手段。

Method: 模拟衍射斑点内部强度变化，并将其与实验观测到的强度变化相关联，从而反演晶体取向；在硅单晶上围绕测角器主倾轴进行长曝光倾转序列（tilt series）实验，并与标准方法对比，同时考察短曝光引入泊松噪声（Poisson noise）的影响。

Result: 经精修后，施加的倾转角回收精度为 0.044°–0.054°，而标准方法为 0.25°–0.33°；短曝光条件下因泊松噪声精度略微下降至 0.054°–0.066°。该方法还修正了 ACOM 在晶带轴附近的系统误差。

Conclusion: 基于斑点内强度变化模拟与关联的取向测量方法显著优于标准 ACOM，可实现亚 0.1° 量级的角分辨率，并对噪声具有一定鲁棒性，同时纠正晶带轴附近的系统偏差。

Abstract: We develop a method to measure crystallographic orientations with high angular resolution for nano-beam precession electron diffraction patterns, and applicable to 4D scanning precession electron diffraction. We show that internal variations of intensity in the diffraction spots can be simulated and correlated to experimental variations to measure the crystallographic orientation with a much better angular resolution that with the standard method of automated crystal orientation mapping (ACOM). In a tilt series carried out with long exposure time on a monocrystal of silicon around the main goniometer tilt axis, the imposed tilt angles are retrieved with a 0.044° to 0.054° precision with refinement, against 0.25° to 0.33° with the standard method. Shorter exposure times, introducing Poisson noise, marginally degrade the precision to a 0.054° to 0.066° range. The method also corrects a systematic error of ACOM near zone axis.

</details>


### [74] [Optimizing the magnetic hyperthermia efficiency from the structural dependence of dipolar interactions in magnetic nanoparticles assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02977)
*Abdelhamid Morjane,François Vernay,Vincent Russier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用时间量化蒙特卡洛（TQMC）框架，研究各向异性聚集体中局部结构对偶极相互作用贡献的影响，强调易轴分布需以偶极硬球流体的平衡结构为起点自洽确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁热疗（magnetic hyperthermia）通过磁性纳米颗粒（MNP）在交变外场下将磁能转化为热，具有癌症治疗等应用前景。MNP 组装的频率响应既取决于颗粒本身性质（尺寸、形状、磁晶各向异性），也取决于颗粒间相互作用引发的集体效应。

Method: 采用近期开发的时间量化蒙特卡洛（Time Quantified Monte Carlo, TQMC）框架进行动态模拟，重点关注薄膜、链或拉长聚集体等各向异性外形的 MNP 组装。局部结构被定义为纳米颗粒的位置及其易轴（easy axes）分布，其中易轴分布需以偶极硬球流体（dipolar hard sphere fluid）的平衡结构为起点自洽确定。

Result: 研究揭示了各向异性聚集体中局部结构对偶极相互作用贡献的重要性；结果表明，偶极相互作用的远程分量首先导致退磁效应（demagnetizing effects），使响应依赖于样品的外形；并表明易轴分布必须自洽确定。

Conclusion: 在模拟各向异性 MNP 聚集体的动态响应时，局部结构（颗粒位置与易轴分布）不可忽略，且易轴分布应基于偶极硬球流体的平衡结构自洽求解，以恰当刻画偶极相互作用对交变场响应的贡献。

Abstract: Magnetic hyperthermia, based on the conversion of the magnetic energy of magnetic nanoparticles (MNP) under an alternating external field into heat, presents a great interest, especially because of important applications such as promising advances in cancer treatment. The underlying frequency-dependent response of the MNP assembly under the excitation field depends on the first hand on the individual properties of the MNP such as their size, shape and magnetocrystalline anisotropy and on the other hand on collective properties generated by interactions between MNP. Due to the long-range dipolar component of the latter, the first consequence is the so-called demagnetizing effects, making the response dependent on the external sample shape. This is the reason for studying the properties of MNP in assemblies of anisotropic external shapes such as films, chains, or elongated aggregates. In the present work, using a recently developed Time Quantified Monte Carlo (TQMC) framework for dynamic simulations, we focus more precisely on the importance of the local structure in such anisotropic aggregates to the dipolar interactions contribution of the response to the external field. Here the local structure is understood as both the location of the nanoparticles and the distribution of their easy axes. We show that the latter must be determined in a self-consistent way starting with the equilibrium structure of a ferrofluid modeled as the dipolar hard sphere fluid.

</details>


### [75] [Ab initio design of enhanced and sign-reversible spin Hall and spin Nernst conductivity in skutterudite materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02998)
*Saikat Debnath,Babu Baijnath Prasad,Shishir Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理计算与Wannier紧束缚模型，研究Co基和Rh基方钴矿（skutterudites）的本征自旋霍尔效应（spin Hall effect）与自旋能斯特效应（spin Nernst effect），并探索通过重金属替代与位点选择性掺杂增强自旋输运响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找能够稳定产生自旋流（spin-current generation）的新材料是当前活跃的研究方向，因此需要探究方钴矿体系中本征自旋输运响应及其可调控性。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）和基于Wannier的紧束缚模型（Wannier-based tight-binding models），分析纯净MX₃（M = Co, Rh；X = As, Sb）及化学修饰体系的自旋贝里曲率（spin Berry curvature）与自旋输运响应，并考察不同位点替代与掺杂（8c位、2a间隙位）的影响。

Result: 纯净化合物虽具有可观的动量分辨自旋贝里曲率，但因布里渊区（Brillouin zone）内的显著抵消，净自旋霍尔响应较小。8c位同价Ir、Bi替代在费米能级（E_F）附近增强微弱，非同价Pt替代响应相对较好。在RhAs₃的2a间隙位引入Pt，其费米能级附近自旋霍尔电导率提升近一倍至约400 (ħ/e)(Ω·cm)⁻¹；300 K下同一体系的自旋能斯特电导率约为1.46 (ħ/e) A·m⁻¹·K⁻¹，比M位Pt替代高近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 响应增强源于Pt 5d态、强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）以及避免交叉（avoided crossings）产生的显著自旋贝里曲率热点（hot spots）。结果表明，位点选择性化学工程（site-selective chemical engineering）可有效克服动量空间抵消，从而增强方钴矿中的自旋霍尔与自旋能斯特响应，为稳健自旋流生成提供可能路径。

Abstract: The search for novel materials capable of robust spin-current generation remains an active area of research. Here, we investigate the intrinsic spin Hall and spin Nernst effects in Co- and Rh-based skutterudites and explore their enhancement through heavy-metal substitution and site-selective doping. Using first-principles calculations and Wannier-based tight-binding models, we analyze the spin Berry curvature and spin-transport responses of pristine MX$_3$, where M = Co, Rh and X = As, Sb, as well as chemically modified systems. Despite sizable momentum-resolved spin Berry curvature, the pristine compounds exhibit relatively small net spin Hall responses due to substantial cancellation across the Brillouin zone. Isoelectronic Ir and Bi substitution at 8$c$ Wycoff M site produces little enhancement near the E$_F$, whereas non-isoelectronic Pt substitution yields a relatively better response. Most interestingly, the incorporation of Pt at the interstitial $2a$ Wycoff site of RhAs$_3$ nearly doubles the spin Hall conductivity to $\sim400~(\hbar/e)(Ω,\mathrm{cm})^{-1}$ near the E$_F$ when compared to Pt substitution at 8$c$ position. At 300 K, the same interstitially filled system exhibits a spin Nernst conductivity of $\sim1.46~(\hbar/e),\mathrm{A,m^{-1}K^{-1}}$ at the E$_F$, nearly an order of magnitude larger than that of $M$-site Pt substitution. The enhanced responses arise from Pt $5d$ states, strong spin-orbit coupling, and avoided crossings that generate pronounced spin-Berry-curvature hot spots. These results demonstrate that site-selective chemical engineering can effectively overcome momentum-space cancellation resulting in enhanced spin Hall and spin Nernst responses in skutterudites. Our results provide a possible route towards robust spin-current generation in this class of materials.

</details>


### [76] [Coercivity-Aware Machine Learning Discovery of Rare-Earth-Free Soft Magnetic Alloys with First-Principles Magnetic Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03171)
*Avik Mahata,David Priefer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出矫顽力感知（coercivity-aware）机器学习框架，以居里温度（Curie temperature）与矫顽力（coercivity）为耦合设计目标筛选无稀土软磁合金（rare-earth-free soft magnetic alloys），并结合不确定性与第一性原理（first-principles）验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向无稀土软磁合金的成分筛选，需同时兼顾居里温度与矫顽力两个耦合性能指标；且传统随机划分评估可能高估模型对未知成分的迁移能力，因此有必要引入更严格的评估与筛选策略。

Method: 构建实验磁性材料数据集，采用按成分分组划分（composition-grouped partitioning）以避免训练集与测试集中相同成分重叠；比较元素分数（elemental fractions）与成分加权元素描述符（composition-weighted elemental descriptors）对两种磁性能的预测；引入基于残差的预测区间（residual-based prediction intervals）与距训练集距离过滤（distance-to-training filters）筛选 Fe-Co-Ni-Mn-Al-Si 成分；辅以第一性原理计算与有限温度原子自旋模拟（atomistic spin simulations）。

Result: 成分分组评估的预测精度低于常规随机划分，表明评估向未见成分迁移的重要性；加权元素描述符与元素分数结合可提升居里温度预测，但未能改善矫顽力预测，与矫顽力强烈依赖工艺与微观结构一致；不确定性感知筛选显著缩小候选空间并识别出富 Fe-Co 成分；第一性原理显示代表性 Fe-Co 结构具强磁极化与磁体积效应，自旋模拟将其置于高磁有序温度尺度。

Conclusion: 该组合方法为无稀土软磁化学成分的优先级排序提供了计算框架，并明确区分成分级筛选与候选物特异性物理验证两个层面。

Abstract: Coercivity-aware machine learning is developed to screen rare-earth-free soft magnetic alloys using Curie temperature and coercivity as coupled design targets. A compiled experimental magnetic-material dataset is evaluated using composition-grouped partitioning to avoid overlap of identical compositions between training and testing, and elemental fractions and composition-weighted elemental descriptors are examined for prediction of the two magnetic properties. Residual-based prediction intervals and distance-to-training filters are then incorporated to screen Fe-Co-Ni-Mn-Al-Si compositions. Composition-grouped evaluation shows lower predictive accuracy than conventional random partitioning, demonstrating the importance of evaluating transfer to previously unseen compositions. Combining weighted elemental descriptors with elemental fractions improves Curie-temperature prediction, whereas the additional descriptors do not improve coercivity prediction, consistent with the strong dependence of coercivity on processing and microstructure. Uncertainty-aware screening substantially narrows the candidate space and identifies Fe-Co-rich compositions for further evaluation. First-principles calculations independently show strong magnetic polarization and magnetovolume effects in representative Fe-Co structures, while finite-temperature atomistic spin simulations place the Fe-Co benchmark systems on high magnetic-ordering-temperature scales. The combined approach provides a computational framework for prioritizing rare-earth-free soft-magnetic chemistries while distinguishing composition-level screening from candidate-specific physical validation.

</details>


### [77] [Algebra of symmetry allowed physical properties of crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03244)
*Piotr Fabrykiewicz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出“与性质相关的点群（property-related point groups, PRPGs）”新概念，指出21个与非中心对称晶体点群同构的PRPG对应Birss的21种张量形式（A–U）。基于Neumann原理将张量以多极自由度表示，并由此引出张量形式代数，其中允许张量分量空间与PRPG算符空间呈对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统的对称性由122个磁点群（magnetic point groups, MPGs）描述，但对任何物理张量仅产生21组限制，这些张量形式传统上以字母A至U标记（Birss, 1964）。字母标记缺乏明确的数学对象对应，因此需要引入更具数学结构的表示方式。

Method: 提出PRPG概念，并证明21个与非中心对称晶体点群（crystallographic point groups, CPGs）同构的PRPG恰好对应21种张量形式；借助Neumann原理，将给定张量表达为多极自由度（multipole degrees of freedom）的组合，对应PRPG所允许的多极分量子集。

Result: 21个PRPG与21种张量形式一一对应，可用PRPG作为替代A–U字母的数学对象；每个张量可表示为给定PRPG所允许的全部多极分量的一个子集；由此形成张量形式代数（algebra of tensor forms），其中允许张量分量空间与PRPG算符空间具有某种对偶性。

Conclusion: 以PRPG替代传统字母标记，为张量形式提供了明确的群论数学对象；通过多极自由度与Neumann原理，建立了张量形式与PRPG之间的代数结构，揭示两类空间的对偶关系。

Abstract: The symmetry of a physical system given by the 122 magnetic point groups (MPGs) leads to only 21 sets of restrictions on any physical tensors. Those, so called, tenor forms were labeled by letters from A to U by R. R. Birss (Symmetry and Magnetism, North Holland, Amsterdam, 1964). A new concept of property-related point groups (PRPGs) is proposed. It is shown that the 21 PRPGs isomorphic to the 21 non-centrosymmetric crystallographic point groups (CPGs) corresponds to the 21 tensor forms. Therefore instead of a symbols from A to U one can use PRPGs as mathematical objects corresponding to the tenor forms. From the Neumann's principle given multipole component is only allowed in a system when a group describing symmetry of the system is a subgroup of the multipole component symmetry. Each tensor can be expressed in the terms of the multipole degrees of freedom, i.e. it corresponds to a subset of all multipole components allowed by given PRPG. Those relations leads to the algebra of tensor forms, where space of allowed tensor components and space of PRPG operators are somehow dual to each other.

</details>


### [78] [Second-order Raman and anharmonic lattice dynamics in lead-halide perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03274)
*Chao Shen,Florian Kluibenschedl,Ayan A. Zhumekenov,Mikhail Lemeshko,Osman M. Bakr,Artem G. Volosniev,Zhanybek Alpichshev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用二维太赫兹克尔效应光谱（2D THz Kerr-effect spectroscopy）探究 CH₃NH₃PbBr₃ 中太赫兹驱动晶格动力学的微观起源，判定主导激发路径为声子介导（phonon-mediated）而非纯光子过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲基铵溴化铅（methylammonium lead bromide）等铅卤钙钛矿（lead-halide perovskites）中太赫兹驱动晶格动力学的微观机制尚不明确，存在多种竞争性非线性激发机制需要甄别。

Method: 采用二维太赫兹克尔效应光谱对 CH₃NH₃PbBr₃ 进行实验分析与机制判别，区分不同的非线性激发通道。

Result: 常观测到的 1.15 THz 拉曼活性（Raman-active）A_g 模并非由太赫兹电场直接激发，而是通过立方非谐耦合（cubic anharmonic coupling）与 0.68 THz 红外活性（infrared-active）声子耦合产生；1.36 THz 的卫星峰（satellite feature）被识别为同一红外活性模的双声子拉曼响应（two-phonon Raman response）。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持声子介导为主导激发路径；红外活性声子所表现出的强二次耦合（quadratic coupling）暗示铅卤钙钛矿中存在一种非常规形式的电子-声子缀饰（electron-phonon dressing）。

Abstract: We explore the microscopic origin of THz-driven lattice dynamics in methylammonium lead bromide CH$_3$NH$_3$PbBr$_3$ using two-dimensional THz Kerr-effect spectroscopy. The analysis of the experiment enables discrimination between competing nonlinear excitation mechanisms and reveals that the dominant pathway is phonon-mediated rather than purely photonic. In particular, we argue that the commonly observed 1.15~THz Raman-active $A_g$ mode is not excited directly by the THz electric field. Instead, it emerges through cubic anharmonic coupling to an infrared-active phonon at 0.68~THz. Furthermore, a satellite feature at 1.36~THz is identified as a two-phonon Raman response of the same infrared-active mode. The observation of strong quadratic coupling involving the infrared-active phonon suggests an unconventional form of electron-phonon dressing present in lead-halide perovskites.

</details>


### [79] [Illuminating early ZrC oxidation using ensemble small-cell DFT with dynamic gas management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03293)
*Philip Wurzner,Boyang Chen,Matthias Möller,Sebastian Feld,Qi-Jun Hong,Yinglu Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用小细胞系综方法（small-cell ensemble method）与从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics, AIMD），在873–1273 K及持续高氧通量、气体移除条件下模拟碳化锆（zirconium carbide）氧化，揭示碳在氧化物下滞留、以气体逸出或形成碳链的分配行为随温度的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳化锆氧化过程中释放的碳可能滞留于氧化物下方或以气体形式逸出，使质量测量结果的解读变得复杂；因此需借助原子尺度模拟厘清碳的去向与温度依赖性。

Method: 改造小细胞系综方法，使用十六条140 ps的从头算分子动力学轨迹，温度区间873–1273 K，施加持续高氧通量并配合气体移除。

Result: 净质量增益速率与总碳转移（进入气相或滞留于碳链）平均速率均遵循阿伦尼乌斯（Arrhenius）趋势；973 K以上气体析出趋于平缓而总碳转移持续增加；滞留于碳链的碳占比由973 K的40 %升至1273 K的66 %；40 ps后的拟合显示碳转移速率随时间下降，但并非每条轨迹皆如此。

Conclusion: 早期氧化阶段的碳分配取决于温度，973 K以上随温度升高，碳更倾向于形成碳链而非气化。

Abstract: During zirconium carbide oxidation, liberated carbon can remain beneath the oxide or escape as gas, complicating the interpretation of mass measurements. We adapt the small-cell ensemble method using sixteen 140 ps ab initio molecular dynamics trajectories at 873-1273 K under a sustained high oxygen flux with gas removal. The average rates of net mass gain and total carbon transfer into gas or retained chains follow Arrhenius trends. Above 973 K, gas evolution levels off while total carbon transfer increases. The fraction of transferred carbon retained in chains rises from 40 % at 973 K to 66 % at 1273 K. Fits after 40 ps suggest declining carbon transfer rates over time, although not in every trajectory. These results show that carbon partitioning during early oxidation depends on temperature, with increasing temperature above 973 K favouring chain formation over gasification.

</details>

### [80] [Breakdown of material-time scaling in the microscopic dynamics of metallic glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02426)
*Till Böhmer,Jie Shen,Jan Philipp Gabriel,Rolf Zeißler,Federico Zontone,Fan Yang,Eloi Pineda,Beatrice Ruta,Thomas Blochowicz*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究结合时间分辨 X 射线衍射（X-ray diffraction, XRD）与 X 射线光子关联谱（X-ray photon-correlation spectroscopy, XPCS），追踪金属玻璃（metallic glass）在小温度跳跃后的完整弛豫过程，发现材料时间（material time）可描述平均结构性质的演化，但微观动力学并不服从材料时间标度（material-time scaling）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃中的物理老化（physical aging）通常以材料时间概念描述，其假定弛豫机制与平衡态相同、仅速率随老化改变。然而金属玻璃的微观动力学在玻璃态与平衡过冷液体（supercooled liquid）之间存在定性差异，材料时间能否刻画其微观动力学尚不明确。

Method: 在从平衡态出发的小温度跳跃后，利用同步的时间分辨 X 射线衍射与 X 射线光子关联谱，跟踪金属玻璃的完整平衡化过程，并分析第一尖锐衍射峰（first sharp diffraction peak）位置的时间演化以及微观密度-密度自关联函数（density-density autocorrelation functions）。

Result: 第一尖锐衍射峰位置的时间演化表现出材料时间老化所特有的升温与降温跳跃不对称性，并可由基于该概念的 Tool-Narayanaswamy-Moynihan 模型定量描述；相反，微观密度-密度自关联函数强烈违反材料时间标度，即便在低至 4K 的温度变化后也出现压缩指数弛豫（compressed-exponential relaxation），且随平均结构平衡化而连续演变为拉伸指数弛豫（stretched-exponential relaxation）形态。

Conclusion: 该行为与体积受挫（volumetric frustration）相关的瞬态应力介导贡献（transient stress-mediated contribution）一致，该贡献在平衡化后消失。结果表明，材料时间可描述平均结构性质的演化，即使相应的微观动力学不服从材料时间标度。

Abstract: Physical aging in glasses is often described using the concept of material time, which assumes the underlying relaxation mechanism to remain the same as in equilibrium, but with a rate that changes during aging. Whether material time also captures the microscopic dynamics of metallic glasses, which differ qualitatively between the glass and the equilibrium supercooled liquid, is unclear. Using simultaneous time-resolved X-ray diffraction and X-ray photon-correlation spectroscopy, we follow the full equilibration of a metallic glass after small temperature jumps starting from equilibrium. The time evolution of the first sharp diffraction peak position exhibits the asymmetry between temperature up- and down-jumps characteristic of material-time aging and is quantitatively described by the Tool-Narayanaswamy-Moynihan model, which is based on this concept. By contrast, the microscopic density-density autocorrelation functions strongly violate material-time scaling. Compressed-exponential relaxation emerges even after temperature changes as small as 4K, irrespective of the direction of the temperature jump, and continuously evolves into a stretched-exponential relaxation shape following the equilibration of the average structure. This behavior is consistent with a transient stress-mediated contribution associated with volumetric frustration that disappears upon equilibration. Our results show that a material time can describe the evolution of average structural properties even when the corresponding microscopic dynamics do not obey material-time scaling.

</details>


### [81] [Universal energy barrier for plastic flow in a network glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02454)
*Ibrahim Ghanem,Richard Jana,Wolfram G. Nöhring,Michael Moseler,Lars Pastewka*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potential），在非晶碳（amorphous carbon）中证明剪切转变（shear transformation）对应单根共价键（covalent bond）的断裂或形成，并据此建立可从键级量预测塑性流动应力的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪切转变是玻璃塑性（plasticity）的基本事件，也是众多介观塑性流动模型的基础，但其原子起源与能量学（energetics）长期难以确定。作者希望从原子尺度揭示剪切转变的本质机制。

Method: 采用机器学习原子间势，以非晶碳作为代表性网络玻璃（network glass）体系，将键长（bond length）作为反应坐标（reaction coordinate）提取能量景观（energy landscape）与势垒（barrier），并结合剪切转变的激活统计（activation statistics）建模。

Result: 剪切转变对应于单根共价键的断裂或形成；尽管存在固有结构无序（structural disorder），所有能量景观坍缩为统一的势垒形状，仅键跳跃距离（bond-jump distance）保持键特异性，并通过三次律（cubic law）决定势垒高度。

Conclusion: 结合激活统计，模型可由独立测定的键级量预测塑性流动应力，为玻璃塑性流动的唯象模型（phenomenological models）提供微观支持，并指向非晶塑性的定量描述路径。

Abstract: Shear transformations are the elementary events of plasticity in glasses and underpin many mesoscopic models of plastic flow, yet their atomic origin and energetics remain difficult to establish. Here we use a machine-learned interatomic potential to show that, in amorphous carbon as a representative network glass, a shear transformation corresponds to the breaking or formation of a single covalent bond. The simplicity of the associated reaction coordinate, the bond length, enables us to extract energy landscapes and barriers. Despite the intrinsic structural disorder, we find that all energy landscapes collapse onto a universal barrier shape. What remains bond-specific is a single scale, the bond-jump distance, which fixes the barrier height through a cubic law. Combined with the activation statistics of shear transformations, this yields a model that predicts the plastic flow stress from independently determined bond-level quantities. Our results provide microscopic support for phenomenological models of plastic flow in glasses and suggest a route towards a quantitative description of amorphous plasticity.

</details>


### [82] [Quantum transmission in 1D disordered stealthy hyperuniform Kronig--Penney-like models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02501)
*Shaobing Yuan,Carlo Vanoni,Salvatore Torquato*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文首次提出一维连续点散射体在无序隐身超均匀（SHU）位置分布下的量子输运微扰理论，揭示了因傅里叶空间隐身条件导致的低阶项精确抵消及实空间超均匀约束对四阶 Lyapunov 指数的进一步压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序隐身超均匀（SHU）体系因在 stealthy 区间内单散射贡献消失，成为操控经典与量子输运的新平台；然而针对连续点散射体位置无序下的一维量子输运缺乏微扰理论描述，本文试图填补该空白。

Method: 构建一维连续点散射体体系中量子输运的微扰理论（perturbation theory），其中散射体位置服从 SHU 无序分布，并结合傅里叶空间结构因子条件 $S(q)=0$ 与实空间超均匀约束进行逐阶分析。

Result: 微扰理论表明，由于傅里叶空间的隐身条件 $S(q)=0$ ，所有低阶项被精确抵消；在实空间超均匀约束 $\lim_{D\to\infty}\sigma_D^2=c<\infty$ 作用下，Lyapunov 指数 $\lambda(k)$ 中最低非零的四阶项被进一步压低。

Conclusion: 该结果为无序 SHU 体系中一般波传播现象提供了普适性启示，说明 SHU 无序对量子输运的抑制具有一般性与普适意义。

Abstract: Disordered stealthy hyperuniform (SHU) systems are emerging as platforms for controlling classical and quantum transport due to vanishing single-scattering contributions in the stealthy regime. Here, to our knowledge, we present the first perturbation theory of one-dimensional quantum transport through a continuum of identical point scatterers with SHU positional disorder. Our perturbation theory indicates the exact cancellation of all the lower-order terms due to the Fourier-space stealthy condition $S(q)=0$, and the further suppression of the lowest non-vanishing fourth order in the Lyapunov exponent $λ(k)$ due to the real-space hyperuniform constraint $\lim\limits_{D\to\infty}σ_D^2=c<\infty$. Our findings presents universal implications of the general wave-propagation phenomena in disordered SHU systems.

</details>


### [83] [Cup and Cap Topological Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03169)
*Marta Niedostatek,Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Runyue Wang,Federica Baccini,Lorenzo Giambagli,Pietro Lió,Ginestra Bianconi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Cup and Cap Topological Neural Network（CCNN），通过引入上积与下积（cup and cap products）突破传统拓扑深度学习（Topological Deep Learning, TDL）每层仅能跨一维传递信号的限制，并在 TopoBench 数据集上验证其与同类单纯复形神经网络的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑深度学习（Topological Deep Learning, TDL）旨在学习与高阶单纯形（simplices，如节点、边、三角形）相关的特征。然而，现有方法多基于边界算子（boundary operators）与 Hodge 拉普拉斯算子（Hodge Laplacians），其局限在于单层内特征与信号无法跨越超过一个维度进行提升或降低。

Method: 论文采用上积与下积（cup and cap products）作为核心工具，构建了 Cup and Cap Topological Neural Network（CCNN）这一拓扑深度学习架构，用于学习基于节点的变量（0-上链，0-cochains），并同时考虑数据中存在的多体相互作用（many-body interactions，例如三角形）。

Result: 通过在 TopoBench 数据集上进行评估，CCNN 展现出与其他单纯复形神经网络（simplicial complex neural networks）相当的竞争力。

Conclusion: 引入上积与下积可有效突破传统基于边界算子与 Hodge 拉普拉斯算子的维度跨越限制，CCNN 作为一种可捕捉多体相互作用的拓扑深度学习架构，在基准数据集上具备有竞争力的表现。

Abstract: Topological Deep Learning (TDL) is designed to learn features associated with higher-dimensional simplices including not only nodes but also edges, triangles and so on. However, most TDL approaches, being based on boundary operators and Hodge Laplacians, have the limitation that features and signals cannot be lifted or lowered across more than one dimension per layer. To overcome this limitation, in this work, we propose the adoption of the cup and cap products. Specifically, we formulate the Cup and Cap Topological Neural Network (CCNN), a topological deep learning architecture designed to learn node-based variables (or 0-cochains) by taking into account their many-body interactions (e.g. triangles) present in the data. We validate CCNN by assessing its performance on the TopoBench datasets, revealing its competitiveness with respect to other simplicial complex neural networks.

</details>

### [84] [Conditional Capacity and Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Particle Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02701)
*Kaushik Pendiyala,Haris Zia,Trevin Lee,Timothy Legge,Alejandro J. De Leon,Zihan Zhao,Aaron Wang,Abhijith Gandrakota,Jennifer Ngadiuba,Richard Cavanaugh,Javier Duarte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在JetClass-II 188类喷注分类任务上比较稠密与混合专家（MoE）粒子Transformer，发现避免token丢弃（token dropping）时top-1 MoE在近乎相同的名义前向计算量下优于稠密基线，但增加存储专家数量收益甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型可在不按比例增加激活计算量的前提下提升参数容量，但这一权衡在粒子物理Transformer中的表现尚不明确，故需系统研究。

Method: 在188类JetClass-II上研究稠密与MoE粒子Transformer，变化专家数量、路由容量（routing capacity）、top-K及辅助损失（auxiliary loss）等配置，并开展路由分析。

Result: 避免token丢弃时，top-1 MoE在近乎不变的名义前向计算下优于稠密基线，继续增加存储专家数量仅带来很小精度增益；每token激活多个专家可进一步提升预测性能但计算成本更高；路由分配在某些配置下与粒子身份和运动学（kinematics）关联更强，但该结构并不随分类性能单调增强。

Conclusion: 评估喷注分类的稀疏专家模型时，需区分存储参数容量（stored parameter capacity）、激活计算量（active computation）、路由容量（routing capacity）与路由组织（routing organization）。代码与实验配置已公开。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models can increase parameter capacity without proportionally increasing active computation, but it is unclear how this trade-off behaves in particle-physics transformers. We study dense and MoE Particle Transformers on 188-class JetClass-II, varying expert count, routing capacity, top-K, and auxiliary loss. We find that, when token dropping is avoided, top-1 MoE models improve over the dense baseline at nearly unchanged nominal forward compute, while further increasing the number of stored experts produces little additional accuracy gain. Activating multiple experts per token yields additional predictive improvements at higher computational cost. Routing analyses show that expert assignments become more strongly associated with particle identity and kinematics in some configurations, but this structure does not increase monotonically with classification performance. These results highlight the need to distinguish stored parameter capacity, active computation, routing capacity, and routing organization when evaluating sparse expert models for jet classification. Code and experiment configurations are available at https://github.com/kpendiyala/MPT.

</details>


### [85] [S$^{2}$-PINN: Stochastic Separable Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03303)
*Zhendong Li,Akwum Onwunta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 S²-PINN，一种随机可分离物理信息神经网络，用于随机偏微分方程的不确定性量化，通过低秩张量分解耦合空间字典、时间傅里叶特征与 gPC 随机基，在精度、标定与参数效率上超越九个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机偏微分方程（random PDEs）的不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification, UQ）在计算科学与工程中广泛存在，但经典谱求解器面临维数灾难（curse of dimensionality），现有神经求解器又常忽略使矩与标定可处理的随机结构。

Method: S²-PINN 以可学习高斯空间字典、傅里叶时间特征与广义多项式混沌（generalized polynomial chaos, gPC）随机基表示解 u(t,x,Z)，并由低秩 Canonical Polyadic（CP）张量分解核耦合，采用强形式与 gPC 投影残差混合损失训练。理论分析证明可分离类在温和条件下于 L² 中稠密，投影残差恰对应随机 Galerkin 约束，且小批量投影系数对 gPC 模态数呈对数依赖、正交惩罚控制空间字典条件数。

Result: 在四个构造型随机 PDE 基准上，S²-PINN 在均值与方差精度及标定方面优于九个基线，且参数量显著更少。进一步在非构造型 Poisson 与 Darcy 问题、随机 Navier–Stokes 问题、更高随机维度的扩散尺度研究及两个随机反问题上验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结果支持将随机可分离性（stochastic separability）作为物理信息神经不确定性量化的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) for random partial differential equations (PDEs) is ubiquitous in computational science and engineering. However, classical spectral solvers for this class of problems face the curse of dimensionality, and existing neural solvers often ignore the stochastic structure that makes moments and calibration tractable. We introduce a stochastic separable physics-informed neural network, dubbed S$^{2}$-PINN, that represents the solution $u(t,\mathbf{x},\mathbf{Z})$ of a random PDE with a learnable Gaussian spatial dictionary, Fourier temporal features, and a generalized polynomial chaos (gPC) stochastic basis, coupled by a low-rank Canonical Polyadic (CP) tensor decomposition core. The method is trained with a hybrid strong-form and gPC-projected residual loss. Our theoretical analysis establishes that the separable class is dense in $L^2$ under mild conditions, and the projected residual corresponds exactly to a stochastic Galerkin constraint. Furthermore, we show that mini-batch projection coefficients are logarithmically dependent on the number of gPC modes, and that the orthogonality penalty controls the conditioning of the learned spatial dictionary. Using four manufactured random PDE benchmarks, we show that S$^{2}$-PINN outperforms nine baselines in terms of mean and variance accuracy, as well as calibration, while using significantly fewer parameters. Further evaluations on non-manufactured Poisson and Darcy problems, a stochastic Navier--Stokes problem, a diffusion scaling study of higher random dimensions, and two stochastic inverse problems reveal the generalization capabilities of the proposed structure. Together, these results support stochastic separability as an effective design principle for physics-informed neural UQ. The code for the experiments can be found in https://github.com/DMax1314/s2pinn

</details>


### [86] [The AI Theorist reveals excitonic structure in $α$-RuCl$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02417)
*Hongjian Zhou,Xianfan Nie,Sean Wu,Tarun Patel,Jinge Wu,Andrew Liu,Adam Wei Tsen,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出 AI Theorist——一套可自主生成假说、执行第一性原理计算并根据证据迭代修正的 AI 智能体系统，用于从实验数据中自主发现物理模型。以 Kitaev 量子自旋液体候选材料 α-RuCl₃ 的光谱为对象，系统给出对光学与光电流观测的新解释，识别出具有不同光学选择定则与实空间分布的激子态，属首次由 AI 自主完成针对未发表量子材料实验观测的物理建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验仪器与自动化的进步产生了日益丰富的数据集，但从实验观测中提炼微观理解仍是科学发现的瓶颈。该文旨在加速这一过程，实现物理模型的自主发现，从而将实验观测转化为可检验的物理认识。

Method: 采用名为 AI Theorist 的人工智能（artificial intelligence, AI）智能体系统，通过假说生成（hypothesis generation）、第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）与证据驱动的修正（evidence-driven refinement）闭环开展自主发现。将该框架应用于 α-RuCl₃，结合第一性原理电子结构与多体计算（many-body calculations）分析其光学光谱。

Result: AI Theorist 对光学与光电流观测给出了新的解释，识别出多种具有对比鲜明的光学选择定则（optical selection rules）与实空间分布（real-space distributions）的激子态（excitonic states）。据作者所述，这是首次由 AI 系统自主建立物理模型，用以解释量子材料中此前未发表的实验观测。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了材料科学中自主理论发现（autonomous theoretical discovery）的路径：AI 智能体借助第一性原理计算，将实验观测转化为物理模型与可检验预测（testable predictions）。

Abstract: Advances in experimental instrumentation and automation generate increasingly rich datasets, but turning experimental observations into microscopic understanding remains a bottleneck in scientific discovery. To accelerate this process, we introduce AI Theorist, a system of artificial intelligence (AI) agents for autonomous discovery of physical models through hypothesis generation, first-principles calculations and evidence-driven refinement. We apply the framework to $α$-RuCl$_3$, a leading candidate material for realizing a Kitaev quantum spin liquid, to investigate its electronic structure through optical spectra. AI Theorist develops a new interpretation of the optical and photocurrent observations, identifying distinct excitonic states with contrasting optical selection rules and real-space distributions. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of an AI system autonomously developing a physical model to explain previously unpublished experimental observations in a quantum material, utilizing first-principles electronic-structure and many-body calculations. Our results establish a route to autonomous theoretical discovery in materials science, in which AI agents use first-principles calculations to turn experimental observations into physical models and testable predictions.

</details>


### [87] [An Automated and Reproducible Workflow for Crack Identification and Damage Assessment of Fusion Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03505)
*Rinkle Juneja,Viktor Reshniak,Richard K. Archibald,John W. Duggan,Gregory R. Watson,Cory D. Hauck,Gary M. Staebler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套在 Galaxy 科学工作流环境（scientific workflow environment）中实现的可复现工作流，用于从扫描电子显微镜（SEM）图像中自动识别裂纹并量化损伤，并将其与下游机器学习预测和物理模拟模块衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辐照后显微分析（post-exposure microscopy）是聚变材料（fusion materials）合格性评定的核心环节，但人工分析无法应对现代聚变材料研究中图像数量庞大、异质性强、多分辨率的特点。因此需要可扩展且可复现的自动化分析方案。

Method: 工作流以 SEM 图像与实验元数据（experimental metadata）为输入，在 Galaxy 环境中完成裂纹识别、损伤量化，并保留中间产物与处理历史以保证可复现性。方法设计目标为无需针对具体图像调参，即可跨钨（tungsten）牌号、微观组织、放大倍数与损伤状态运行。

Result: 在稀疏电子束热冲击（electron-beam thermal-shock）数据集上进行了验证，该数据集含 114 个实验、418 张图像，覆盖五种钨牌号与三种微观组织状态。输出包括裂纹掩膜（crack masks）、骨架化裂纹网络（skeletonized crack networks）、质控可视化（quality-control visualizations）及标量损伤描述符（scalar damage descriptors），并定义了裂纹密度描述符（crack-density descriptor）。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一套端到端、可共享、计算可移植（computationally portable）的工作流，将实验表征、自动图像分析、初步损伤预测与模拟引导的数据采集（simulation-guided data acquisition）相连接；预测与模拟组件被有意设计为同一 Galaxy 环境中的可扩展模块。

Abstract: Post-exposure microscopy is central to qualification of fusion materials. However, manual analysis does not scale to the volume, heterogeneity, and multiresolution character of modern fusion-materials campaigns. To address this challenge, we present a reproducible workflow, implemented in the Galaxy scientific workflow environment, for automated crack identification and quantitative damage assessment from scanning electron microscopy images. The workflow processes SEM images and experimental metadata to identify cracks, quantify damage, and retain the intermediate products and processing history needed for reproducibility. Outputs include crack masks, skeletonized crack networks, quality-control visualizations, and scalar damage descriptors. The method is designed to operate without image-specific parameter tuning across tungsten grades, microstructures, magnifications, and damage states. We demonstrate the workflow on a sparse electron-beam thermal-shock dataset containing 418 images from 114 experiments spanning five tungsten grades and three microstructural states. We define a crack-density descriptor, which provides standardized inputs for downstream machine-learning prediction and physics-based crack simulation. These predictive components are exposed in the same Galaxy environment and are intentionally treated here as extensible workflow modules. The principal contribution is therefore an end-to-end, shareable, and computationally portable workflow that links experimental characterization, automated image analysis, preliminary damage prediction, and simulation-guided data acquisition for fusion-materials research.

</details>

### [88] [Lower bound on Dirac resonances with mass-type potentials in the semi-classical limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02356)
*Zhuo Chen,Michael Melgaard*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为三维半经典狄拉克算子（semiclassical Dirac operator）与质量型势（mass-type potential）建立共振（resonance）数目下界：在相对分布 μ 的支撑集边界点 E₀>1 附近（及其对称点 -E₀），两算子合计至少存在 Cℏ⁻³ 个共振（计重数），该指数与 Khochman 的上界一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为带质量型势的三维半经典狄拉克算子，目标是给出其共振数目的定量下界。摘要未展开更广泛背景陈述，核心动机在于将指数与已有上界匹配，并揭示 E₀ 的陷获能量（trapping energy）性质。

Method: 对满足外部分析性（exterior analyticity）、衰减与非退化假设的算子对 D_j=D_0+βv_j，构造相应质量函数的相对分布（relative distribution）μ；将 μ 的解析波前奇异性（analytic wave-front singularity）转移到相对经典态密度（relative classical density of states），并结合 Khochman 局部迹公式（local trace formula）与相对半经典迹公式（relative semiclassical trace formula）完成证明。

Result: 若 E₀>1 为 supp μ 的边界点，则 E₀ 与 -E₀ 的任意复邻域内两算子合计至少含 Cℏ⁻³ 个共振（计重数）。作为推论，对单个有界质量型势 v（外扇区解析且具幂次衰减，inf(1+v)>0 且 sup v>0），算子 D=D_0+βv 在 E₀=1+sup v 及 -E₀ 的任意复邻域内，对充分小的 ℏ 至少含 Cℏ⁻³ 个共振。所用指数与 Khochman 上界相符，两类共振族由谱对称性（spectral symmetry）相联系，且 E₀ 必为陷获能量。

Conclusion: 研究给出质量型势下三维半经典狄拉克算子共振数目的匹配型下界，与既有上界在幂次上一致，并建立共振族的谱对称关系与 E₀ 的陷获性质。此外，证明过程中所需的相对半经典迹公式被推广到一般厄米矩阵值扰动（Hermitian matrix-valued perturbations）。

Abstract: We establish lower bounds on the number of resonances for three-dimensional semiclassical Dirac operators with mass-type potentials. For a pair $\Dl_j=\Dl_0+\mbfβv_j$, $j=1,2$, satisfying the exterior analyticity, decay and non-degeneracy assumptions of the paper, we associate a relative distribution $μ$ to the corresponding mass functions. We prove that if $E_0>1$ is a boundary point of $\suppμ$, then every complex neighbourhood of $E_0$, and likewise of $-E_0$, contains in total at least $C\hbar^{-3}$ resonances of the two operators, counted with multiplicity. The proof transfers an analytic wave-front singularity of $μ$ to the relative classical density of states and combines this with Khochman's local trace formula and a relative semiclassical trace formula. As a consequence, if $v$ is a single bounded mass-type potential, analytic with power decay in an exterior sector, with $\inf(1+v)>0$ and $\sup v>0$, then $\Dl=\Dl_0+\mbfβv$ has at least $C\hbar^{-3}$ resonances in every complex neighbourhood of $E_0=1+\sup v$ and of $-E_0$, for all sufficiently small $\hbar$. The exponent agrees with Khochman's upper bound, the two resonance families are related by spectral symmetry, and $E_0$ is necessarily a trapping energy. We also prove the relative semiclassical trace formula needed in the argument for general Hermitian matrix-valued perturbations.

</details>


### [89] [Döblin--Fourier cancellation and kinetic Aleksandrov estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03083)
*Amélie Loher,Connor Mooney,Clément Mouhot*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为非散度型（non-divergence form）线性二阶动理学方程（kinetic equations）建立一种相消机制（cancellation mechanism），由此得到带增强耗散（enhanced dissipation）的指数 Fourier 衰减估计，并给出动理学 Aleksandrov 估计与环面-球面几何下增强耗散及趋衡收敛两项应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有可测一致椭圆系数的非散度型动理学方程，需要克服粗糙系数下解的正则性缺失，建立可量化的衰减与正则性估计。此前的衰减结果尚未覆盖粗糙系数情形。

Method: 将动力学分解为空间波（spatial waves），沿两族速度路径追踪相位近乎相反的波；用抛物型 Krylov–Safonov 理论给出两个速度边缘分布（velocity marginals）的共同下界，使两部分贡献在小的相位误差内相消，得到 à la Döblin 型收缩（contraction），迭代该收缩得到指数 Fourier 衰减。

Result: 得到三项结果：其一为动理学 Aleksandrov 估计（kinetic Aleksandrov estimate），对不依赖位置的粗糙系数 A(t,v) 在任意 p>2n+1 下成立（n 为位置与速度维度），且一维自治情形 a(x,v) 在任意 p>4 下成立，两个阈值在所有椭圆比下均最优；其二是环面位置与球面速度、系数 A(t,v) 情形下的增强耗散达到最优平方根频率幂次及 Gevrey 正则性，自治系数 A=A(v) 时还得到 L^2（按不变速度测度加权）的指数趋衡与定量谱隙（spectral gap）。

Conclusion: 该相消机制为粗糙系数动理学方程提供了衰减与正则性分析框架；所得结果为首次针对粗糙系数动理学方程建立衰减结论的工作。

Abstract: We develop a cancellation mechanism for linear second-order kinetic equations in non-divergence form with measurable uniformly elliptic coefficients. We decompose the dynamics into spatial waves and follow two families of velocity paths along which the waves acquire nearly opposite phases. The parabolic Krylov--Safonov theory gives a common lower bound for the two velocity marginals. The corresponding contributions cancel up to a small phase error, producing a contraction \textit{à la Döblin}. Iterating this contraction yields exponential Fourier decay estimates with enhanced dissipation.
  We then present two applications.
  The first contribution is a kinetic Aleksandrov estimate: a maximum principle in which the source is measured in an $L^p$ norm. For rough coefficients $A(t,v)$ independent of position, we obtain the estimate for every $p>2n+1$, where $n$ is the dimension of position and velocity. The Fourier decay also gives spatial smoothness, and parabolic regularity gives Hölder continuity in time and velocity. For autonomous coefficients $a(x,v)$ in dimension one, position serves as time away from zero velocity. A Harnack comparison controls returns to small velocity intervals and yields the estimate for every $p>4$. Both thresholds are optimal among those valid for all ellipticity ratios.
  The second contribution concerns position on the torus and velocity on the sphere, with measurable uniformly elliptic diffusion coefficients $A(t,v)$ independent of position. We prove enhanced dissipation with the optimal square-root frequency power and Gevrey regularity in position. For autonomous coefficients $A=A(v)$, we also obtain exponential convergence to equilibrium in $L^2$ weighted by the invariant velocity measure, and a quantitative spectral gap. To our knowledge, these are the first results on decay established for laws of kinetic equations with rough coefficients.

</details>

### [90] [Complex-momentum form factors from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03553)
*Maxwell T. Hansen,Gurtej Kanwar*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出对格点欧几里得关联函数直接施加双边拉普拉斯变换（bilateral Laplace transform），无需解析延拓（analytic continuation）或求解逆问题，即可获得复动量下的形状因子（form factor），并以QCDπ介子形状因子及二维O(3)非线性sigma模型蒙特卡洛数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上从欧几里得位置空间关联函数获取复动量形状因子需解析延拓或求解病态逆问题，过程复杂且不稳定。本文旨在建立一种直接、无需逆问题求解的途径。

Method: 对格点数据直接施加双边拉普拉斯变换，从而给出在收敛区域内的解析延拓形状因子；先在无限体积关联函数上建立方法，再讨论有限体积估计量，包括位置空间拟合及以虚扭曲角边界条件实现的空间化学势（spatial chemical potential）。

Result: 以QCDπ介子形状因子为例，可在实部大于−4m_π^2的所有复Q^2构成的扩展解析区域内，将观测量表达为不变Q^2的函数；并在二维O(3)非线性sigma模型上用蒙特卡洛数据检验了该方法。

Conclusion: 该方法可直接用于改进电荷半径（charge radius）测定，或为基于共形映射（conformal mapping）的拟合提供更完整数据，且具备有限体积实际计算的可行估计方案。

Abstract: We demonstrate that form factors evaluated at complex-valued momenta can be directly accessed from position-space Euclidean correlators without analytically continuing the latter and without solving an inverse problem. The approach directly applies a bilateral Laplace transform to the lattice data, which provides the analytically continued form factor wherever it converges. Focusing on the pion form factor in Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD), this gives access to the observable as a function of invariant \(Q^2\) throughout an extended analytic domain consisting of all complex \(Q^2\) with real part larger than \(-4m_π^2\). This could, for example, be used to improve charge-radius determinations or provide more complete data for fits based on conformal mapping of the data. After developing the approach for infinite-volume correlators, we discuss various options for finite-volume estimators suitable for realistic calculations, including position-space fits or application of a spatial chemical potential represented by boundary conditions with an imaginary twist angle. Finally, we test the method in the two-dimensional \(O(3)\) nonlinear sigma model using Monte Carlo data.

</details>


### [91] [Critical coupling and $Δ\langleφ^2\rangle$ in three-dimensional $φ^4$ theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03028)
*Stephan Durr,Tolga S. H. Kiel*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 在三维欧几里得空间中研究实标量 φ⁴ 理论，通过格点正规化确定对称相与破缺相之间的临界参数，经连续极限外推得到普适常数 f_c = 18.808(78)_tot。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在精确确定三维 φ⁴ 理论在连续极限与无穷体积极限下的临界（裸）参数行为，从而为标量场论的普适性（universality）提供高精度数值检验。

Method: 对多种自耦合（self-coupling）$\hatλ$，在格点正规化（lattice regularization）下确定系统从对称相（symmetric phase）过渡到破缺相（broken phase）的临界裸参数 $\hatμ_{0\mathrm{c}}^2(\hatλ)$；随后将盒体积趋于无穷并转换到普适方案（universal scheme），得到 $\hatμ_\mathrm{c}^2(\hatλ)$，最后进行连续外推（continuum extrapolation）。

Result: 连续极限下 $f_\mathrm{c}=\lim_{\hatλ\to0}\hatλ^2/\hatμ_\mathrm{c}^2(\hatλ)$ 的数值结果为 $f_\mathrm{c}=18.808(64)_\mathrm{stat}(45)_\mathrm{sys}=18.808(78)_\mathrm{tot}$；同时在连续与无穷体积联合极限下得到 $Δ\langleφ^2\rangle_\mathrm{c}/λ=-0.00303(12)_\mathrm{stat}(12)_\mathrm{sys}=-0.00303(17)_\mathrm{tot}$。

Conclusion: 论文给出了三维实标量 φ⁴ 理论中 $f_\mathrm{c}$ 与 $Δ\langleφ^2\rangle_\mathrm{c}/λ$ 在连续、无穷体积极限下的高精度测定值，为相关普适类（universality class）提供了定量约束。

Abstract: We consider the $φ^4$ theory with $φ(x)\in\mathbb{R}$ in three Euclidean dimensions. For a variety of self-couplings $\hatλ$, the critical (bare) parameter $\hatμ_{0\mathrm{c}}^2(\hatλ)$ is determined where the lattice-regulated system changes from the symmetric phase to the broken phase. Next, we send the box volume to infinity and switch to a universal scheme; this yields $\hatμ_\mathrm{c}^2(\hatλ)$ for each simulated $\hatλ$. Finally, a continuum extrapolation gives $f_\mathrm{c}=\lim_{\hatλ\to0} \hatλ^2/\hatμ_\mathrm{c}^2(\hatλ)$, and we find $f_\mathrm{c} = 18.808(64)_{\mathrm{stat}}(45)_{\mathrm{sys}} = 18.808(78)_{\mathrm{tot}}$. In addition, $Δ\langleφ^2\rangle_\mathrm{c}/λ= -0.00303(12)_{\mathrm{stat}}(12)_{\mathrm{sys}} = -0.00303(17)_{\mathrm{tot}}$ is determined in the combined continuum and infinite-volume limit.

</details>

### [92] [Stochastic thermodynamics and predictability of Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes: an application to the Madden-Julian Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03355)
*Roberta Benincasa,Danni Du,Gregory S. Duane,Jeffrey B. Weiss*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了针对 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（OU）过程的粗粒化随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）框架，并通过线性逆模型（linear inverse models, LIMs）应用于气候数据，揭示气候振荡的可预测性与非平衡热力学性质之间的联系，指出路径作用量（path action）与异常相关系数（anomaly correlation coefficient, ACC）关系最密切。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候振荡本质上是非平衡（out-of-equilibrium）现象，但其可预测性与非平衡热力学性质之间的关联尚未被探索。气候振荡常在气候指数构成的二维相空间中研究，且这些指数在时间反演下为偶变量，这促使作者聚焦于含偶变量的二维 OU 动力学。

Method: 作者为 OU 过程构建了粗粒化随机热力学框架，并通过线性逆模型（LIMs）应用于气候数据，将可预测性、熵产生（entropy production）、动力学活动（dynamical activity）与路径作用量（path action）联系起来。研究发现二维 OU 过程的坐标不变性质可约化为由两个扩散率和一个相空间旋转构成的极小三参数描述。

Result: 这三类量从根本上刻画了动力学的不同方面；更高的不可逆性（irreversibility）与动力学活动并不必然意味着可预测性降低，而与常用可预测性度量 ACC 联系最紧密的是路径作用量。以二十世纪 Madden-Julian Oscillation（MJO）为例，MJO 在变得更具可预测性的同时，也变得更为不可逆且更活跃；可预测性主要由平均扩散的降低所驱动，而熵产生则反映了三个参数的共同演化。

Conclusion: 结果表明随机热力学为研究非平衡气候现象提供了有力的视角。

Abstract: Climate oscillations are intrinsically out-of-equilibrium phenomena, yet the connection between their predictability and nonequilibrium thermodynamic properties remains unexplored. We develop a coarse-grained stochastic thermodynamic framework for Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) processes and apply this framework to climate data via linear inverse models (LIMs), linking predictability, entropy production, dynamical activity, and path action. Climate oscillations are often studied in a 2d phase space of climate indices, which are even under time-reversal, motivating our focus on 2d OU dynamics with even variables. We find that coordinate-invariant properties of 2d OU processes can be reduced to a minimal three-parameter description: two diffusivities and a phase-space rotation. We show that these quantities fundamentally characterize distinct aspects of the dynamics. We find that higher irreversibility and dynamical activity do not necessarily imply reduced predictability. Rather, it is the path action which is most closely connected to a commonly used measure of predictability, the anomaly correlation coefficient (ACC). As an illustrative example, we apply this framework to the Madden-Julian Oscillation (MJO) over the twentieth century. We find that the MJO has become simultaneously more predictable, as seen previously, while at the same time becoming more irreversible and more active. Predictability is driven predominantly by the reduced mean diffusion. Entropy production reflects the combined evolution of all three parameters. These results suggest that stochastic thermodynamics provides a powerful lens for studying nonequilibrium climate phenomena.

</details>

### [93] [Zero measure Cantor spectrum for aperiodic metric tiling graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02650)
*Ram Band,Gilad Sofer*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究由一维铺砌（one-dimensional tilings）生成的一类非周期度量图（aperiodic metric graphs），证明 Kirchhoff 拉普拉斯算子（Kirchhoff Laplacian）的谱通常为勒贝格测度零（Lebesgue measure zero），且除离散孤立特征值外为广义 Cantor 集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的度量图（metric graphs）相关结果多假设等长边（equilateral edges）并依赖对称性，从而将问题化归为实轴上的 Sturm-Liouville 算子；本文旨在突破这些限制，将离散遍历 Schrödinger 算子（discrete ergodic Schrödinger operators）在 Z 上的若干已知结论推广到更一般的度量图族。

Method: 研究对象为由一维铺砌（one-dimensional tilings）生成的一族非周期度量图（aperiodic metric graphs），其边不必等长，也不具备可化归为 Sturm-Liouville 问题的对称性，因此需要采用不同于既有工作的分析路径。

Result: 一般情形下，相关 Kirchhoff 拉普拉斯算子（Kirchhoff Laplacian）的谱具有勒贝格测度零（Lebesgue measure zero），且除离散的孤立特征值（isolated eigenvalues）集合外为广义 Cantor 集；对其中一大子类图，进一步证明孤立特征值通常是缺失的。

Conclusion: 该工作将离散遍历 Schrödinger 算子（discrete ergodic Schrödinger operators）在 Z 上的若干经典谱结论推广至一大类非等长、无相应对称性的度量图（metric graphs），拓展了度量图上非周期谱理论的结果范围。

Abstract: We study a family of aperiodic metric graphs generated by one-dimensional tilings. We prove that, generically, the spectrum of the associated Kirchhoff Laplacian has Lebesgue measure zero and, apart from a discrete set of isolated eigenvalues, is a generalized Cantor set. For a large subclass of these graphs, we further show that isolated eigenvalues are generically absent. This extends several well-known results from the setting of discrete ergodic Schrödinger operators on $\mathbb{Z}$ to a large family of metric graphs. Earlier results of this kind for metric graphs typically assume equilateral edges and exploit symmetries that reduce the analysis to a Sturm-Liouville operator on the real line. By contrast, the graph families considered here need not have equilateral edges or the symmetries needed for such a reduction and therefore require a different analytical approach.

</details>

### [94] [Parity-Time Symmetry of Plasma Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03433)
*Hong Qin*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该研究揭示均匀无碰撞磁化等离子体的极化率张量具有宇称-时间（parity-time, PT）对称性，并将此归因于底层动理学系统的PT对称性；动理学不稳定性本质上对应于自发PT对称破缺，且仅通过Krein碰撞实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁化等离子体中动理学不稳定性的深层结构原理尚不明确，需要寻找统一的数学框架来解释非单调分布、环状分布、损失锥及共振高能粒子等所引发的多种不稳定性。作者试图建立PT对称性作为等离子体波与不稳定性的结构性原理。

Method: 在波矢k与平衡磁场B₀位于x–z平面的稳态回旋各向同性平衡下，分析极化率张量的变换关系χ(ω*,k)=Rχ(ω,k)*R（R=diag(1,−1,1)）；在电磁动理学本征模空间上定义宇称P与时间反演T，证明线性化Vlasov–Maxwell哈密顿算符满足PT H_VM (PT)⁻¹=H_VM，并建立均匀磁化Vlasov–Poisson本征问题的PT对称性。

Result: 确认极化率PT对称性是底层动理学系统PT对称性在约化响应空间的表现；表明非单调分布、环、损失锥和共振高能粒子相关的许多动理学不稳定性本质上是自发PT对称破缺，且只能经Krein碰撞实现。三个算例显示：定号回旋残数保护热Bernstein模免于自发PT破缺；非单调Dory–Guest–Harris分布允许变号残数与Krein碰撞，据此推导了解析不稳定性阈值与增长率；新发现的模-极共振不稳定性提供自发PT破缺的另一种实现。

Conclusion: 结果确立PT对称性作为等离子体波与不稳定性的结构性原理，并为研究动理学等离子体中的谱拓扑开辟了新方向。

Abstract: The susceptibility tensor of a homogeneous collisionless magnetized plasma possesses parity-time (PT) symmetry. For a stationary gyrotropic equilibrium, with the wave vector $\mathbf{k}$ and the equilibrium magnetic field $\mathbf{B}_0$ in the $x$--$z$ plane, the kinetic response satisfies $χ(ω^*,\mathbf{k})=Rχ(ω,\mathbf{k})^*R$, where $R=\operatorname{diag}(1,-1,1)$. We show that the susceptibility PT-symmetry is a reduced-response-space manifestation of PT symmetry of the underpinning kinetic system. We identify parity $P$ and time reversal $T$ on the electromagnetic kinetic eigenmode space and show that the Hamiltonian operator $\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{VM}}$ of the linearized Vlasov--Maxwell system is PT-symmetric, satisfying $PT\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{VM}}(PT)^{-1}=\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{VM}}$. The PT symmetry of the homogeneous magnetized Vlasov--Poisson eigenproblem is also established. Many kinetic instabilities associated with nonmonotonic distributions, rings, loss cones, and resonant energetic-particle populations are fundamentally spontaneous PT-symmetry breaking, which is achieved through and only through Krein collisions. Three examples demonstrate distinct utilities of this analysis. Definite-sign cyclotron residues protect thermal Bernstein modes from spontaneous PT-symmetry breaking. The nonmonotonic Dory--Guest--Harris distribution permits sign-indefinite residues and Krein collisions, for which we derive an analytical instability threshold and growth rate. The recently discovered mode-pole resonance instability provides a distinct realization of spontaneous PT-symmetry breaking and illustrates how PT symmetry can guide the discovery of kinetic instabilities with applications to fusion energy. These results establish PT symmetry as a structural principle of plasma waves and instabilities and open new directions for studying spectral topology in kinetic plasmas.

</details>
