### [95] [Quantizing the exterior region of a Kerr-AdS black hole leads to a resolution of the information paradox at the quantum level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02237)
*Claus Gerhardt*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文采用作者的量子引力模型对奇数维、各转动参数相等的Kerr-AdS黑洞外部区域进行量子化，得到双曲型方程，其解由时间本征函数与空间本征分布构成。作者论证，若同时考虑内部区域量子化，则可消除外部区域本征值重数的歧义，并由此在内外希尔伯特空间与哈密顿量之间建立幺正等价，进而主张量子层面不存在信息悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注黑洞信息悖论（information paradox），试图检验其量子引力模型能否在量子层面给出结论。作者特别指出，单独量子化外部区域时，本征值重数取值缺乏判定准则，因此需要引入内部区域以消除这一歧义。

Method: 方法为：对奇数维、所有转动参数相等的Kerr-AdS黑洞外部区域实施量子化，导出双曲型方程，并用时间本征函数（temporal eigenfunctions）与空间本征分布（spatial eigendistributions）的乘积形式求解。论证将内部区域量子化纳入考虑，以确定重数（multiplicities）取值，并通过内外区域时间哈密顿量（temporal Hamiltonian）相同、本征值一致，构造两侧希尔伯特空间与哈密顿量之间的幺正等价（unitary equivalence）。

Result: 所得本征函数对应本征值重数均为1，空间本征分布对应同一本征值但重数为m_i≥1，且原则上m_i可任意大。仅考虑外部区域时，m_i取值无判定准则；纳入内部区域量子化后，该歧义不再出现，因为重数可取最大值，并可自然沿用至外部区域。内外本征值因哈密顿量相同而重合。

Conclusion: 该选择在内外希尔伯特空间及各自哈密顿量之间定义了幺正等价。作者认为，这种幺正等价保留下黑洞内外区域所有正常态（normal states）所携带的完整量子统计信息，故在量子层面不存在信息悖论。

Abstract: We quantize the exterior region of an odd-dimensional Kerr-AdS black hole, in which all rotational parameters are equal, using our model of quantum gravity. The resulting hyperbolic equation is solved by products of temporal eigenfunctions $w_i$, whose corresponding eigenvalues all have multiplicity one, and spatial eigendistributions $v_{ij}$ corresponding to the same eigenvalues but having multiplicities $m_i\ge 1$. In principle, the $m_i$ may be arbitrarily large. When only the exterior region is considered, there is no criterion for determining the values of the $m_i$. However, when the quantization of the interior region is also considered, the same ambiguity does not arise, because the multiplicities can be chosen to be maximal. It is therefore natural to use the same values in the exterior region. Because the temporal Hamiltonian is the same in both regions, the interior and exterior eigenvalues coincide, and this choice defines a unitary equivalence between the respective Hilbert spaces and Hamiltonians. The unitary equivalence between the Hilbert spaces and their respective Hamiltonians preserves the complete quantum-statistical information carried by all normal states in the interior and exterior regions of the black hole. Hence, there is no information paradox at the quantum level.

</details>


### [96] [Simpson-Visser regularization of the quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder-Datt black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02266)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,İzzet Sakallı*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用 Simpson-Visser 处方对圈量子宇宙学中的量子 Oppenheimer-Snyder-Datt（qOSD）黑洞进行球对称几何推广，系统分析其正则性、能量条件、视界相图、测地线/阴影、热力学、拓扑荷以及准正规模（quasinormal modes）与霍金辐射等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将 Simpson-Visser 正则化方案（r→√(r²+a²)）应用于带有 α 修正的 qOSD 黑洞，构建一个可在 qOSD 黑洞、Simpson-Visser 黑洞反弹（black bounce）与 Schwarzschild 解之间插值的统一几何族，并考察反弹参数 a 与量子修正 α 的物理效应。

Method: 采用解析几何分析研究曲率标量与零能量条件；通过 (α,a) 参数平面分类视界结构；依据面积半径分析圆轨道、光子球、阴影、ISCO 与进动频率；推导视界热力学与一阶定律熵；计算标量、电磁与 Dirac 场的准正规模（quasinormal modes），使用三阶 WKB、Chebyshev 谱配置法与时间域积分三种方法互相验证。

Result: 几何对所有 a&gt;0 正则，喉部 Ricci 标量与 α 无关而 Kretschmann 标量依赖 α，零能量条件在喉部以 −1/(4πa²) 被破坏；(α,a) 平面分为双视界正则黑洞、带隐藏反弹的单视界黑洞、无视界柯西面及可穿越虫洞四类区域；阴影仅约束 α，而 ringdown 阻尼与双峰准周期振荡比对 a 敏感；反弹将热力学拓扑荷由 W=−1 变为 W=0；三种方法给出的准正规模谱彼此一致并符合 eikonal 标度，虫洞区域双峰势产生回声（echoes）。

Conclusion: 该插值几何族在中间区域无需微调即可消除正则黑洞的质量膨胀（mass inflation）通道，其可观测特征（阴影、ringdown、QPO 比值、回声）可分别用于约束 α 与 a，为区分量子修正与反弹几何提供了潜在观测判据。

Abstract: We study the spherical geometry from the Simpson-Visser prescription $r\to\sqrt{r^2+a^2}$ to the quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder-Datt (qOSD) black hole of loop quantum cosmology, whose lapse function $A=1-2M/R+αM^2/R^4$ carries the correction $α$. This family interpolates between the qOSD black hole ($a\to0$), the Simpson-Visser black bounce ($α\to0$), and Schwarzschild. The geometry is regular for every $a>0$, the Ricci scalar at the throat is blind to $α$ while the Kretschmann scalar is not, and the null energy condition is violated at the throat by $-1/(4πa^2)$. The $(α,a)$ plane splits into a two-horizon regular black hole, a single-horizon black hole with a hidden bounce, no Cauchy horizon, and a traversable wormhole; the middle region removes the mass-inflation channel of regular black holes without fine tuning. Because circular orbits are governed by the areal radius, photon sphere, shadow, ISCO and azimuthal epicyclic frequency depend on $α$ only, whereas the Lyapunov exponent and radial epicyclic frequency carry the factor $\sqrt{1-a^2/R^2}$: shadow constrains only $α$, while ringdown damping and the twin-peak quasiperiodic-oscillation ratio are sensitive to $a$. We derive horizon thermodynamics with a first-law entropy that departs from the area law, show that the bounce changes the thermodynamic topological charge from $W=-1$ to $W=0$ at every $α$, and compute scalar, electromagnetic and Dirac quasinormal spectra using third-order WKB, Chebyshev spectral collocation and time-domain integration, which agree with one another and with eikonal scaling. Greybody bounds, Hawking emission, cascade sparsity and ringdown quality factor complete the analysis, and in the wormhole region the double-peaked potential produces echoes.

</details>


### [97] [Chaotic signatures in extreme-mass-ratio systems surrounded by bosonic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02317)
*Qi-Xuan Xu,Kyriakos Destounis,Yin-Da Guo,Richard Brito,Taillte May*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在旋转黑洞周围存在标量云（scalar cloud）时，保守轨道动力学可因环境效应而破坏可积性（integrability），表现为有限宽度的共振岛（resonant islands），强而致密的云还会引发混沌运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端质量比旋进（extreme-mass-ratio inspirals）是检验强引力与大质量黑洞环境的重要引力波源；Kerr 时空中的轨道运动本为可积，但真实黑洞周围的环境可能破坏可积性，故需考察环境对保守轨道动力学的影响。

Method: 采用完全非线性数值解（fully nonlinear numerical solutions）与解析后牛顿模型（post-Newtonian model），研究被标量云包围的旋转黑洞周围的保守轨道动力学，并通过庞加莱截面（Poincaré sections）与旋转曲线（rotation curves）进行分析。

Result: 庞加莱截面与旋转曲线显示有限宽度的共振岛，即使云质量仅占黑洞质量很小比例也出现非可积性证据；后牛顿描述在弱云情形下可再现主导共振及其岛宽，但在更强、更致密的云情形下低估岛宽；当标量云质量与黑洞相当，相空间出现更宽的共振岛及散布层，指示混沌运动。

Conclusion: 结合此前工作，结果表明环境效应可普遍性地破坏测地线运动（geodesic motion）的可积性，从而有必要在纳入辐射反作用（radiation reaction）后进一步研究共振穿越及其引力波特征。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals are key gravitational-wave sources for testing strong gravity and the environments of massive black holes. While orbital motion in Kerr black hole spacetimes is integrable, realistic black holes may be surrounded by environments that break integrability. We investigate conservative orbital dynamics around rotating black holes surrounded by scalar clouds using fully nonlinear numerical solutions and an analytical post-Newtonian model. Poincaré sections and rotation curves reveal finite-width resonant islands, providing evidence for non-integrability even when the cloud carries only a small fraction of the black-hole mass. We show that the post-Newtonian description reproduces the dominant resonance and its island width for weak clouds, but underestimates the width in the configurations with stronger, more compact clouds examined here. Moreover, we find that for a scalar cloud as massive as the black hole, the phase space exhibits wider resonant islands as well as scattered layers indicative of chaotic motion. Together with earlier work, these results show that environmental effects can generically break the integrability of geodesic motion, motivating further study of resonance crossings and their gravitational-wave signatures once radiation reaction is included.

</details>


### [98] [Can the gravitational waves emitted by Eta Carinae be detected?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02532)
*Odylio D. Aguiar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出利用脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）技术搜寻船底座η（Eta Carinae）发出的引力波，目标并非探测背景信号，而是识别一个强且孤立的源；并建议将四颗特定脉冲星纳入搜寻网络，借助双星轨道高偏心率产生的谐波结构辅助信号识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTA工作多集中于探测引力波背景（background），而对强且孤立的引力波源（isolated source）的搜寻尚未实现。船底座η作为潜在强引力波源，为这一未完成任务提供了候选目标。

Method: 采用脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing array, PTA）技术，并建议将 PSR J0952-0607、PSR J1719-1438、PSR J2322-2650 和 PSR J1909-3744 纳入搜寻网络。选星依据为其较高的残差与脉冲宽度比（residual-to-pulse-width ratios）以及有利于张量偏振（tensor polarizations）探测的空间位置。数据分析需对照抵达地球的船底座η引力波信号模型进行。

Result: 摘要未给出实际搜寻结果，仅指出部分原始数据（raw data）可能已被记录且可用；剩余工作为将这些数据与引力波信号模型进行比对分析。

Conclusion: 船底座η双星轨道的高偏心率（0.9）可能在发射的引力波中产生谐波（harmonics），从而有助于在数据中识别信号。整体而言，该研究提出的是一条尚未完成的搜寻路径，其可行性依赖于现有数据与信号模型的比对。

Abstract: In this article, we propose a search for gravitational waves emitted by Eta Carinae using the PTA technique, not to detect a background, but rather a strong, isolated source (a task not yet accomplished). We suggest including pulsars PSR J0952-0607, PSR J1719-1438, PSR J2322-2650, and PSR J1909-3744 in the search network, as they exhibit relatively high residual-to-pulse-width ratios and spatial positions that favor the detection of tensor polarizations. The high eccentricity (0.9) of the Eta Carinae binary orbit may facilitate signal identification within the data due to the presence of harmonics in the emitted gravitational waves. It is possible that some of the raw data have already been recorded and are available; the remaining step is to analyze them against a model of the gravitational wave signal arriving at Earth from Eta Carinae.

</details>


### [99] [Multi-Band Constraints on Cosmic Strings: Unifying Harmonic and Burst Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02590)
*Hansong Zhang,Huai-Ke Guo,Mairi Sakellariadou,Fengwei Yang,Yue Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作统一了宇宙弦环（cosmic-string loops）引力波背景（GWB）的离散谐波展开与连续爆发叠加两种形式，并据此进行多波段联合贝叶斯推断，加强了对弦张力 \(G\mu\) 的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙弦环产生的宽带引力波背景（GWB）通常由两种互不相同的形式描述：脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）研究常用离散谐波展开，地面GWB搜索则采用连续爆发叠加。二者缺乏统一处理，导致跨频段谱计算不一致，妨碍多波段推断。

Method: 形式上统一两种形式，指出其为同一辐射过程的离散与连续表示，并分析其在标度不变谱平台及低频上升分支上的收敛与分歧行为。随后在统一框架下，利用 NANOGrav、EPTA 与 LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA（LVK）数据集，对四种代表性弦环分布模型进行联合贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference），同时采样弦张力 \(G\mu\) 与平均尖点、扭结数目 \(\{N_c, N_k\}\)。

Result: 在标度不变谱平台处两种形式收敛，而在由前几个谐波主导的低频上升分支上，离散性质变得重要并使二者出现分歧。采用谐波展开使其中三个模型的 LVK 限制（bounds）加强 \(21\%-31\%\)；同时，严格的 LVK 限制排除了其中三个模型解释所观测到的 PTA 公共信号（common signal）的可能性。

Conclusion: 统一框架使跨频段的谱计算更一致，从而支持多波段推断（multi-band inference）。对于唯一存活的模型，多波段分析改进了对 \(G\mu\) 的推断上限。

Abstract: Cosmic-string loops generate a broadband gravitational-wave background (GWB) that is typically modeled via two distinct formalisms: a discrete harmonic expansion, commonly used in pulsar-timing-array (PTA) studies, and a continuous burst superposition, employed in ground-based GWB searches. In this work, we formally unify the two formalisms, showing that they provide discrete and continuous representations of the same underlying radiation process and converge on the scale-invariant spectrum plateau. On the rising low-frequency branch, where the first few harmonics dominate, the discrete nature becomes important, causing the two formalisms to diverge. Within this unified framework, the spectral calculation is more consistent across frequency bands, enabling multi-band inference. Leveraging this physical insight, we perform joint Bayesian inference across four representative loop-distribution models using the NANOGrav, EPTA, and LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA (LVK) datasets. By jointly sampling the string tension $Gμ$ alongside the average cusp and kink numbers $\{N_c, N_k\}$, the loop-radiation uncertainties are accounted for. We find that employing the harmonic expansion strengthens the LVK bounds by $21\%-31\%$ for three of these models. Additionally, the stringent LVK bounds preclude three of these models from explaining the observed PTA common signal. For the sole surviving model, our multi-band analysis improves the inferred limit on $Gμ$.

</details>


### [100] [Non-Gaussian timing residual skewness in pulsar timing arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02735)
*Reginald Christian Bernardo,Rituparna Mandal,Kin-Wang Ng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文受宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）中弱原初非高斯性（primordial non-Gaussianity）搜寻的启发，利用脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing arrays, PTA）研究高斯随机引力波背景统计的微扰偏离。作者提出一个二次型（quadratic）拟设，在非线性耦合一阶给出三点相关、二阶给出协方差修正；但该拟设在引力波螺旋振幅上产生的计时残差双谱（bispectrum）在张量与矢量模的局域极化基旋转下并不显式不变。随后构建极化标架不变的现象学模型，其单脉冲星计时残差三阶矩对应的归一化偏度极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机来自对CMB中弱原初非高斯性的搜寻，并将类似思路推广至随机引力波背景的统计检验，以探索高斯统计之外的可观测信号。作者希望借助脉冲星计时阵列检验引力波背景中微扰的非高斯偏离。

Method: 采用二次型拟设描述对高斯统计的偏离：三点相关出现在非线性耦合的一阶，协方差修正出现在二阶。先考察该拟设在引力波螺旋振幅（helicity amplitudes）上的表现，再构建对探测器投影振幅的极化标架不变（polarization-frame-invariant）现象学模型，并在简化响应下计算单脉冲星计时残差的三阶矩。

Result: 直接由螺旋振幅拟设得到的计时残差双谱在张量与矢量模的局域极化基旋转下并不显式不变。对极化标架不变的现象学模型，数值基准显示其给出的、以高斯均方根（Gaussian rms）归一化的偏度（skewness）非常小。

Conclusion: 对引力波螺旋振幅的直接二次型拟设存在极化基变换下的不变性问题，因此需要采用极化标架不变的现象学建模。基于简化响应的估计表明，该非高斯效应对应的归一化偏度极小，意味着在脉冲星计时阵列中探测此类弱非高斯性可能具有较大挑战。

Abstract: Motivated by searches for weak primordial non-Gaussianity in the cosmic microwave background, we investigate perturbative departures from Gaussian stochastic gravitational wave background statistics using pulsar timing arrays. We consider a quadratic ansatz that shows three-point correlations at first order in the nonlinear coupling and covariance corrections at second order. We first examine the ansatz for gravitational wave helicity amplitudes. However, the timing residual bispectrum that this produces is not manifestly invariant under local polarization basis rotations for tensor and vector modes. Then, we formulate a polarization-frame-invariant phenomenological model for detector-projected amplitudes and evaluate its one-pulsar timing residual third moment with a simplified response. Our numerical benchmarks have shown that this gives a very small Gaussian-rms-normalized skewness.

</details>


### [101] [Uniform Strong-Deflection Lensing in Near-Extremal Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02900)
*Shakibul Chowdhury*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文构建了次极端Kerr黑洞（subextremal Kerr black hole）顺行（prograde）赤道零测地线在近临界与近极端联合极限下的统一渐近描述，通过将径向势约化为二次正规形（quadratic normal form）得到解析交叉函数，连接次极端的对数发散与极端情形的幂律行为，并经高精度数值积分验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次极端Kerr黑洞的强偏折透镜（strong-deflection lensing）呈现对数临界发散，而严格极端（exactly extremal）顺行情形表现为更强的幂律行为，二者之间的过渡缺乏统一描述。作者旨在构建能够覆盖这两种区域之间转换的一致渐近框架。

Method: 针对顺行赤道零测地线，识别出当碰撞参数偏离精确临界值达到 \(M\kappa\) 量级（\(\kappa=\sqrt{1-a^2/M^2}\)）时的联合近临界—近极端极限，将Kerr径向势（radial potential）约化为二次正规形，得到解析交叉函数，并推导匹配的次领头阶（next-to-leading-order）修正。

Result: 所得渐近描述经精确Kerr测地线方程（geodesic equations）的高精度积分验证，在全部55条测试轨迹上均有改进。同一渐近结构给出高阶像分支规律（image-branch law），在代数累积与指数累积之间插值，交叉阶数按 \(\kappa^{-1}\) 缩放；并推导并数值验证了相应的像分支坐标时间延迟规律（coordinate-time delay law），其高阶类极端极限趋近于视界周期间距 \(4\pi M\)。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了从次极端到极端Kerr黑洞顺行强偏折的统一解析过渡描述，涵盖偏折角度与像分支累积、时间延迟等可观测量，并经数值验证，为近极端黑洞透镜现象的建模提供了可用的渐近工具。

Abstract: Strong-deflection lensing by a subextremal Kerr black hole exhibits a logarithmic critical divergence, whereas the exactly extremal prograde problem develops stronger power-law behavior. We construct a uniform asymptotic description of the transition between these regimes for prograde equatorial null geodesics. The distinguished joint near-critical and near-extremal limit occurs when the impact-parameter offset from the exact critical value is of order \(Mκ\), where \(κ=\sqrt{1-a^2/M^2}\). In this limit, the Kerr radial potential reduces to a quadratic normal form, yielding an analytic crossover function that connects the subextremal logarithmic and extremal power-law regimes. We derive a matched next-to-leading-order correction and validate it against high-precision integrations of the exact Kerr geodesic equations, finding improvement for all 55 trajectories tested. The same asymptotic structure yields a higher-order image-branch law interpolating between algebraic and exponential accumulation, with crossover order scaling as \(κ^{-1}\). We also derive and numerically validate the corresponding image-branch coordinate-time delay law, whose high-order extremal-like limit approaches the horizon-period spacing \(4πM\).

</details>


### [102] [Semiclassical multi-mode squeezed states in harmonic cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03177)
*Bekir Baytaş*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在调和宇宙学（harmonic cosmology）的多模玻色子实现中构造多模压缩态（multimode squeezed states），发现当独立模式数足够大时，即使无位移，体积算符的相对不确定度也可任意小，表明多模压缩真空可呈现半经典行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和宇宙学基于 $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$ 李代数（Lie algebra），是圈量子宇宙学（loop quantum cosmology）与群场论（group field theory）有效动力学的代数框架。单模压缩真空（single-mode squeezed vacua）始终呈现大量子涨落，因此需要考察多模情形能否改善半经典性。

Method: 聚焦 $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$ 的多模玻色子实现（multimode bosonic realization），构造调和宇宙学中的多模压缩态，并在运动学层面（kinematical level）计算体积算符（volume operator）的相对不确定度以分析其半经典性质。

Result: 与单模压缩真空恒有大量子涨落不同，当模式数较大时，即使不施加位移（displacement），体积的相对不确定度也可被任意减小。

Conclusion: 结果表明，包含大量独立模式（independent modes）的运动学压缩真空态（kinematical squeezed vacuum state）可表现出半经典行为，为反弹宇宙学（bouncing cosmologies）的量子本质及经典宇宙的出现提供了启示。

Abstract: Harmonic cosmology is an algebraic framework based on the $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$ Lie algebra that underlies the effective dynamics of loop quantum cosmology and group field theory. Focusing on the multimode bosonic realization of $\mathfrak{su}(1,1)$, we construct multimode squeezed states in harmonic cosmology and analyze their semiclassical properties by computing the relative uncertainties of the volume operator at the kinematical level. In contrast to single-mode squeezed vacua, which invariably exhibit large quantum fluctuations, we find that the relative uncertainty of the volume can be made arbitrarily small when the number of modes is large, even without displacement. Our results demonstrate that a kinematical squeezed vacuum state with a large number of independent modes can behave semiclassically providing insights into the quantum nature of bouncing cosmologies and the emergence of a classical universe.

</details>


### [103] [Sampling biases in sprinkled causal sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03247)
*Marián Boguñá,Dmitri Krioukov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 泊松撒点（Poisson sprinkling）虽能洛伦兹不变地由连续时空生成因果集（causal sets），但数值研究必然限制于有限区域。该有限体积限制对涉及因果链接（links）的可观测量产生一种偏差，简单增加撒点数无法消除。其根源是几何性的：闵可夫斯基时空中固定固有时处的候选链接填充于非紧双曲快度空间（non-compact hyperbolic space of rapidities），在 $d+1$ 维（$d\geq 2$）中该空间为边界主导（boundary dominated）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果集数值模拟必须截断至有限体积，而以链接为基的可观测量受截断影响，其偏差不随撒点密度增加而消失，亟需厘清该偏差的几何起源与正确的极限定义。

Method: 将问题归因于快度双曲空间的边界主导特性，联系非 amenable 空间上朴素开边界热力学极限（thermodynamic limit）的失效；显式推导链接固有时分布对有限截断的依赖，区分良定义的链接强度剖面（link-intensity profile）与良态不清的“均匀选取链接”概念；并通过 $d+1$ 维（$d=1,2,3$）的独立泊松撒点模拟加以验证；进而提出以事件为中心的产品窗估计量（event-centered product-window estimator）及紧双曲商空间（compact hyperbolic quotients）方案。

Result: 不同的有限体积正则化（regularization）可产生不同的极限归一化统计量；论文显式给出链接固有时分布随有限截断的依赖关系，并在 $d=1,2,3$ 的数值模拟中验证了上述结果。所提出的以事件为中心的产品窗估计量具有可控的双标度极限（double-scaling limit）。

Conclusion: 链接固有时统计的有限体积偏差本质上是几何与拓扑问题，而非单纯的有限尺寸效应；应在以事件为中心的双标度极限下定义可观测统计量，并可将无边界紧双曲商空间视为替代正则化方案。

Abstract: Poisson sprinkling provides a Lorentz-invariant way of generating causal sets from a continuum spacetime, but numerical work necessarily restricts the sprinkling to a finite region. For observables involving causal-set links, this finite-volume restriction can generate a bias that does not disappear by simply increasing the number of sprinkled points. The origin of the problem is geometric. At fixed proper time from an event in Minkowski spacetime, candidate links populate a non-compact hyperbolic space of rapidities. In dimensions $d+1$ with $d\geq 2$, this space is boundary dominated: the fraction of volume close to the boundary of a large region does not vanish in the infinite-volume limit. Different finite-volume regularizations can therefore produce different limiting normalized statistics. We relate this phenomenon to the failure of naive open-boundary thermodynamic limits on non-amenable spaces, derive explicitly the dependence of the link proper-time distribution on the finite cutoff, and distinguish a well-defined link-intensity profile from the ill-defined notion of a uniformly chosen link. We verify these results with independent Poisson-sprinkling simulations in $d+1$ dimensions with $d=1,2,3$. We then propose an event-centered product-window estimator with a controlled double-scaling limit, and discuss compact hyperbolic quotients as a possible boundary-free alternative.

</details>


### [104] [Unimodular boundary time for Regge calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03479)
*Steffen Gielen,Sofie Ried*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将 Henneaux-Teitelboim 引力（Henneaux-Teitelboim gravity）用欧几里得 Regge 演算（Euclidean Regge calculus）进行离散化，并在 Regge 宇宙学（Regge cosmology）的对称约化模型中验证该框架，表明连续极限可自然地用单模时间（unimodular time）而非固有时（proper time）定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Henneaux-Teitelboim 引力是单模引力（unimodular gravity）的微分同胚不变表述，局域等价于广义相对论（general relativity），但包含额外的边界变量——单模时间，其在不同超曲面间的差值度量所包围的四维体积。单模版本的量子引力通常预期会偏离基于广义相对论的理论，然而 Henneaux-Teitelboim 引力的离散路径积分（discrete path-integral）表述尚未建立。

Method: 采用欧几里得 Regge 演算方法对 Henneaux-Teitelboim 引力进行离散化，并在 Regge 宇宙学的对称约化模型中对所提框架进行检验。

Result: 在对称约化模型中重现了既有文献的结果，并证明连续极限（continuum limit）可自然地使用单模时间而非固有时来定义。由于单模时间作为边界数据出现、而非体（bulk）内的自由度，该方法允许将一般单纯三角剖分（simplicial triangulations）而非仅高度对称的离散化与连续理论进行比较。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Henneaux-Teitelboim 引力的离散路径积分表述迈出第一步，并为量子引力中时间作为边界变量（boundary variable）提供了新视角。

Abstract: Henneaux-Teitelboim gravity is a diffeomorphism-invariant formulation of unimodular gravity that is locally equivalent to general relativity but includes an extra boundary variable, unimodular time, whose difference between hypersurfaces measures the enclosed 4-volume. In general, unimodular versions of quantum gravity can be expected to deviate from theories based on general relativity, but a discrete path-integral formulation for Henneaux-Teitelboim gravity has not yet been established. In this paper, we discretise Henneaux-Teitelboim gravity using the methods of Euclidean Regge calculus. Testing this framework in a symmetry-reduced model of Regge cosmology, we reproduce results in previous literature and demonstrate that the continuum limit can be naturally defined using unimodular time rather than proper time. Because unimodular time appears as boundary data as opposed to being a degree of freedom in the bulk, this approach allows for the comparison of general simplicial triangulations, beyond highly symmetric discretisations, to the continuum. Our work provides a first step towards a discrete path-integral formulation of Henneaux-Teitelboim gravity and adds a new perspective on time as a boundary variable in quantum gravity.

</details>


### [105] [Born rule in Schroedinger-Newton scenarios of semiclassical gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03501)
*Tanguy Fontana,Axelle Coulon,Giovanni Manfredi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在半经典引力（semiclassical gravity）的薛定谔-牛顿（Schrödinger-Newton）方程框架下，研究量子动力学向玻恩定则（Born rule）的弛豫过程，发现自引力的膨胀行为可决定弛豫是否发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究半经典自引力（self-gravity）是否能驱动系统弛豫至量子平衡（quantum equilibrium），以回应 Underwood 与 Valentini 关于原初量子非平衡（primordial quantum non-equilibrium）的提议。

Method: 采用自引力波包的高斯变分解（Gaussian variational solution），并结合量子动力学的纳尔逊随机表述（Nelson's stochastic representation），追踪初始概率分布偏离 $|\Psi|^2$ 的粒子系综演化。

Result: 当波包保持束缚或随时间缓慢增长时，系统弛豫至玻恩定则；而对于薛定谔-牛顿方程中快速膨胀的解，偏离玻恩定则的状态可无限持续。数值模拟证实了该解析图像。

Conclusion: 半经典自引力既可促进也可抑制向玻恩定则的弛豫，为原初量子非平衡提议提供了一个最小模型。

Abstract: We investigate dynamical relaxation to the Born rule in a semiclassical gravity model described by the Schrödinger-Newton equations. Using a Gaussian variational solution for the self-gravitating wave packet, combined with Nelson's stochastic representation of quantum dynamics, we track the evolution of particle ensembles whose initial probability distribution differs from $|Ψ|^2$. Relaxation to the Born rule ("quantum equilibrium") occurs when the wave packet remains bounded, or grows sufficiently slowly in time; in contrast, for rapidly expanding solutions of the Schrödinger-Newton equations, deviations from the Born rule can persist indefinitely. Numerical simulations confirm this analytical picture. These results show that semiclassical self-gravity can either enable or inhibit relaxation towards the Born rule, providing a minimal model relevant to proposals of primordial quantum non-equilibrium, as suggested by Underwood and Valentini [Phys. Rev. D 92, 063531 (2015)].

</details>


### [106] [Parity Isospectrality of the Kerr--Newman--de Sitter Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03701)
*Colin Weller,Dongjun Li,Pratik Wagle,Yanbei Chen,Nicolas Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究将真空 Kerr 微扰中偶宇称与奇宇称准正频率的宇称等谱性（parity isospectrality），推广至亚极值 Kerr–Newman–de Sitter 黑洞的线性引力电磁微扰，且不依赖慢旋转或小电荷展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知真空 Kerr 微扰具有奇偶宇称准正频率相同的等谱性，但该性质在含电荷与宇宙学常数的黑洞时空中的适用范围尚需澄清。本文旨在检验并确立此性质在更一般背景下的成立性。

Method: 针对亚极值 Kerr–Newman–de Sitter 黑洞（subextremal Kerr–Newman–de Sitter black holes）的线性引力电磁微扰（gravitoelectromagnetic perturbations），在辐射规范（radiation gauge）下由规范不变的主变量（gauge-invariant master variables）直接重构辐射度规与规范势微扰，其间不引入中间复势（complex potential）；分析不采用慢旋转或小电荷展开。

Result: 确立了该背景下偶宇称与奇宇称微扰的宇称等谱性（parity isospectrality）成立，并实现了微扰量的直接重构。

Conclusion: 宇称等谱性并非仅局限于真空 Kerr 情形，而可延伸至含电荷与正宇宙学常数的旋转黑洞的引力电磁微扰，且无需微扰式近似假设。

Abstract: Vacuum Kerr perturbations in general relativity have identical even- and odd-parity quasinormal frequencies. We establish this parity isospectrality for linear gravitoelectromagnetic perturbations of subextremal Kerr--Newman--de Sitter black holes, without a slow-rotation or small-charge expansion. We reconstruct the radiative metric and gauge-potential perturbations directly from gauge-invariant master variables in radiation gauge, without an intermediate complex potential.

</details>

### [107] [Cyclic $L_3$ Models and Holomorphic Reduction in Heterotic $G_2$ Deformation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03301)
*Bram Brongers*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文构造了异质（heterotic）$G_2$ 超势变分形变理论在无挠标准嵌入附近、$α'$ 一阶下的有限循环 $L_3$ 表示，并在乘积流形 $Y=S^1\times X$ 上将其约化为全纯理论，证明后者可由哈密顿约化（Hamiltonian reduction）得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在异质 $G_2$ 紧化背景下建立变分形变理论的代数表述，将物理泛函编码为有限循环 $L_\infty$ 代数（$L_3$ 代数），以获得对偶与约化结构。摘要未提及超出此构造范围的其他动机。

Method: 引入逆余标架、旋量导数、通量与诱导切联络的辅助场（auxiliary fields），构造次数不超过四的移位余切哈密顿量；消去辅助场后回到物理泛函。随后在 $Y=S^1\times X$（$X$ 为 Calabi–Yau 三维流形）上限制到具有水平规范与 gerbe 数据的乘积轨迹，借助诱导 $SU(3)$ 结构选取 Hermitian 切 Courant 子代数胚（Courant subalgebroid），并用其复化中的横截迷向提升（transverse isotropic lifts）编码全纯联络与弦方程。

Result: 在平坦七维环面上，该泛函在局部物理旋量坐标下具有非零五次泰勒系数。乘积情形下泛函约化为 $\frac L4\operatorname{Im}\int_X(H+i\,dω)\wedgeΩ$，其中 $L$ 为圆周长，$Ω=e^{-2Φ}Ψ$ 为加权复体积。其泰勒括积构成 $L_3$ 代数，其中弦部分与已知的全纯异质代数一致。全纯理论可由乘积变分理论的有限可缩稳定化（contractible stabilization）经特征哈密顿约化在每个上链次数上得到。

Conclusion: 约化商虽能捕获全纯方程，但共形平衡（conformal balance）、规范本原性（gauge primitivity）与领头通量容许性（leading-flux admissibility）需作为实代表上的额外条件。摘要未给出这些条件的进一步物理解释或验证结果。

Abstract: We construct a finite cyclic $L_3$ presentation (that is, a cyclic $L_\infty$ algebra with brackets vanishing above arity three) of the variational deformation theory defined by the heterotic $G_2$ superpotential near a torsion-free standard embedding, to first order in $α'$. Auxiliary fields for the inverse coframe, spinor derivative, flux and induced tangent connection give a shifted-cotangent Hamiltonian of degree at most four. Eliminating the auxiliary fields recovers the physical functional. On the flat seven-torus, this functional has a nonzero quintic Taylor coefficient in local physical spinor coordinates.
  For $Y=S^1\times X$, with $X$ a Calabi--Yau threefold, we restrict to the product locus with horizontal gauge and gerbe data. The functional reduces to $
  \frac L4\operatorname{Im}\int_X(H+i\,dω)\wedgeΩ, $
where $L$ is the circle length and $Ω=e^{-2Φ}Ψ$ is the weighted complex volume. The induced $SU(3)$ structure selects a Hermitian tangent Courant subalgebroid. Transverse isotropic lifts in its complexification encode the holomorphic connection and string equations, together with the induced tangent connection, weighted canonical line and gerbe hierarchy. Their Taylor brackets form an $L_3$ algebra whose string part agrees with the known holomorphic heterotic algebra.
  We then show that this holomorphic theory is obtained by characteristic Hamiltonian reduction of a finite contractible stabilization of the product variational theory, in every cochain degree. While the quotient captures the holomorphic equations, conformal balance, gauge primitivity and leading-flux admissibility are additional conditions on a real representative.

</details>


<div id='math.DS'></div>

# math.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Asymptotic approximation of sensitivities in finite dimensional continuous data assimilation with application to parameter estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02686)
*Joshua Newey,Jared P. Whitehead*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文为一种将连续数据同化（continuous data assimilation）与通用优化（generic optimization）耦合的参数估计算法提供严格理论依据，并在有限维系统下证明其梯度下降收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在为耦合连续数据同化与通用优化的参数估计方法建立严格数学基础，弥补此类算法缺乏理论保证的问题。

Method: 针对有限维系统（finite dimensional systems），对底层建模动力系统的灵敏度渐近近似（asymptotic approximation of the sensitivity）给出严格论证；证明 L² 损失函数满足近似的 Polyak-Lojasiewicz 不等式，并据此论证所提算法梯度下降（gradient descent）的收敛性。

Result: 理论部分给出了灵敏度渐近近似的严格依据、损失函数的近似 Polyak-Lojasiewicz 不等式以及梯度下降收敛性的证明；数值实验表明严格理论结果具有较高精度。

Conclusion: 该框架为耦合连续数据同化与优化的参数估计算法提供了严格的理论支撑，且数值算例验证了理论结果的精确性。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous justification for a parameter estimation algorithm which couples continuous data assimilation to generic optimization. For finite dimensional systems, we provide a rigorous justification of an asymptotic approximation of the sensitivity for the underlying modeled dynamical system, prove that the $L^2$ loss function satisfies an approximate Polyak-Lojasiewicz inequality, and use that result to justify convergence of gradient descent for the proposed algorithm. Numerical examples are provided that demonstrate the precision of the rigorous results.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Testing internal dynamics in neutron stars with radio pulsar timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02291)
*Reginald Christian Bernardo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过比较射电发射壳层与内部超流体的两类模型，检验脉冲星角速度涨落的平稳性，并给出三颗孤立脉冲星的推断结果。PSR J1359-6038 数据中度支持独立力矩（odds ratio 700:1），PSR B1937+21 无明显偏好，Vela 在 glitch 前区间受额外散布与未解释残差结构限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星自转变化可源于内部等大反向力矩导致的自旋噪声（stationary spin noise），或源于独立力矩导致总角动量的扩散变化（nonstationary spin noise）。区分这两种机制有助于理解脉冲星内部角动量交换过程，因此需要检验角速度涨落的平稳性。

Method: 构建并存两类模型：一类为射电发射壳层（crust）与内部超流体（interior superfluid）之间的等大反向力矩，另一类为独立力矩；并利用观测到的脉冲星相位（pulsar phases）检验角速度涨落的平稳性。

Result: 对三颗具有可测计时噪声（timing noise）的孤立脉冲星给出不同推断：PSR J1359-6038 数据中度偏好独立力矩，odds ratio 为 700:1；PSR B1937+21 在两模型间无明确偏好；Vela 在 glitch 前区间，额外散布（excess scatter）与未解释的残差结构限制了结果解释。

Conclusion: 基于脉冲星相位的平稳性检验可区分等大反向力矩与独立力矩两类机制，但推断结果因脉冲星而异，且部分情形（如 Vela 前 glitch 区间）受数据质量与残差结构制约。

Abstract: Rotational variations in pulsars reflect either internal angular momentum exchange via equal and opposite torques (stationary spin noise) or independent torques causing diffusive changes in total angular momentum (nonstationary spin noise). We compare two models of a radio-emitting crust and interior superfluid to test the stationarity of angular velocity fluctuations through observed pulsar phases. We present results for three isolated pulsars with measurable timing noise, demonstrating distinct inference outcomes. For PSR J1359-6038, the data mildly favor independent torques over equal-and-opposite torques, with an odds ratio of seven hundred to one. For PSR B1937+21, the analysis shows no clear preference between the models. For Vela in a pre-glitch interval, excess scatter and unexplained residual structure limit the interpretation.

</details>


### [110] [Forward Bayesian Inference for the Binary Black Hole Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03459)
*Poulami Dutta Roy,Tanmaya Mishra,Achal Kumar,Sergey Klimenko*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: PopCWB 是一个基于相干波 burst（coherent WaveBurst, cWB）非模板搜索管线的贝叶斯（Bayesian）双黑洞（binary black hole, BBH）族群推断框架，采用前向（forward）策略，利用机器学习回归估计的模拟事件总质量构建似然，并在 O3 数据上约束了含脉动对不稳定性超新星与动力学并合效应的族群模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准族群分析依赖已探测事件的单事件后验样本，采用逆向（inverse）推断，受事件级后验与阈值选择影响。PopCWB 旨在为基于非模板搜索（unmodeled search）的 cWB 管线提供直接的前向族群推断途径。

Method: PopCWB 通过变动族群模型以寻找最能描述观测事件的最优模型，实现前向族群推断；不使用事件级后验样本，而是采用 cWB 重建的大量模拟事件及其经机器学习回归（machine-learning regression）估计的总质量（total mass）构建边缘概率分布（marginal probability distributions），并据此构造似然（likelihood）。

Result: 将该框架应用于 LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA 第三次观测运行（third observing run, O3）期间 cWB 探测到的 BBH 事件，对包含脉动对不稳定性超新星（pulsational pair-instability supernovae）与动力学并合（dynamical mergers）效应的天体物理动机族群模型给出约束：恒星演化预言的最大黑洞质量为 45.5^{+5.3}_{-6.5} M_⊙，本地 BBH 并合率为 23.1^{+11.3}_{-8.4} Gpc^{-3} yr^{-1}。

Conclusion: PopCWB 为基于 cWB 的 BBH 族群推断提供了前向替代方案，所得最大黑洞质量与本地并合率结果与文献中已有族群模型进行了比较。

Abstract: We present PopCWB, a Bayesian framework for the inference of the binary black hole (BBH) population based on the unmodeled search pipeline coherent WaveBurst (cWB). The standard population analysis takes an inverse approach by inferring the underlying population model parameters for a given set of detected events using the individual posterior samples. In PopCWB, we take a forward approach to population inference by varying population models to identify the most optimal one which describes the observed events. Rather than using event-level posterior samples, PopCWB uses a large set of simulated events reconstructed by cWB and their total mass, estimated with a machine-learning regression, to construct marginal probability distributions. These marginal probabilities are then used in the construction of the likelihood. We apply PopCWB to BBH events detected by cWB during the third observing run (O3) of the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA detectors. We constrain an astrophysically motivated BBH population model that incorporates the effects of pulsational pair-instability supernovae and dynamical mergers. The analysis predicts a maximum black hole mass of $\mathbf{45.5^{+5.3}_{-6.5} M_{\odot}}$ from stellar evolution and an inferred local BBH merger rate of $\mathbf{23.1^{+11.3}_{-8.4} \, Gpc^{-3} yr^{-1}}$. We compare these results with the existing population models from the literature.

</details>

### [111] [Projective symmetry group classification of fermionic $Z_2$ spin liquids on the dipolar-octupolar pyrochlore magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02409)
*Rohit Deb,Sambuddha Sanyal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在偶极-八极（DO）烧绿石晶格上构造并分类了费米子部分子（fermionic parton）的 Z2 量子自旋液体（QSL），指出其投影对称群（PSG）给出与通用费米子分类相同的 48 个对称 Z2 类，但 DO 对称作用选取出物理上不同的平均场通道与拟设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DO 赝自旋具有与有效自旋-1/2 Kramers 二重态相同的空间群与时间反演代数，已有玻色子 DO 分类与 U(1) 费米子分类，但 Z2 拓扑序在费米子与玻色子自旋子中同样自然实现，费米子部分子在 DO 烧绿石中的 Z2 分类尚待扩展。

Method: 将各向异性 XYZ 交换模型分解为所有对称的单态与三重态跳跃及配对通道，在自洽平均场（mean-field）框架下最小化能量；利用由子晶格无关算符编码的 DO 对称作用选取对称允许通道，并在（Jx~, Jz~）平面计算相图与自旋子谱。

Result: 相图中仅少数 Z2 态被稳定，其中若干支持无能隙或平带自旋子谱；计算了这些态的自旋子能带结构、低温比热与动力学自旋结构因子，并论证幂律比热指数可作为自旋子节点结构的诊断量。

Conclusion: 近线性温标比热与结构因子中的宽连续谱为 Nd2ScNbO7 等 DO 材料所报道的无能隙分数化热力学提供了费米子部分子解释，补充了 Ce 基化合物的玻色子量子自旋冰图像；该工作将 DO 烧绿石 QSL 的费米子部分子分类拓展至 U(1) 扇区之外。

Abstract: We construct and classify $Z_2$ quantum spin liquids (QSLs) of fermionic partons on the dipolar-octupolar (DO) pyrochlore lattice. The DO pseudospin shares the space-group and time-reversal algebra of the effective spin-1/2 Kramers doublet, its projective symmetry group (PSG) yields the same 48 symmetric $Z_2$ classes as the generic fermionic classification. We show, the distinct DO symmetry action, encoded in a set of sublattice-independent operators, selects a physically different set of symmetry-allowed mean-field channels, so that the resulting ansatze, spinon spectra, and observables differ from both the effective spin-1/2 case and the bosonic DO classification. Decomposing the anisotropic XYZ exchange model into all symmetric singlet and triplet hopping and pairing channels and minimizing the mean-field energy self-consistently, we obtain phase diagrams in the ($\tilde{J_x}, \tilde{J_z}$) plane in which only a few $Z_2$ states, several supporting gapless or flat-band spinon spectra, are stabilized. For these states we compute the spinon band structures, the low-temperature specific heat, and the dynamical spin structure factor, and we argue that the power-law specific-heat exponent is a diagnostic of the spinon nodal structure. A near-linear-in-temperature specific heat together with broad continua in the structure factor gives a fermionic-parton account of the gapless, fractionalized thermodynamics reported in DO materials such as $Nd_2ScNbO_7$, complementing the bosonic quantum-spin-ice picture of the Ce-based compounds. The work extends the fermionic-parton classification of DO pyrochlore QSLs beyond the $U(1)$ sector, where $Z_2$ topological order is realized equally naturally by fermionic and bosonic spinons.

</details>


### [112] [Fractionalization-Induced Time-Reversal Symmetry Breaking at Continuous Superconducting Transitions in Low-Symmetry Kondo Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02635)
*Wonjune Choi,Shi-Zeng Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出，在具有 $\mathbb Z_2$ 自旋液体（spin liquid）的近藤晶格（Kondo lattice）中，对称性分数化（symmetry fractionalization）可为超导与额外对称性破缺的同步连续出现提供新途径，且无需多维序参量或精细调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统朗道理论（Landau theory）中，超导与额外对称性破缺的同时连续出现通常需要多维序参量或精细调节（fine tuning）。作者意在寻找一条不依赖这些条件的替代机制。

Method: 作者分析 $\mathbb Z_2$ 自旋液体中近藤场（Kondo fields）继承自旋子（spinons）的投影对称作用（projective symmetry action）。当这些场上的投影时间反演作用（projective time-reversal action）平方为 $-1$ 时，投影对称代数（projective symmetry algebra）禁止保持时间反演对称的凝聚，并强制临界模式（critical modes）二重简并。并以各向异性近藤-Kitaev 模型（anisotropic Kondo-Kitaev model）进行演示。

Result: 在 $\mathbb Z_2$ 自旋液体中，近藤凝聚（Kondo condensation）同时破坏电磁 $U(1)$ 对称性，因此超导与时间反演对称破缺可在单一连续相变中同时出现，即使晶体对称性仅允许一维不可约表示（one-dimensional irreducible representations）。在各向异性近藤-Kitaev 模型中，超导与时间反演及镜面对称（mirror symmetry）破缺同时涌现。

Conclusion: 对称性分数化为超导与附加对称性破缺的同步连续出现提供了一条不同于传统朗道理论的路径，其关键在于投影对称代数对序参量凝聚的约束，而非多维序参量或精细调节。

Abstract: In conventional Landau theory, simultaneous continuous onset of superconductivity and the breaking of additional symmetries generally requires a multidimensional order parameter or fine tuning. We show that symmetry fractionalization provides a different route. In a Kondo lattice with a $\mathbb Z_2$ spin liquid, the Kondo fields inherit the projective symmetry action of the spinons. When the projective time-reversal action on these fields squares to $-1$, the projective symmetry algebra forbids a condensate that preserves time-reversal symmetry and enforces twofold degeneracy of the critical modes. Because Kondo condensation in a $\mathbb Z_2$ spin liquid also breaks electromagnetic $U(1)$ symmetry, superconductivity and time-reversal symmetry breaking can emerge at a single continuous transition, even if crystal symmetry admits only one-dimensional irreducible representations. We demonstrate this mechanism in an anisotropic Kondo-Kitaev model, where superconductivity and the breaking of time-reversal and mirror symmetries emerge simultaneously.

</details>


### [113] [Correlated Metals, Metamagnetism, and Orbital Nematic Order in moiré Materials with Neural Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03145)
*Wei Zhang,Ataç İmamoğlu,Tao Shi,Eugene A. Demler,Ivan Morera*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用基于消息传递神经网络（message-passing neural network）的神经量子态（neural quantum states）与变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo），研究三角周期势下填充因子 ν=2 的二维长程库仑电子体系。结果表明强关联可重塑绝缘态，并在部分相图区域以关联金属相取代能带绝缘体，且相变与自旋极化强烈相关，形成自旋依赖的分子绝缘体与轨道向列分子晶体。采用短程相互作用时相图定性结构不变，显示结果的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相互作用情形下该体系预期形成常规能带绝缘体（band insulator），本文动机在于考察强关联（strong correlations）能否定性改变这一绝缘态，并厘清长程库仑相互作用与自旋极化在金属—绝缘体转变中的作用。

Method: 采用二维连续模型（continuum model），电子经受长程库仑力与三角周期势，填充因子 ν=2；以消息传递神经网络（message-passing neural network）构建神经量子态（neural quantum states），并用变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo）优化，确定基态相图随周期势强度与塞曼场（Zeeman field）的变化；另以短程排斥相互作用作对照。

Result: 强关联在部分相图区域抑制能带绝缘体、形成关联金属相，二者之间为一阶转变（first-order transition）。金属—绝缘体转变强烈依赖自旋极化：未极化体系在更弱周期势下即绝缘，形成 s 波分子绝缘体（s-wave molecular insulator），其中相反自旋电子在势极小值附近配对；完全极化体系则需更深势阱，形成对称破缺的轨道向列分子晶体（symmetry-broken orbital nematic molecular crystal）。该自旋依赖分子转变导致磁化曲线不连续、变磁转变（metamagnetic transitions）及多个多临界点（multicritical points），并在密度分布、实空间关联函数与结构因子中留下特征信号。

Conclusion: 长程库仑相互作用并非决定相图定性结构的关键：改用可在超冷原子（ultracold-atom）平台实现的短程排斥相互作用后，多体相图的定性结构保持不变，表明该结果在不同相互作用模型下具有稳健性。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate a continuum model of two-dimensional electrons interacting via long-range Coulomb forces and subject to a triangular periodic potential at filling factor $ν=2$. Using neural quantum states based on message-passing neural networks optimized with variational Monte Carlo, we determine the ground-state phase diagram as a function of periodic potential strength and Zeeman field. Although the noninteracting system is expected to form a conventional band insulator, we show that strong correlations can qualitatively reshape the insulating state and, in part of the phase diagram, suppress the band insulator in favor of a correlated metallic phase, with a first-order transition separating the two regimes. Moreover, the metal--insulator transition exhibits a strong dependence on spin polarization: the unpolarized system becomes insulating at substantially weaker periodic potentials through the formation of an $s$-wave molecular insulator, where opposite-spin electrons bind near each potential minimum, whereas the fully polarized system requires a significantly deeper potential and instead forms a symmetry-broken orbital nematic molecular crystal. This spin-dependent molecular transition gives rise to discontinuous magnetization curves, metamagnetic transitions, and several multicritical points in the phase diagram, together with clear signatures in the density profiles, real-space correlation functions, and structure factors. To further elucidate the role of the long-range character of the Coulomb interaction, we also study short-range repulsive interactions, which can be realized with ultracold-atom platforms. We find that the qualitative structure of the many-body phase diagram remains unchanged, demonstrating the robustness of our results across different interaction models.

</details>

### [114] [A coupled geometric surface and surface reaction-diffusion framework for modelling nuclear mechanics during confined cell migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02231)
*Francesca Ballatore,Silvia Comunian,Anotida Madzvamuse,Slimane Ait-Si-Ali,Rachele Allena*

Main category: q-bio.CB

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个二维数学模型，将细胞膜与核膜表示为受表面张力、弯曲弹性和外部力学作用支配的演化闭合曲面，并在演化核表面上耦合核周分子调控的反应-扩散系统，用于研究受限细胞迁移中核力学与基因调控的相互作用。模型应用于SETDB1/SUV39H1调控H3K9me3外周沉积的肺癌细胞模型，数值结果定性再现了SETDB1敲除细胞的核力学变化及部分进入时间增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限细胞迁移（confined cell migration）受核与细胞变形支配，而核力学又受染色质组织与压缩状态（受表观遗传修饰调控）影响。为理解分子调控与力学变形之间的耦合机制，需要一个将膜力学与核周反应-扩散调控统一描述的数学框架。

Method: 建立二维数学框架：细胞膜与核膜作为演化闭合曲面（evolving closed surfaces），由包含表面张力（surface tension）、弯曲弹性（bending elasticity）与外部力学相互作用的力平衡（force-balance）定律支配；核力学依赖染色质组织与压缩等受表观遗传调控的因素；核周分子调控用定义在演化核表面上的表面反应-扩散系统（surface reaction-diffusion system）描述；采用演化表面有限元方法（evolving surface finite element method）求解，并应用于SETDB1与SUV39H1平衡调控H3K9me3外周沉积的肺癌细胞模型。

Result: 使用实验测量的力学参数模拟对照（Ctrl）与SETDB1敲除（SETDB1 KO）条件，二者呈现不同的H3K9me3分布，尤以核周区域显著。数值解定性再现了核力学变化以及SETDB1 KO细胞进入时间（entry time）增加的部分现象。

Conclusion: 模型再现了实验观测的方向性趋势，但模拟与实测进入时间之间的残差表明细胞响应是多因素（multifactorial）的，提示除H3K9me3外周分布外还有其他因素参与调控。

Abstract: Confined cell migration is governed by nuclear and cellular deformation. In this work, we formulate a two-dimensional mathematical framework in which the cell and nuclear membranes are represented as evolving closed surfaces governed by force-balance laws incorporating surface tension, bending elasticity, and external mechanical interactions. Nuclear mechanics depends on several factors, including chromatin organization and compaction, which are regulated by epigenetic modifications. Molecular regulation at the nuclear periphery is described by a surface reaction-diffusion system posed on the evolving nuclear surface. We apply this coupled framework to an experimentally characterized lung cancer cell model in which the balance between the enzymes SETDB1 and SUV39H1 regulates the peripheral deposition of H3K9me3. Using experimentally measured mechanical parameters, we represent control (Ctrl) and SETDB1 knockout (SETDB1 KO) conditions, which display distinct H3K9me3 distributions, especially at the nuclear periphery. Numerical solutions obtained using the evolving surface finite element method qualitatively reproduce the changes in nuclear mechanics as well as part of the experimentally observed increase in the entry time in SETDB1 KO cells. The model therefore reproduces the direction of the experimental observations, while the residual difference between simulated and measured entry times indicates that the cellular response is multifactorial.

</details>

### [115] [Maximal graded subalgebras of simple vectorial Lie superalgebras with polynomial coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03340)
*Dimitry Leites,Irina Shchepochkina*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在带有多项式系数的单（或接近单的 Cartan 延拓）向量李超代数（vectorial Lie superalgebras）中，借助与 Weisfeiler 滤过（Weisfeiler filtrations）相关的分级，描述了极大单（及接近单）分级子代数，从而实现了 Sophus Lie 早期工作的超化（superization）。结合 Shchepochkina 关于矩阵李超代数极大子代数的工作，本文解决了 Dynkin 问题（Dynkin's problem）的超对称与无限维版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S. Lie 曾尝试对多项式系数的单向量李代数（simple vectorial Lie algebras）的极大子代数进行分类。附录表明该问题按原始提法是"wild"的：描述所有子代数包含矩阵对在同时共轭下的分类，因此子代数过多而无法完全枚举。为此，多位研究者转而区分在应用中重要的若干极大子代数类。本文动机即在于对相应超对称情形（superization）给出可处理的分类结果。

Method: 研究对象为带多项式系数的单（或接近单的 Cartan 延拓（Cartan prolongations））向量李超代数，并赋予与 Weisfeiler 滤过（Weisfeiler filtrations）相关联的分级（gradings）；在此框架下描述其极大单（及接近单）分级子代数。结合 I. Shchepochkina 对矩阵李超代数（matrix Lie superalgebras）极大子代数的描述结果，共同处理 Dynkin 问题。

Result: 给出（接近）单向量李超代数中极大（接近）单分级子代数的描述；附录说明原始极大子代数分类问题为"wild"（包含矩阵对同时共轭分类）。结合 Shchepochkina 的结果，解决了单有限维李代数极大子代数描述（Dynkin 问题）的超对称与无限维版本，仅余 3 个例外情形（excellent ambients）另文处理。

Conclusion: 本文完成了 Dynkin 问题在超对称与无限维情形下的求解（除 3 个例外背景另处讨论外），构成 Sophus Lie 原始极大子代数分类尝试的超化（superization），并与 Shchepochkina 关于矩阵李超代数的工作相互补。

Abstract: S. Lie was the first to try to classify maximal subalgebras of simple vectorial (i.e., consisting of vector fields) Lie algebras with polynomial coefficients. In Appendix it is shown that, as stated, the problem is wild: describing all subalgebras contains the classification of pairs of matrices up to simultaneous conjugation, so there are too many such subalgebras. Several researchers distinguished various classes of maximal subalgebras important in applications.
  Here, in simple (or in Cartan prolongations close to simple) vectorial Lie superalgebras with polynomial coefficients considered with gradings associated with Weisfeiler filtrations, we describe maximal simple (and close to simple) graded subalgebras --- a superization of what Sophus Lie tackled.
  Together with I. Shchepochkina's description of the maximal subalgebras of matrix Lie superalgebras, see arXiv:hep-th/9702122, this paper solves the super and infinite-dimensional version of Dynkin's problem --- the description of the maximal subalgebras of simple finite-dimensional Lie algebras --- except for the 3 exceptional ambients considered elsewhere.

</details>


### [116] [Maximal subalgebras in the three exceptional simple finite-dimensional Lie superalgebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03354)
*Dimitry Leites,Oleksandr Lozhechnyk*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文补全了有限维单李超代数（simple finite-dimensional Lie superalgebras）极大子代数分类中遗留的三个例外情形：$\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$ 与 $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$，对每个代数的所有适用 $\mathbb{Z}$-分次（grading）逐一列出全部极大分次子代数，并借助判据将多数结论由“极大分次”提升为“极大”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dynkin 曾分类单有限维李代数的极大子代数；Shchepochkina 与 Leites–Shchepochkina 已解决矩阵李超代数及具 Weisfeiler 分次的简单向量李超代数情形，但 $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ 因具有多种向量实现方式而不适用一般论证，构成本问题的遗留缺口。

Method: 对三个李超代数分别枚举方法所适用的 $\mathbb{Z}$-分次（$\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$ 两种、$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$ 五种、$\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ 八种），在每个分次下计算全部极大分次子代数（maximal graded subalgebras），其中半单（semi-simple）者显式给出；全部计算在 $\mathbb{Q}$ 上进行，并以一条简单判据将“极大分次”结论提升为“极大”。

Result: 完整给出三个例外代数的所有分次及其对应的极大分次子代数清单，半单子代数显式列出，多数结论被提升为极大子代数。另得到两项副产品：向量李超代数分次子代数的经典五情形分类在深度大于 1（depth $>1$）的环境下不完整，需补充第六情形，且该情形恰在此处非空；$\mathfrak{ag}(2)$ 与 $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ 内所含 $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$ 副本的参数 $a$ 被内蕴地计算出来。

Conclusion: 三个遗留例外情形在分次子代数层面得到解决，并部分推广至一般极大性子代数；同时修正了分次子代数的情形分类，并给出参数 $a$ 的内蕴确定方式。

Abstract: Dynkin classified the maximal subalgebras of the simple finite-dimensional Lie algebras. The super version of his problem is solved, for the matrix Lie superalgebras, by Shchepochkina, and, for the simple vectorial ones with polynomial coefficients taken with their Weisfeiler gradings, in the companion paper by Leites and Shchepochkina --- with three exceptions left open: the three simple finite-dimensional Lie superalgebras $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$, $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$ and $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$, which are vectorial in more than one way and are not covered by the general argument.
  Here we settle these three cases for \textbf{graded} subalgebras. For each of the three Lie superalgebras, we list all the $\mathbb{Z}$-gradings to which the method of that paper applies --- two for $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$, five for $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$, eight for $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$ --- and describe, for every one of them, all the maximal graded subalgebras, the semi-simple ones explicitly. A simple criterion upgrades most of the answers from ``maximal graded\rq\rq\ to ``maximal\rq\rq. Every answer is computed over $\mathbb{Q}$.
  Two by-products: the classical list of \textbf{five} cases into which the graded subalgebras of a vectorial Lie superalgebra split is incomplete for ambients of depth $>1$ --- a sixth case has to be added, and it is non-empty precisely here; and the parameter $a$ of the copies of $\mathfrak{osp}_a(4|2)$ sitting inside $\mathfrak{ag}(2)$, resp. $\mathfrak{ab}(3)$, is computed intrinsically.

</details>

### [117] [Chaotic Griffiths phase in neuron map networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03344)
*Juan Velez-Rojas,M. G. Cosenza*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 该论文证明，基于映射神经元的全局耦合网络中，局部参数的淬火无序（quenched disorder）可独立于拓扑无序生成混沌 Griffiths 相（chaotic Griffiths phase）；其与小型世界连接（small-world connectivity）的联合作用可显著扩展该临界区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Griffiths 相（Griffiths phases）可在无需精细调节至孤立转变点的前提下，于较宽参数范围内维持临界动力学，但此前主要归因于复杂网络的结构异质性（structural heterogeneity）。本文旨在检验局部参数的内在动力学异质性是否构成一种独立的生成机制。

Method: 采用全局耦合的 Chialvo 映射（Chialvo maps）神经元网络，在局部神经元参数中引入淬火无序，从而将内在参数无序与拓扑无序分离；改变耦合强度以观察动力学转变，并进一步引入小型世界连接（small-world connectivity）以考察拓扑无序的叠加效应。

Result: 在去同步与相干混沌动力学之间出现混沌 Griffiths 相，其特征为神经元簇（neuronal clusters）的间歇性形成与破碎，以及成簇神经元比例的大幅涨落；簇尺寸呈幂律分布（power-law distributions），指数依赖耦合强度，表明在有限参数区间内存在临界行为。该相在均匀极限下不存在，并随参数异质性程度增加而逐步展宽。全局耦合与小型世界网络中，正 Lyapunov 指数（positive Lyapunov exponents）数目均随系统尺寸呈现反常标度（anomalous scaling）。

Conclusion: 内在动力学异质性（intrinsic dynamical heterogeneity）是生成混沌 Griffiths 相的独立机制，且其与结构无序的相互作用可增强神经元网络中的延展临界性（extended criticality）。

Abstract: Griffiths phases provide a mechanism for sustaining critical dynamics over extended parameter regions without fine-tuning to an isolated transition point. This behavior has been mainly associated with structural heterogeneity in complex networks. Here we show that quenched heterogeneity in local parameters can generate a chaotic Griffiths phase in a network of map-based neurons. We study globally coupled Chialvo maps with quenched disorder in a local neuronal parameter, thereby isolating intrinsic parameter disorder from topological disorder. As the coupling strength is varied, a chaotic Griffiths phase emerges between desynchronized and coherent chaotic dynamics, characterized by intermittent formation and breakup of neuronal clusters and large fluctuations in the fraction of clustered neurons. Within this regime, cluster sizes exhibit power-law distributions with coupling-dependent exponents, providing evidence of critical behavior over a finite parameter interval. The chaotic Griffiths phase is absent in the homogeneous limit and broadens progressively as the degree of parameter heterogeneity increases. We further introduce topological disorder through small-world connectivity and find that its combined action with local parameter heterogeneity substantially extends the chaotic Griffiths phase. In both globally coupled and small-world networks, the number of positive Lyapunov exponents displays anomalous scaling with system size. These results demonstrate that intrinsic dynamical heterogeneity constitutes an independent mechanism for generating chaotic Griffiths phases and that its interplay with structural disorder enhances extended criticality in neuronal networks.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Inferring constitutive forces from noisy Allen-Cahn movies with moment equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02672)
*Soobeen Jung,Hyunju Kim*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文针对从图像推断 Allen-Cahn 本构力（constitutive force）时非线性观测噪声与共享噪声带来的偏差问题，提出观测感知、噪声校正的矩方程（moment equations）及表面张力校准本构变换器（SCCT），实现由平面影像推断可预测未见演化的力场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依靠精确优化器不足以从图像推断 Allen-Cahn 本构力：非线性观测噪声（nonlinear observation noise）会改变力特征均值，共享噪声使测量仪器与响应耦合，且借用其他求解器的时间平衡（temporal balance）即便在干净数据上仍可能有偏。因此需在反演前处理这些估计偏差。

Method: 提出观测感知、噪声校正的矩方程，在反演前消除上述效应；构建表面张力校准本构变换器（surface-tension-calibrated constitutive transformer, SCCT），将所得矩与正 Bernstein 逆（positive Bernstein inverse）耦合，并可依据图像与矩自适应调整先验与精度。理论上给出条件稳定性界（conditional stability bound），将矩残差与先验误差分离。

Result: 合成实验表明，校正估计方程（estimating equation）比提升正则化器灵活性更重要；学习式自适应的收益较小且依赖训练对比。由平面影片推断的力可预测未见的平面与三维演化。环形笼破裂（ring-cage breakup）案例显示，即使拓扑预测一致，扩散界面（diffuse-interface）精度仍能区分不同力，而外部表面张力单独设定能量尺度。

Conclusion: 从图像反演本构力的关键在于对估计方程进行噪声与观测偏差校正，而非单纯增强优化或正则化灵活性；SCCT 结合校正矩方程可实现跨维度可预测推断，并揭示扩散界面精度与表面张力在区分力场与设定能量尺度中的不同作用。

Abstract: Inferring an Allen-Cahn constitutive force from images requires more than an accurate optimizer: nonlinear observation noise alters the mean of force features, shared noise couples instruments to the response, and a temporal balance borrowed from another solver can remain biased even on clean data. We develop observation-aware, noise-adjusted moment equations that address these effects before inversion. The surface-tension-calibrated constitutive transformer (SCCT) couples the resulting moments to a positive Bernstein inverse and can adapt its prior and precision from images and moments. A conditional stability bound separates moment residuals from prior error. Synthetic tests show that correcting the estimating equation matters more than increasing regularizer flexibility; the benefit of learned adaptation is smaller and depends on the training comparison. Forces inferred from planar movies predict unseen planar and three-dimensional evolutions. A ring-cage breakup shows why diffuse-interface accuracy distinguishes forces even when their topological predictions agree, while external surface tension sets the energy scale separately.

</details>


### [119] [A Polynomial-Scaling PDE Solver with Entanglement-Basis Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02316)
*Abhijatmedhi Chotrattanapituk,Michael J. Landry,Chu-Liang Fu,Mingda Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出基于纠缠基（entanglement-basis）表示的有限元方法（FEM）用于偏微分方程（PDE）求解，将非线性有限元方程提升至增广系数空间，借助张量网络（tensor network）实现高效表示，并以矩阵乘积态（MPS）与密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）扫描实现逐元素优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元求解非线性偏微分方程时，控制方程与边界、初始及元素间约束往往需分别处理，缺乏统一表述；同时增广空间随元素数指数增长，直接构造不可行，因此需要兼顾统一性与可扩展性的求解框架。

Method: 将非线性有限元方程提升到增广系数空间（augmented coefficient space），用统一的二次残差最小化（quadratic residual minimization）表达控制方程及边界、初始、元素间约束；利用其张量积结构，以矩阵乘积态（MPS）为例进行张量网络表示，通过密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）扫描实现逐元素优化，避免显式构造完整增广空间；并通过隐式时间离散（implicit temporal discretization）扩展至含时问题。

Result: 在有限键维（bond dimension）下，计算成本随有限元数量呈多项式（polynomial）增长；以扩散方程（diffusion equations）为例，在网格细化与多项式细化下均展示出收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，纠缠基表示与张量网络方法结合，可避免构造指数规模的增广空间，从而为基于有限元的偏微分方程求解提供具备多项式复杂度的可行路径，并可通过隐式离散推广到含时问题。

Abstract: We develop a finite element method (FEM) for partial differential equation (PDE) solver based on the entanglement-basis representation introduced in our companion work. By lifting non-linear finite-element equations into an augmented coefficient space, the governing PDE together with boundary, initial, and inter-element constraints can be expressed through a unified quadratic residual minimization. Although this augmented space grows exponentially with the number of elements, its tensor-product structure allows it to be represented efficiently using tensor networks. Using the matrix product state (MPS) as a concrete example, we show that density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) sweeps enable element-by-element optimization without explicitly constructing the full augmented space. For bounded bond dimension, the resulting computational cost scales polynomially with the number of finite elements. We extend the framework to time-dependent problems through implicit temporal discretization and demonstrate convergence under both mesh and polynomial refinement using diffusion equations.

</details>


<div id='physics.data-an'></div>

# physics.data-an [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Developing Multi-Dimensional, Sequential sWeighting Routines for Reaction Selection with Emphasis on Kaon Identification at CLAS12](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02246)
*A. Acar,M. Bashkanov,D. P. Watts,M. Nicol*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 该文提出 sWeight 技术的一种多维序贯扩展（multidimensional sequential framework），可同时使用任意数量的判别变量进行事件选择，并在相关背景存在时仍能获得高纯度信号样本，最终在 CLAS12 探测器的 K 介子鉴别（kaon identification）任务上验证了该方法的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核与粒子物理实验中，事件选择通常是涉及多个相关可观测量（correlated observables）的多维分类问题。广泛使用的 sWeight 技术虽在统计上严格，但传统形式一般仅适用于单一判别变量或需同时进行多维拟合，难以灵活处理多变量序贯选择的需求。

Method: 提出 sWeight 技术的新型扩展，允许以任意数量的判别变量（discriminating variables）构建多维序贯框架，从而在受控变量中严格提取信号分布。方法特别关注信号与背景相关性（correlations）的处理，以及统计不确定性（statistical uncertainties）在各连续加权阶段的传播。

Result: 即使存在大量且相关的背景，该方法仍能给出高纯度信号样本。以 Thomas Jefferson 国家实验室 CLAS12 探测器采集的数据中用于级联重子（cascade baryon）搜寻的 K 介子鉴别作为基准应用，最终工作流在不同运行周期与束流/探测器条件下保持一致且稳健，方法相关的系统不确定性（systematical uncertainties）极小，统计不确定性可用基于自助法（bootstrap-based procedure）的方式评估。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个通用的多维事件加权（multidimensional event weighting）框架，适用于需要在复杂相关背景下进行稳健信号选择的复杂分析。

Abstract: In nuclear and particle physics experiments, event selection is often a multidimensional classification problem which involves several correlated observables. The widely used sWeight technique provides a statistically rigorous means of separating signal and background using discriminating variables, but its conventional formulation is generally applied to a single discriminating variable or a simultaneous multidimensional fit. In this paper we present a novel extension of the widely used sWeight technique, in which an arbitrary number of discriminating variables can be used to construct a multidimensional sequential framework. This method allows the extraction of signal distributions in controlled variables in a rigorous way. Particular attention is given to the treatment of signal and background correlations, as well as the propagation of statistical uncertainties through successive weighting stages. The method yields high-purity signal samples even in the presence of large and correlated backgrounds. As a benchmark application, the validity of this technique is demonstrated on kaon identification in a dataset collected with the CLAS12 detector at the Thomas Jefferson National Laboratory for cascade baryon searches. The final workflow is shown to be robust and consistent across different run periods and beam/detector conditions, demonstrating minimal systematical uncertainties associated with the method. Statistical uncertainties are proved to be evaluable using a bootstrap-based procedure. The method provides a general framework for multidimensional event weighting and is intended for application in complex analyses requiring robust signal selection in the presence of complex correlated backgrounds.

</details>

### [121] [Reformulating plastic instabilities within a damage-like variational framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03328)
*Baptiste Reyne*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种极小化的、与物理场无关的本构框架（field-agnostic constitutive framework），用类损伤内变量（damage-like internal variable）显式区分稳定硬化与不稳定软化，从而统一描述多种塑性失稳现象，并借助延性断裂（ductile fracture）领域的正则化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属屈服点现象、土壤与积雪中的反裂纹（anticracks）、点阵超材料中的平面塌缩等，本质上是具有应力软化（stress softening）与应变局部化（strain localization）特征的塑性失稳的不同表现。现有多个独立本构描述面临共性挑战：难以将局部事件与全局测量关联的实验困难，以及计算中的不收敛、伪局部化（spurious localization）与网格敏感性（mesh sensitivity）。

Method: 提出一个极小化的、与物理场无关的本构框架，将塑性软化用类损伤内变量表示，从而把稳定硬化行为与不稳定软化行为显式分离。所得公式化具有变分结构（variational structure），可利用延性断裂领域已有的正则化工具。

Result: 该框架通过显式分离硬化与软化并引入变分结构，使塑性软化问题可借助延性断裂社区的正则化手段处理，为局部化塑性流动的正则化变分公式化（regularized variational formulations）开辟路径。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在强调各类塑性失稳现象之间的相似性，并建立与连续介质损伤力学（continuum damage mechanics）的桥梁，从而为推动局部化塑性流动的正则化变分描述提供可能路径。

Abstract: Yield point phenomena in metals, anticracks in soils and snows, or planewise collapses in lattice metamaterials, are different manifestations of plastic instabilities characterized by stress softening and strain localization. Several independent constitutive descriptions exist and suffer from similar challenges: experimental difficulties to link local events to global measurements, and computational lack of convergence, spurious localization and mesh sensitivity. This work proposes a minimal field-agnostic constitutive framework where plastic softening is represented by a damage-like internal variable which explicitly separates stable hardening behavior from unstable softening. The resulting formulation admits a variational structure and can leverage regularization tools originally developed by the ductile fracture community. The framework aims at emphasizing similarities within plastic instability phenomena and establishing a bridge with continuum damage mechanics, to open a path towards regularized variational formulations of localized plastic flows.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.IM'></div>

### [122] [Learning Approximate Isometric Embeddings for Efficient Template Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02448)
*Alexandra Wernersson,Jessica Irwin,Melissa Lopez,Sarah Caudill*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 提出 \texttt{isobank}：一种归一化流（normalizing flow），学习近似等距（isometric）坐标变换，使潜在空间（latent space）中的欧氏距离近似物理参数空间中的度规诱导真距离，从而以欧氏距离检验完成球面覆盖（sphere covering），避免重复度规（metric）计算。在五维偏心双星引力波模板库（template bank）构建中，达到 97% 覆盖率、模板数比 \texttt{mbank} 少 23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球面覆盖问题应用广泛，但在弯曲高维流形上由于距离须由位置相关度规评估，精确解在计算上不可行。引力波模板放置（template placement）问题即属此类，需避免放置过程中反复的度规计算。

Method: 采用归一化流学习近似等距的坐标变换，使潜在空间欧氏距离逼近物理参数空间的度规诱导真距离；据此在潜在空间中用欧氏距离检验进行模板放置，规避放置期间的重复度规评估。在五维偏心双星参数空间上与随机放置代码 \texttt{mbank} 对比。

Result: 在最大失配（maximum mismatch）$\mu=0.03$ 下达到 97% 覆盖率，模板数比达到 94% 覆盖率的 \texttt{mbank} 少 23%。覆盖率在独立注入（injection）上测量，注入在质量、自旋与偏心率（eccentricity）上均匀抽取，并使用精确波形匹配而非度规近似。训练约 2.5 小时，在 32 个 CPU 核心上放置模板库不足 1 小时。

Conclusion: \texttt{isobank} 通过等距归一化流将弯曲高维流形上的覆盖问题转化为潜在空间欧氏距离检验，在引力波模板库构建中以更少模板获得更高覆盖率，且计算开销可控；代码已公开于 GitHub。

Abstract: The sphere covering problem has broad applications in science however, exact solutions are computationally infeasible on curved, high-dimensional manifolds because distances must be evaluated using a position-dependent metric. We present $\texttt{isobank}$, a normalizing flow that learns an approximately isometric coordinate transformation, such that Euclidean distances in the latent space approximate metric-induced proper distances in the physical parameter space. This enables template placement using Euclidean distance checks in the latent space, avoiding repeated metric evaluations during placement.
  We evaluate $\texttt{isobank}$ in the context of gravitational wave template bank construction, where the sphere covering problem corresponds to the template placement problem. On a five-dimensional eccentric binary parameter space, $\texttt{isobank}$ reaches 97\% coverage at a maximum mismatch of $μ=0.03$ with 23\% fewer templates than the state-of-the-art stochastic placement code $\texttt{mbank}$, which reaches 94\% coverage. Coverage is measured on independent injections drawn uniformly over the masses, spins and eccentricity, using the exact waveform match rather than the metric approximation. Training the flow takes about 2.5h and placing the bank under 1h on 32 CPU cores. $\texttt{isobank}$ is publicly available on Github https://github.com/Alexandra-Wernersson/isobank

</details>


<div id='math.CT'></div>

# math.CT [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [An Equivalence of Categories between 3-Crossed Modules and Gray 4-Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24034)
*Masaki Fukuda,Tommy Shu*

Main category: math.CT

TL;DR: 本文证明了新定义的 3-交叉模（3-crossed module）范畴与 Gray 型 4-群（Gray 4-group）范畴等价，从而为后者提供具体的群论计算工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arvasi 等人最初提出 3-交叉模以刻画同伦 4-型（homotopy 4-type），但其与高阶范畴的关系此前并不明确；同时 Gray 4-群虽为四维高阶结构提供概念框架，却常难以计算。本文旨在厘清二者的对应并解决可计算性问题。

Method: 引入一个 4-范畴（4-category），当限制到单一对象与单一 1-态射时退化为半严格编织幺半 2-范畴（semistrict braided monoidal 2-category）；并以该 4-范畴中所有态射可逆的单对象版本定义 Gray 4-群，进而证明其范畴与作者先前提出的 3-交叉模范畴等价。

Result: 证明了 3-交叉模范畴与 Gray 4-群范畴之间的等价关系。该等价将 3-交叉模确立为 Gray 4-群的具体群论演算体系。

Conclusion: 该等价为研究曲面纽结（surface knots）、状态和不变量（state-sum invariants）以及高阶规范理论（higher gauge theories）提供了强有力的计算工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the relation between the category of 3-crossed modules and the category of Gray-type 4-groups. The notion of a 3-crossed module was first introduced by Arvasi \textit{et al.}, motivated by the question of what kind of algebraic structure completely encodes a homotopy 4-type. On the other hand, from the point of view that higher groups are equivalent to algebraic realizations of higher categories -- as exemplified by the relationship between 2-crossed modules and Gray 3-groups established by Sarikaya--Ulualan -- it had not been clear how the 3-crossed modules of Arvasi \textit{et al.} relate to any higher category. In our previous paper, we proposed a new definition of a 3-crossed module and observed that it admits a natural interpretation in terms of higher categories. In this paper, we make this interpretation precise: we introduce a 4-category, which reduces to a semistrict braided monoidal 2-category when restricted to a single object and a single 1-morphism, and prove that the category of our 3-crossed modules is equivalent to the category of Gray 4-groups, defined as single-object versions of this 4-category in which all morphisms are invertible. Furthermore, while Gray 4-groups provide a conceptual framework for 4-dimensional higher structures, they are often computationally intractable. Our equivalence establishes 3-crossed modules as a concrete, group-theoretic calculus for Gray 4-groups, providing a powerful computational tool for studying surface knots, state-sum invariants, and higher gauge theories.

</details>


<div id='physics.bio-ph'></div>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Migrating cell clusters as active droplets with asymmetric shape and contact angles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03536)
*Hanno I. Hennighausen,Jaime A. Espina,Elias H. Barriga,Ricard Alert*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验与理论结合，揭示迁移中的非洲爪蟾（Xenopus）神经嵴（neural crest）细胞簇具有前低后高的非对称高度轮廓；作者将主动润湿（active wetting）模型扩展为形状由方程求解而非假定，提出非对称牵引力（traction）通过杨-拉普拉斯方程（Young-Laplace equation）在该组织上表面产生曲率梯度，从而解释非对称形状的成因，并据此估算组织黏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胚胎发育与癌症进程中，细胞簇会沿外部线索进行集体迁移（collective migration），常被建模为润湿基底（substrate）的主动液滴（active droplet）。然而既有主动润湿模型多假定细胞簇呈球形冠（spherical cap）的对称形状，与真实迁移细胞的形态是否相符缺乏检验，这一假定构成现有模型的潜在局限。

Method: 研究首先对蛙神经嵴细胞迁移簇的高度轮廓进行实验测量，观察其接触角（contact angle）在前后端的差异；随后在理论上扩展主动润湿模型，推导细胞簇形状的控制方程，使形状成为解的一部分而非预设假设；进而分析非对称牵引力如何经组织内压力梯度（pressure gradient）与杨-拉普拉斯方程产生上表面曲率梯度；最后将理论预测与实测高度轮廓进行拟合以估算组织黏度（tissue viscosity）。

Result: 实验显示迁移的蛙神经嵴细胞簇具有非对称高度轮廓，前端接触角低于后端。理论结果表明，形状非对称性源于驱动细胞簇迁移的非对称牵引力：其跨组织产生压力梯度，并通过杨-拉普拉斯方程在上表面形成曲率梯度。通过对实测高度轮廓的拟合，得到了组织黏度的估计值。

Conclusion: 研究提出了迁移细胞簇非对称形状涌现的通用机制，即将其理解为处于润湿性梯度（wettability gradient）上的主动液滴（active droplet），并表明形状可由模型自洽求解而非人为假定。

Abstract: During embryonic development and cancer progression, clusters of cells migrate collectively following external cues. Such migrating cell clusters have been modeled as active droplets wetting a substrate. So far, this active wetting model assumed the cluster to take the symmetric shape of a spherical cap. Here, in contrast, we experimentally show that migrating clusters of frog neural crest cells have asymmetric height profiles, with a lower contact angle at the front than at the rear. We then extend the active wetting model by deriving an equation for the cluster shape, which becomes part of the solution rather than an assumption. Our model shows that shape asymmetry can arise from the asymmetric tractions that drive cluster migration: They generate a pressure gradient across the tissue which, via the Young-Laplace equation, leads to a curvature gradient on the cluster's upper surface. Finally, by fitting the theoretical predictions to the measured height profile, we estimate the tissue viscosity. Overall, our results provide a general mechanism for the emergence of asymmetric shapes in migrating cell clusters, understood as active droplets on a wettability gradient.

</details>

### [125] [Preparation sequence controls oxygen partitioning between boron and lithium on tungsten](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02905)
*Predrag S Krstic,Meral Sharkass,Swarit Dwivedi,Dalia Sayed Ahmed,Yun Kyung Shin,Adri CT van Duin*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 经典反应分子动力学（classical reactive molecular dynamics）模拟表明，硼与氧的引入顺序、而非硼的总量，决定硼/锂调制钨表面的表面化学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究针对硼（boron）与锂（lithium）调制钨（tungsten）表面在氧化条件下的化学行为，试图厘清硼储量与引入顺序对表面化学的支配作用，并预判其对氘（deuterium）辐照的响应差异。

Method: 采用经典反应分子动力学（classical reactive molecular dynamics）模拟，考察三类体系：薄工程化氧化钨、约2 nm的WO3/WO2混合膜，以及先共沉积W-B后暴露于氧的基底；并进一步引入锂进行比较。

Result: 在薄工程化氧化钨上，硼捕获81%的氧并消耗该氧化物；在约2 nm的WO3/WO2混合膜上，硼仅捕获20%的氧，反应区中52%的钨保持类WO3配位；在共沉积W-B基底中，尽管硼储量高出十倍，硼仅捕获28%的氧。加入锂对“氧化物优先”情形无影响，但在共沉积情形中使锂成为主要氧捕获者（58%），并形成类硼酸锂玻璃网络。

Conclusion: 表面化学由硼与氧的引入顺序主导，而非硼的总量；锂的作用高度依赖于初始构型。据此，研究对纯硼材料与硼加锂材料在氘辐照下的响应差异作出了预期性描述。

Abstract: Classical reactive molecular dynamics of boron- and lithium-conditioned tungsten surfaces show that the order in which boron and oxygen are introduced - not the boron inventory - controls surface chemistry. On a thin engineered tungsten oxide, boron captures 81% of oxygen and consumes the oxide; on an engineered ~2 nm mixed WO3/WO2 film, boron captures only 20% and leaves 52% of reaction-zone tungsten in WO3-like coordination; and in a co-deposited W-B substrate later exposed to oxygen, boron captures only 28% despite a tenfold larger inventory. Adding lithium leaves the oxide-first cases unchanged but transforms the co-deposited case, where lithium becomes the dominant oxygen captor (58%) and a lithium-borate-glass-like network form. We outline the expected, divergent responses of the boron-only and boron-plus-lithium materials to deuterium irradiation.

</details>


### [126] [Bogoliubov self-consistent $GW$ and second-order Green's function methods for attractive fermionic interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03357)
*Mauricio Rodr{í}guez-Mayorga,Johannes Tölle,Pierre-François Loos*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文在统一的虚时（imaginary-time）与虚频（imaginary-frequency）框架下实现并比较了 Bogoliubov 全自洽 $GW$（Bog. scGW）与二阶格林函数（Bog. scGF2）方法，以吸引型哈伯德模型（attractive Hubbard model）对照全组态相互作用（full configuration interaction, FCI）进行基准测试。结果显示 Bog. scGF2 的精度优于 Bog. scGW，后者系统性高估关联能（correlation energy）且随吸引相互作用增强误差快速增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对超导体、原子核等具有吸引相互作用的费米子体系（fermionic systems），超越平均场（mean-field）层次的描述仍是核心挑战。该工作旨在评估自洽格林函数类方法在此类强关联体系中的表现，并厘清交换效应（exchange effects）的作用。

Method: 采用统一实现于虚时与虚频框架下的 Bogoliubov 全自洽 $GW$（Bog. scGW）与二阶格林函数（Bog. scGF2）两种方法。以吸引型哈伯德模型为基准，将两者结果与全组态相互作用（FCI）进行对照比较。

Result: Bog. scGF2 相比 Bog. scGW 给出更准确的描述。Bog. scGW 系统性高估关联能，且误差随吸引相互作用强度的增加而迅速增大。结果凸显了交换贡献在强关联费米子体系中的重要性，即便交换效应仅在微扰论（perturbation theory）的低阶被纳入。

Conclusion: 在吸引型费米子体系的自洽格林函数计算中，纳入低阶交换贡献（如 scGF2）对准确描述关联能至关重要，$GW$ 类近似因缺失或不足的交换处理而表现欠佳。这提示强关联情形下交换项不可忽略。

Abstract: Describing fermionic systems with attractive interactions, such as superconductors and atomic nuclei, beyond the mean-field level remains a central challenge. In this work, we investigate the performance of Bogoliubov fully self-consistent $GW$ (Bog.~sc$GW$) and second-order Green's function (Bog.~scGF2) methods, implemented within a unified imaginary-time and imaginary-frequency framework. Using the attractive Hubbard model as a benchmark against full configuration interaction (FCI), we find that Bog. scGF2 provides a more accurate description than Bog.~sc$GW$. In particular, Bog.~sc$GW$ systematically overestimates the correlation energy, with errors increasing rapidly as the attractive interaction strength grows. These results highlight the importance of exchange contributions in strongly correlated fermionic systems, even when exchange effects are included only at low order in perturbation theory.

</details>


### [127] [Electronic Density versus Geometry for Machine-Learned Molecular Absorption Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03444)
*Siddharth Dhanpal,Peter Elliott,Paolo Emilio Trevisanutto,Alin M. Elena,Gilberto Teobaldi*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该论文比较了以基态电子密度（ground-state electron density）与分子几何结构（molecular geometry）作为机器学习（ML）输入对吸收光谱（absorption spectra）预测性能的影响。结果显示，基于密度的卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network）验证相关系数达 0.9926，优于最佳几何图模型的 0.9795，残余去相关降低约 64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子光吸收光谱（optical absorption spectroscopy）是探测电子结构（electronic structure）的直接手段，但第一性原理激发态方法（first-principle excited-state methods）计算成本高昂，限制其大规模应用。机器学习代理模型（ML surrogates）可降低成本，但其性能高度依赖分子信息的表示方式，因此需探究更优的输入表示。

Method: 研究从 QM7 数据集中选取 6874 个分子作为训练集，对每个分子使用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算基态密度，并使用线性响应（linear-response, LR）含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）计算吸收光谱。比较以基态电子密度与分子几何结构分别作为机器学习模型输入的表现，密度输入的选择依据 Hohenberg-Kohn 与 Runge-Gross 定理。

Result: 基于密度的卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network）取得 0.9926 的验证相关系数，而最佳的基于几何结构的图模型（graph model）为 0.9795，前者使残余去相关（residual decorrelation）降低约 64%。

Conclusion: 使用基态密度作为机器学习模型输入的性能优势，超过其所需的额外单点 DFT 计算（single-point DFT calculation）所带来的（并非过高的）代价。基态密度能编码成键、电荷定域（charge localisation）与电子离域（electronic delocalisation）信息，可有效解耦基态化学性质，是较几何结构更明智的建模起点。

Abstract: Molecular optical absorption spectroscopy provides a direct probe of electronic structure and is widely used for molecular identification, interpretation of photophysical behaviour, and planning of spectroscopy experiments. Calculating the absorption spectra using first-principle excited-state methods, however, is computationally demanding, at least compared to ground-state calculations, which limits their routine application across large molecular sets. Machine-learning (ML) surrogates can reduce this cost and allow rapid spectral prediction. However, their performance depends strongly on how molecular information is represented. Here, we compare using the ground-state electron density versus the molecular geometry as inputs to a ML model for predicting absorption spectra, for a training set of 6874 molecules selected from the QM7 dataset. For each of these molecules, the density was calculated using density functional theory (DFT) and the absorption spectrum was calculated using linear-response (LR) time-dependent DFT (TDDFT). Utilizing the ground-state density as the input to the ML model is motivated by the Hohenberg-Kohn and Runge-Gross theorems, and the fact that the ground-state density encodes information about bonding, charge localisation, and electronic delocalisation. Hence, it may be a more judicious starting point for the ML model compared to the geometry, as it effectively decouples the chemistry of the ground-state. The question we test is whether the benefits of using the density outweigh the (notprohibitive) penalty of requiring an additional single-point DFT calculation for the density. We find that the density-based convolutional neural network achieves a validation correlation of 0.9926, compared with 0.9795 for the best geometry-based graph model, reducing the residual decorrelation, by approximately 64%.

</details>
