### [128] [How Quantum Is Bottomonium in the Quark-Gluon Plasma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02313)
*Nora Brambilla,Tom Magorsch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在开放量子系统框架下，通过求解从势非相对论QCD导出的Lindblad方程，研究底偶素（bottomonium）在夸克-胶子等离子体中演化时保留的量子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨底偶素在夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）演化过程中保留多少量子结构，并检验经典Langevin型近似所依赖的关键条件是否成立。

Method: 在开放量子系统（open quantum system）框架内，模拟由势非相对论QCD（potential nonrelativistic QCD）在量子布朗区（quantum Brownian regime）导出的Lindblad方程，由底偶素密度矩阵的实时演化计算Wigner变换，并以负性（negativity）作为非经典性度量，同时考察位置空间相干性。

Result: 介质压低负性，但其趋近于接近1S态的有限平台；与激发态2S、3S的重叠源于相空间正负贡献的近乎抵消，由束缚态投影算符Wigner变换的负区域驱动，而1S重叠对此不敏感；位置空间相干性同样被压低并趋于1S值附近。

Conclusion: 残余的有限量子结构可归因于条件纯化（conditional purification）：介质优先溶解弱束缚与不束缚、具有大⟨r²⟩的模式，使幸存的单态S波系综趋向1S态；结果表明经典化（classicalization）是部分的且依赖于所观测量。

Abstract: We study how much quantum structure bottomonium retains during its evolution in the quark-gluon plasma. Within the open quantum system framework, we simulate the Lindblad equation obtained from potential nonrelativistic QCD in the quantum Brownian regime. From the resulting real-time evolution of the bottomonium density matrix, we compute the Wigner transform and study its negativity as a measure of nonclassicality and a test of a key condition underlying classical Langevin-type approximations. We find that the medium suppresses the negativity, which nevertheless approaches a finite plateau close to that of the $1\mathrm{S}$ state. The overlaps with the excited $2\mathrm{S}$ and $3\mathrm{S}$ states arise from near-canceling positive and negative phase-space contributions, driven by negative regions in the Wigner transforms of the bound-state projectors, while the $1\mathrm{S}$ overlap is insensitive to such contributions. We further show that the position-space coherence is similarly suppressed and plateaus close to the 1S value. We trace the finite residual quantum structure to conditional purification: the medium preferentially dissolves weakly bound and unbound modes with large $\langle r^2\rangle$, driving the surviving singlet $\mathrm{S}$-wave ensemble toward the $1\mathrm{S}$ state. More broadly, these results reveal that classicalization is partial and observable dependent, a feature that is relevant across applications of open quantum system methods in fundamental physics.

</details>


### [129] [Precise QCD Predictions for Photon-Associated Hadron Production at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02327)
*Gabriele Fiore,Aude Gehrmann-De Ridder,Alexander Huss,Francesco Merlotti,Giovanni Stagnitto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作首次给出大型强子对撞机（LHC）上光子伴随已鉴别强子产生的完全微分 NNLO QCD 预言，并与 ALICE 和 ATLAS 在 5.02 TeV 的数据进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子-光子型过程伴随反冲 QCD 辐射对新物理搜寻、部分子分布函数与碎裂函数（fragmentation function, FF）的确定，以及重离子碰撞中的喷注淬火（jet quenching）研究均具有关键意义，因而需要更高精度的理论预言。

Method: 采用微扰 QCD 的次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-leading order, NNLO）计算，给出光子伴随已鉴别强子产生的完全微分（fully differential）截面，并使用不同的碎裂函数（FF）集合进行对比分析。

Result: 将所获得的预言与 ALICE 和 ATLAS 在质心能量 √s = 5.02 TeV 下的实验数据进行对比，考察不同 FF 集合带来的差异。

Conclusion: 该计算为碎裂函数全局拟合提供了重要理论输入，并为光子标记（photon-tagged）的重离子研究提供了精确的质子-质子基线。

Abstract: Prompt-photon production with recoiling QCD radiation is crucial for new-physics searches, determinations of parton distribution and fragmentation functions, and jet-quenching studies in heavy-ion collisions. We present the first fully differential NNLO QCD predictions for photon-associated identified-hadron production at the LHC. We compare predictions obtained with different fragmentation function (FF) sets to ALICE and ATLAS data at $\sqrt{s}=5.02\,{\rm TeV}$. Our calculation provides an important theoretical ingredient for FF global fits and offers a precision proton--proton baseline for photon-tagged heavy-ion studies.

</details>


### [130] [Third-Order Logarithmic Resummation for Jet-Vetoed Higgs Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03687)
*Samuel Abreu,Jonathan R. Gaunt,Pier Francesco Monni,Luca Rottoli,Robert Szafron*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作首次给出 QCD 中第三对数阶（third logarithmic order）的希格斯（Higgs）喷注否决截面（jet-vetoed cross section）预言，将 N³LO 计算与 N³LL 重求和一致结合，残余微扰不确定度约 3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喷注否决（jet vetoes）是大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）上希格斯玻色子性质精确测量的核心手段，用于压低含硬 QCD 辐射（hard QCD radiation）的本底。为满足高亮度阶段（high-luminosity phase）的精度需求，需要更高阶的理论预言。

Method: 在 QCD 中首次计算第三对数阶的希格斯喷注否决截面，将次次次领头阶（next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order, N³LO）计算与当前最优的次次次领头对数重求和（next-to-next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic resummation, N³LL）一致地结合。

Result: 在唯象相关的否决尺度（p_T^v ∼ 30 GeV）与喷注半径（R = 0.4）下，重求和使固定阶预言增大约 3%，两者结合后的残余微扰不确定度约为 3%。

Conclusion: 该结果是强子对撞机上喷注否决希格斯产生（jet-vetoed Higgs production）精确描述的一个里程碑，达到了与 LHC 高亮度阶段相关的精度水平。

Abstract: Jet vetoes are central to precision measurements of Higgs-boson properties at the Large Hadron Collider, where they are used to suppress backgrounds containing hard QCD radiation. We present the first prediction for the Higgs jet-vetoed cross section at the third logarithmic order in QCD, consistently combining the next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order calculation with state-of-the-art next-to-next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic resummation. At phenomenologically relevant veto scales ($p_T^{\rm v}\sim30$ GeV) and jet radii ($R=0.4$), the resummation increases the fixed-order prediction by about $3\%$, and their combination leads to a residual perturbative uncertainty of approximately $3\%$. Our results mark a milestone in the precise description of jet-vetoed Higgs-production at hadron colliders, reaching the level of precision relevant for the high-luminosity phase of the LHC.

</details>


### [131] [Derivation of color projectors on multi-quark states using the Casimir operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02319)
*Stéphane Peigné,Clément Liesse*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文给出多夸克（multi-quark）SU(N) 颜色态上厄米投影算符（Hermitian projectors）的一种初等递归构造，构造依据是这些态由二次卡西米尔算符（quadratic Casimir）刻画这一性质，并借助鸟迹图（birdtrack）图示技巧实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多夸克 SU(N) 颜色态空间需要按不可约表示进行投影与分解，而投影算符的显式构造通常较为繁复。该文旨在提供一种初等且递归的构造方式，以简化此类投影算符的获取。

Method: 方法基于二次卡西米尔算符对多夸克 SU(N) 颜色态的刻画（characterization），并结合鸟迹图（birdtrack）图示技巧，以递归（recursive）方式构造厄米投影算符（Hermitian projectors）。

Result: 摘要仅说明该构造是初等的、递归的，并成功给出多夸克 SU(N) 颜色态上的厄米投影算符；未提供具体算符形式或计算示例。

Conclusion: 多夸克 SU(N) 颜色态的二次卡西米尔刻画与鸟迹图技巧相结合，可为厄米投影算符提供一种初等递归的构造途径。摘要未给出进一步的推广或应用说明。

Abstract: We present an elementary recursive construction of Hermitian projectors on multi-quark ${\rm SU}(N)$ color states, based on the characterization of these states by their quadratic Casimir, and using the birdtrack pictorial technique.

</details>


### [132] [$SU(\mathcal{N})$ baryon formation in the early Universe and dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02464)
*Luca Di Luzio,Samuele Di Valeriano,Enrico Nardi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在 $SU(\mathcal{N})$ 规范理论的禁闭交叉（confinement crossover）中，$\mathcal{N}$-夸克重子（$\mathcal{N}$-quark baryon）通过夸克团簇（quark-cluster）逐级融合形成；色相互作用速率遵循二次卡西米尔标度（quadratic Casimir scaling）。大 $\mathcal{N}$ 下出现卡西米尔瓶颈（Casimir bottleneck），使重子形成受到强烈抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大 $\mathcal{N}$ 规范理论中重子形成机制的动机，在于考察重子-介子产额比（baryon-to-meson yield ratio）随 $\mathcal{N}$ 的变化，并将该机制嵌入具体模型以评估宇宙学稳定中性重子的遗迹丰度（relic density）是否可作为暗物质。

Method: 采用逐步重子形成假设，即夸克团簇成对逐次融合；引入 $SU(\mathcal{N})$ 反对称团簇（antisymmetric clusters）的完整反应集合，并计入其自旋-味多重数（spin-flavor multiplicities）；色相互作用速率按二次卡西米尔标度设定。进一步将机制嵌入矢量型三味（vectorlike three-flavor）模型，纳入轴矢反常诱导耦合（axial-anomaly-induced couplings）与六维算符（dimension-six operators）。

Result: 在大 $\mathcal{N}$ 下，初始融合步骤中少夸克团簇的融合被团簇破坏过程强烈压过，形成卡西米尔瓶颈。$\mathcal{N}=12\ (18)$ 时，重子-介子产额比降至 $\mathcal{N}=3$ 对应值的 $10^{-12}\ (10^{-23})$。模型中不稳定介子与带电重子经反常诱导耦合及六维算符衰变，可在太初核合成（big-bang nucleosynthesis）之前安全完成。

Conclusion: 大 $\mathcal{N}$ 下宇宙学稳定的中性 $\mathcal{N}$-重子遗迹密度在禁闭时期被设定得极小；当 $\mathcal{N}$ 足够大时，为饱和暗物质能量密度所需的重子质量比湮灭截面（annihilation cross sections）的单位性界（unitarity bound）高出若干数量级。

Abstract: We study the formation of $\mathcal{N}$-quark baryons during the confinement crossover of an $SU(\mathcal{N})$ gauge theory containing quarks in the fundamental representation, with masses around or below the confinement scale. We assume stepwise baryon formation through the successive fusion of pairs of quark-clusters, with color interaction rates obeying quadratic Casimir scaling. We include the complete set of reactions between $SU(\mathcal{N})$ antisymmetric clusters dressed with their specific spin-flavor multiplicities. We show that at large $\mathcal{N}$, baryon formation is hindered by a Casimir bottleneck, such that fusion of few-quark clusters in the initial steps of the baryon assembly chain is strongly outweighed by cluster destruction processes. We find that for $\mathcal{N}=12\,(18)$ the baryon-to-meson yield ratio is suppressed down to $10^{-12}\,(10^{-23})$ of its value at $\mathcal{N}=3$. We embed the mechanism in a vectorlike three-flavor model whose lightest baryon can be electrically neutral, while axial-anomaly-induced couplings and dimension-six operators induce decays of the unstable mesons and charged baryons safely before big-bang nucleosynthesis. The relic density of cosmologically stable neutral $\mathcal{N}$-baryons is thus set during confinement at values so small that, for sufficiently large $\mathcal{N}$, the baryon masses required to saturate the dark matter energy density exceed by several orders of magnitude the unitarity bound on annihilation cross sections.

</details>


### [133] [Photon Quantization and Propagator in Rotating Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02656)
*Alejandro Ayala,Santiago Bernal-Langarica,Jorge David Castaño-Yepes,José Jorge Medina-Serna*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在刚性旋转柱坐标系中构造了量子化电磁场与光子传播子（photon propagator），通过求解协变麦克斯韦理论（covariant Maxwell theory）的规范固定场方程，建立了光子模式、量子化及完整传播子的两套互补构造，并验证其满足格林函数方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在旋转参考系中进行含内光子线（internal photon lines）的协变微扰计算（covariant perturbative calculations），需要构造相应的量子化电磁场与光子传播子。电磁场的矢量性质要求区分轨道角动量与内禀角动量（orbital and intrinsic angular momentum）。

Method: 从协变麦克斯韦理论出发，在柱坐标下建立规范固定场方程并求解；以贝塞尔函数（Bessel functions）构造两个独立物理光子模式，并给出基于协变常数四元组（tetrad）生成的协变矢量本征函数基的替代构造；经麦克斯韦内积（Maxwell inner product）归一化后量子化电磁场，定义光子态及大于与小于两点函数（greater and lesser two-point functions）。完整规范固定传播子通过两种途径构造：由标量格林函数结合惯性费曼规范传播子的张量变换，以及由协变矢量本征系统经谱分解与 Fock--Schwinger 固有时间表示。

Result: 物理时序传播子（physical time-ordered propagator）直接由量子化模式得到，其横向依赖编码于保持柱空间结构的极化核（polarization kernels）中，谱极点（spectral poles）体现旋转能移。两种构造均满足完整规范固定麦克斯韦算符的格林函数方程。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了旋转参考系中含内光子线的协变微扰计算所需的传播子，其两套独立构造相互印证并满足格林函数方程。

Abstract: We construct the quantized electromagnetic field and the corresponding photon propagators in a rigidly rotating cylindrical reference frame. Starting from covariant Maxwell theory, we formulate the gauge-fixed field equations and solve them in cylindrical coordinates. The vector nature of the electromagnetic field requires distinguishing orbital and intrinsic angular momentum, with the total angular momentum as the natural quantum number. We construct the two independent physical photon modes in terms of Bessel functions by solving the coupled differential equations, and provide an alternative construction based on a covariant basis of vector eigenfunctions generated by a covariantly constant tetrad. After normalizing the modes with the Maxwell inner product, we quantize the electromagnetic field and define the corresponding photon states and greater and lesser two-point functions. The physical time-ordered propagator is obtained directly from the quantized modes, with its transverse dependence encoded in polarization kernels that retain the cylindrical spatial structure and its spectral poles exhibiting the rotational energy shift. The full gauge-fixed photon propagator is constructed in two complementary ways: from the scalar Green function combined with the tensor transformation of the inertial Feynman-gauge propagator, and from the covariant vector eigensystem through its spectral decomposition and the Fock--Schwinger proper-time representation. We verify that both constructions satisfy the Green-function equation for the complete gauge-fixed Maxwell operator, providing the propagator required for covariant perturbative calculations with internal photon lines.

</details>


### [134] [Higgsino dark matter compatible with the LUX-ZEPLIN high-energy nuclear-recoil event and IceCube constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03692)
*Katherine Freese,Dionysios P. Theodosopoulos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估Higgsino暗物质（Higgsino dark matter, DM）能否解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验报告的248 keV核反冲感兴趣事件，并同时纳入IceCube对太阳中暗物质湮灭的限制。分析表明，在忽略太阳内弹性冷却与LZ高能边带约束、并考虑大麦哲伦云（LMC）诱导的高速尾时，1.1 TeV热Higgsino仅边际兼容；更高质量Higgsino则是该事件的优秀候选，兼容参数区域可延伸至数百TeV乃至更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验报告了248 keV核反冲感兴趣事件，需要理论解释；Higgsino暗物质在近纯极限下天然实现非弹性暗物质（inelastic DM），因此有必要检验其与该事件的兼容性。同时，IceCube对太阳中暗物质湮灭的限制构成重要约束，需一并纳入分析。

Method: 采用似然分析（likelihood analysis），确定与LZ事件兼容且符合IceCube零结果的90%置信区域，并分别考虑是否加入LZ高能边带无事件约束。暗物质分布方面，仅采用标准晕模型（Standard Halo Model），并考虑LMC诱导的高速尾增强；IceCube限制则依据太阳内冷却通道的不同假设给出四种变体，以应对Higgsino-核子弹性散射的理论不确定性。

Result: 1.1 TeV热Higgsino仅在纳入LMC高速尾、并忽略太阳内弹性冷却与LZ高能边带约束时，才与LZ事件和IceCube限制边际兼容。更高质量Higgsino在IceCube约束下是该事件的优秀候选；依晕分布、冷却相互作用及是否纳入LZ高能边带而定，兼容区域在Higgsino参数空间（m_χ, δ）中从约2 TeV延伸至数百TeV或更高，质量劈裂约为350至525 keV。

Conclusion: Higgsino暗物质，特别是较高质量情形，可解释LZ报告的248 keV核反冲事件并与IceCube太阳湮灭限制相容；其兼容参数区域对暗物质晕分布、太阳内冷却相互作用及LZ高能边带约束的选择较为敏感。

Abstract: We examine Higgsino dark matter (DM) as a potential explanation of the $248~\mathrm{keV}$ nuclear-recoil event of interest reported by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment, taking into account IceCube constraints on DM annihilation in the Sun. In the nearly pure Higgsino limit, two neutral Majorana states separated by a small mass splitting $δ$ are connected by an off-diagonal coupling to the $Z$ boson, providing a natural realization of inelastic DM. Using a likelihood analysis, we identify 90\% confidence regions compatible with the LZ event and consistent with the null results of IceCube searches, both with and without including a constraint from the absence of events in the LZ high-energy sideband. In our LZ analysis, we consider the DM distribution in the Standard Halo Model alone as well as possible enhancements of the high-speed tail induced by the Large Magellanic Cloud (LMC). We present four variants of bounds from IceCube on Higgsino dark matter, depending on various assumptions on cooling channels in the Sun, motivated by severe theoretical uncertainties about Higgsino-nucleon elastic scattering (unimportant for direct detection but crucial for IceCube bounds). Whereas the thermal Higgsino with a mass of $1.1~\mathrm{TeV}$ remains only marginally compatible with both the LZ event and the IceCube limits when the LMC-induced high-speed tail is included and both elastic cooling in the Sun and the LZ high-energy sideband constraint are neglected, higher mass Higgsinos are excellent candidates for the LZ event in light of IceCube constraints. Depending on the halo distribution, cooling interactions, and whether the LZ high-energy sideband is included, the compatible regions in the Higgsino parameter space $(m_χ,δ)$ extend from masses of $\sim2~\mathrm{TeV}$ to hundreds of TeV or higher, with mass splittings ranging approximately from $350$ to $525~\mathrm{keV}$.

</details>

### [135] [Mirror symmetry for gCICY threefolds (II): $[3,-1]$ blocks, VGIT, and toric phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03688)
*Atsushi Kanazawa*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{P}^1\times B$ 上含 $[3,-1]$ 块的广义完全交 Calabi-Yau（gCICY）三维流形，通过辅助变量将其映射为相对 GIT 变化，使镜对称（mirror symmetry）问题归结为普通的 Batyrev-Borisov 对偶。作者对 18 个此类构型进行分类并详细分析五个例子，显式构造镜像并计算低阶 Picard-Fuchs 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: gCICY 的配置矩阵含负元素，其定义线丛非 nef，导致 Batyrev-Borisov 构造无法直接适用，因此需要寻找新的途径来研究这类流形的镜对称与 Hodge 数。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{P}^1\times B$ 上考虑由 $O(3)\boxtimes L$ 与 $O(-1)\boxtimes(L+R)$ 的截面定义的 $[3,-1]$ 块；引入辅助变量将广义截面转化为相对 GIT 变化，其相反相位为 $\mathbb{P}^2\times B$ 上秩 3 向量丛的零点轨迹，两相位同构或由 $\int_B c_1(L)^4$ 个不交 Atiyah flop 相联系。

Result: 在射影空间乘积上恰有 18 个含 $[3,-1]$ 块的 Calabi-Yau 三维构型；除伴随论文中的非 Gorenstein 情形外，其余均具有 Gorenstein toric nef 相位，从而将镜对称约化为 Batyrev-Borisov 对偶。作者详细分析四个 $[3,-1]$ 块例子及一个类外例子，构造显式镜像，计算基本周期与低阶 Picard-Fuchs 系统，并发现极大单值边界点的指标代数同构于 A-模型相位的有理偶上同调环。

Conclusion: 含 $[3,-1]$ 块的 gCICY 三维流形除个别非 Gorenstein 情形外，均可经 GIT 变化后在 Gorenstein toric nef 相位下借助普通 Batyrev-Borisov 对偶获得其镜像 Hodge 数；指标代数与 A-模型上同调环的同构为镜对称提供了新的验证途径。

Abstract: The configuration matrices of generalized complete intersection Calabi-Yau (gCICY) threefolds contain negative entries, so their defining line bundles are not nef and the Batyrev-Borisov construction does not apply directly. We study gCICY threefolds in $\mathbb{P}^1\times B$ with a $[3,-1]$ block, that is, cut out by sections of $O(3)\boxtimes L$ and $O(-1)\boxtimes(L+R)$, where $B$ is a smooth projective fourfold and $2L+R=-K_B$. Auxiliary variables turn the generalized section into a relative variation of GIT, whose opposite phase is the zero locus of a rank-3 bundle on $\mathbb{P}^2\times B$; for general data the two phases are isomorphic or related by $\int_B c_1(L)^4$ disjoint Atiyah flops. Over products of projective spaces there are exactly 18 Calabi-Yau threefold configurations with a $[3,-1]$ block. All of them except the non-Gorenstein case studied in the companion paper have Gorenstein toric nef phases. Thus, for every other configuration in this classification, mirror symmetry is reduced after variation of GIT to ordinary Batyrev-Borisov duality, which gives the mirror Hodge numbers of the generalized phase itself. We analyze four examples with a $[3,-1]$ block and one outside this class in detail. For each we construct an explicit mirror and compute its fundamental period and low-order Picard-Fuchs system; the indicial algebras at the maximally unipotent boundary points are isomorphic to the rational even cohomology rings of the A-model phases.

</details>


### [136] [Mirror symmetry for gCICY threefolds (I): a $[3,-1]$ block and a non-Gorenstein toric phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03719)
*Atsushi Kanazawa*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为广义完全交 Calabi-Yau 三维流形（generalized complete intersection Calabi-Yau threefold）$X_+$ 构造并分析镜像族，通过引入辅助变量将其转化为环面 GIT（toric GIT）的变体，越墙翻缩（flop）得到 $X_-$，并构造光滑射影 crepant 紧化 $Y$，其周期满足秩 6 的 Picard-Fuchs 系统，镜像计数与独立枚举一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象 $X_+$ 为带负次数截面的广义完全交，其上同调维数为 $(h^{1,1},h^{2,1})=(2,46)$。其镜像对应 $X_-$ 位于非 Gorenstein 的环面 $\mathbb{Q}$-Fano 八维环境空间中，导致 Batyrev-Borisov 构造不适用，需要另辟蹊径构造镜像。

Method: 采用辅助变量表示（auxiliary-variable presentation），把负次数截面化为环面 GIT 变体；越墙翻缩 16 条不相交 $(-1,-1)$-曲线得 $X_-$。仅借助 Hori-Vafa 方程（Hori-Vafa equations）筛选出一个 2 参数 Laurent 族，并构造其光滑射影 crepant 紧化 $Y$，进而研究其周期与 Picard-Fuchs 系统。

Result: $X_-$ 是环面 $\mathbb{Q}$-Fano 八维中的 nef 完全交，带 index 4 的亏格一纤维化（genus-one fibration），非常一般参数下 Mordell-Weil 群为 $\mathbb{Z}/4\mathbb{Z}$。紧化 $Y$ 的 Hodge 数为 $(h^{1,1},h^{2,1})=(46,2)$，其周期满足秩 6 Picard-Fuchs 系统，含两个极大单幂零边界点，其指标代数（indicial algebra）分别同构于 $X_-$ 与 $X_+$ 的有理偶上同调环。

Conclusion: 所得的亏格零（genus-zero）镜像预测与对亏格一纤维化 $X_-\to\mathbb{P}^2$ 上 176 条竖直直线与 100 条竖直二次曲线（vertical conics）的独立计数相符，验证了该镜像构造与周期系统在非 Gorenstein 情形下的有效性。

Abstract: We construct and analyze a mirror family for the generalized complete intersection Calabi-Yau threefold $X_+=[\mathbb{P}^1\,|\,3\ {-1};\ \mathbb{P}^4\,|\,2\ 3]$, with $(h^{1,1},h^{2,1})=(2,46)$. An auxiliary-variable presentation turns the negative-degree section into a variation of toric GIT. Crossing one wall flops 16 disjoint $(-1,-1)$-curves and yields a Calabi-Yau threefold $X_-$, a nef complete intersection in a toric $\mathbb{Q}$-Fano eightfold, which carries a genus-one fibration of index 4. Because this ambient space is not Gorenstein, the Batyrev-Borisov construction does not apply. We use the Hori-Vafa equations only to select a 2-parameter Laurent family, and construct a smooth projective crepant compactification $Y$ of the Laurent model with $(h^{1,1},h^{2,1})=(46,2)$; for very general parameters the Mordell-Weil group is $\mathbb{Z}/4\mathbb{Z}$. The periods of $Y$ satisfy a rank-6 Picard-Fuchs system with two maximally unipotent boundary points, whose indicial algebras are isomorphic to the rational even cohomology rings of $X_-$ and $X_+$. The resulting genus-zero predictions agree with independent counts of 176 vertical lines and 100 vertical conics on the genus-one fibration $X_-\to\mathbb{P}^2$.

</details>

### [137] [Reply to "Comment on 'Topography of Fermi arcs in t-PtBi2 using high-resolution angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy'"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02239)
*Adam Kaminski,Paul C. Canfield,Benjamin Schrunk*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文为对 Sergey Borisenko 评论（Comment）的回应（Reply），作者基于自身原文数据否认 t-PtBi2 中存在 i-wave 对称超导能隙（superconducting gap），认为对方结论源于费米弧（Fermi arc）定位错误及能带隙与超导能隙的混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Sergey Borisenko 的评论，作者需澄清其原论文结论被误读，即费米弧为真实的费米面（Fermi surface）断开段，且未观测到超导能隙。

Method: 以原有角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）数据为依据进行再论证，并指出评论作者使用了插值图像（interpolated images）分析数据，此类图像不适合提取 meV 量级的能隙。

Result: 作者指出评论中的结论源于费米弧位置识别错误以及能带隙（band gap）与超导能隙的混淆；且相关数据局限性此前已在邮件交流中告知评论作者。

Conclusion: 作者坚称 3 K 以上不存在超导能隙的结论仍然成立，且与同一样品上近期扫描隧道显微镜（STM）测量所得 Tc = 3 K 的表面超导性一致。

Abstract: In a recent work [1] we demonstrated that the Fermi arcs in t-PtBi2 are indeed disconnected segments of the Fermi surface. We showed that the surface state band crosses EF between Weyl points and is has finite band gap at all other momenta until it merges with bulk bands. We did not find evidence of a superconducting gap in the spectra down to 3K. In recent Comment, Sergey Borisenko reanalyzed data from repository posted with our paper and concluded that a superconducting gap with i-wave symmetry is present in our data up to temperatures of 19K. We argue that this claim arises from incorrect identification of the location of Fermi arc and confusion between band gap and superconducting gap. This error was compounded by the fact that Author of the Comment analyzed data from interpolated images used for mapping the Fermi surface that are not suitable for extraction of energy gap on meV scale. This fact was previously communicated to the Author of the Comment during e-mail exchange. We maintain that our conclusion of absence of a superconducting gap above 3 K are valid and consistent with recent STM measurements performed on the same samples, which found surface superconductivity with Tc = 3K, below the temperature at which the data for our ARPES paper was measured.

</details>


### [138] [Sideband fingerprint of the Leggett mode in terahertz two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy of multiband superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02978)
*Silvia Neri,Ryo Shimano,Dirk Manske,Naoto Tsuji*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该理论工作提出利用太赫兹二维相干光谱（2DCS）直接探测多带超导体中的 Leggett 模式：有限脉宽提供的宽频谱使该模式可被共振激发，并在非线性二维谱的边带（ω_t = ω_L ± Ω）处留下指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导凝聚体的集体模式（collective modes）携带配对相互作用（pairing interaction）的基本信息，但直接探测在实验上长期困难。多带超导体中的 Leggett 模式（凝聚体间相对相位的相干振荡）虽具有拉曼活性（Raman-active），在通常情形下仅与光非线性耦合，难以直接观测。

Method: 采用理论分析，研究太赫兹二维相干光谱（terahertz two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy, 2DCS）对多带超导体的响应。核心机制在于太赫兹脉冲的有限持续时间（finite duration）提供宽频谱成分，从而无需预先知道 Leggett 模式频率 ω_L 或调节外部参数，即可通过非线性频率组合共振激发该模式。

Result: Leggett 模式在非线性二维谱中于探测频率 ω_t = ω_L ± Ω（Ω 为入射脉冲中心频率）处产生边带发射（sideband emission），构成直接指纹。该特征对温度与准粒子弛豫（quasiparticle relaxation）具有鲁棒性；扫描 Ω 可实现对 Leggett 模式的无歧义识别。

Conclusion: 太赫兹二维相干光谱可作为直接探测 Leggett 模式的可行手段，其可行性源于有限脉宽的宽谱特性，为多带超导体集体模式的实验研究提供新途径。

Abstract: Collective modes of the superconducting condensate encode fundamental information on the pairing interaction, yet their direct detection remains experimentally challenging. In multiband superconductors, the Leggett mode, i.e., a coherent oscillation of the relative phase between two condensates, is Raman-active and couples to light only nonlinearly in ordinary situations. Here we show theoretically that terahertz two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy (2DCS) provides a direct fingerprint of the Leggett mode (with frequency $ω_L$) through sideband emission at the detection frequency $ω_t = ω_L\pmΩ$ ($Ω$ is the central frequency of the incident pulses) in the nonlinear 2D spectrum. The key insight is that the finite duration of the THz pulses is the physical mechanism that makes the mode accessible: the broad spectral content of a finite pulse provides the nonlinear frequency combinations needed to resonantly excite the mode at ωL without any prior knowledge of its value or tuning of external parameters. We demonstrate robustness of this signature against temperature and quasiparticle relaxation, and show that sweeping $Ω$ allows unambiguous identification of the Leggett mode.

</details>


### [139] [Binary kagome superconducting candidates hosting topological electronic states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03003)
*Xin-Wei Yi,Jing-Yang You,Gang Su*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文对二元笼目（kagome）超导候选材料进行了系统的第一性原理筛选，覆盖 $A_mB_n$（3:1、3:2、1:1）多种结构族，得到 286 个非磁且动力学稳定的候选体系，其中 84 个估算 $T_\text{c}$ 超过 3 K、七个超过约 9 K 的现有常压笼目超导参考尺度，并预测多个体系在费米能级附近存在拓扑表面态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑超导（topological superconductivity）因与马约拉纳费米子（Majorana fermions）及量子计算相关而受到广泛关注，而笼目（kagome）材料可自然地将特殊电子结构、拓扑表面态与超导电性汇聚于相近能量窗口，因此需要系统搜寻兼具拓扑电子态与超导电性的二元笼目候选平台。

Method: 采用系统的第一性原理（first-principles）筛选流程，覆盖 $A_mB_n$（$m$:$n$ = 3:1、3:2、1:1）多个结构族，依次结合形成能与声子稳定性过滤、磁性基态搜索、电声耦合（electron-phonon-coupling）计算以及拓扑电子结构分析。

Result: 筛选出 286 个非磁、动力学稳定且进入电声耦合分析的候选体系，其中 84 个估算转变温度高于 3 K，七个超过现有常压笼目超导体的约 9 K 参考尺度；多个体系被预测在费米能级附近具有丰富的拓扑表面态。NbBi 被识别为 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 拓扑金属，具有清晰狄拉克锥（Dirac-cone）拓扑表面态及费米能级附近的笼目衍生平带；Ti$_3$Si 在本数据集中估算 $T_\text{c}=15.2$ K 为最高，呈现节线（nodal-line）衍生的鼓面（drumhead）表面态及费米能级附近经自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）后仍存活的选定律节点。

Conclusion: 所预测的二元笼目化合物将超导电性与非平庸拓扑电子结构相结合，可作为紧凑且化学可调的候选平台，用于拓扑超导及笼目相关量子现象的研究。

Abstract: Topological superconductivity has attracted broad interest because of its connection to Majorana fermions and quantum computation. Kagome materials provide a natural setting in which interesting electronic structures, topological surface states, and superconductivity may occur in nearby energy windows. Here we carry out a systematic first-principles search for binary kagome superconducting candidates with topological electronic states across several structural families $A_mB_n$ ($m$:$n$=3:1; 3:2; 1:1). The screening combines formation-energy and phonon-stability filtering, magnetic-ground-state searches, electron-phonon-coupling calculations, and topological electronic-structure analysis. We identify 286 nonmagnetic dynamically stable candidates for electron-phonon-coupling analysis, among which 84 have estimated transition temperatures above 3 K and seven exceed the $\sim 9$ K reference scale of existing ambient-pressure kagome superconductors. In addition, several systems are predicted to host abundant topological surface states near the Fermi level. NbBi is identified as a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological metal with clear Dirac-cone topological surface states and a kagome-derived flat band near $E_F$. Ti$_3$Si, with the largest estimated $T_\text{c}=15.2$ K in this dataset, shows nodal-line-derived drumhead surface states and selected nodes that survive spin-orbit coupling near $E_F$. Combining superconductivity with nontrivial topological electronic structures, the binary kagome compounds predicted here represent compact and chemically tunable candidate platforms for topological superconductivity and for exploring kagome-related quantum phenomena.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum critical superconductivity in a dense fermi dilute bose mixture close to mechanical instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03094)
*Vibhu Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在二维体系中，将金属与具有排斥性玻色-费米相互作用的未凝聚玻色气体耦合。在随机相近似（random phase approximation）框架内，玻色子的密度涨落可在相互作用适当调节下变为长程，从而将超导转变温度由 BCS 的指数形式提升为参数化线性形式 $T_c \\propto g$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 BCS 超导（BCS superconductivity）的转变温度受指数抑制 $T_c \\propto \\exp(-1/g)$，提升幅度有限。本文动机在于探索通过玻色子密度涨落作为媒介，突破该指数瓶颈以获得更高超导温度。

Method: 构建金属与未凝聚玻色气体耦合的二维模型，玻色-费米相互作用为排斥性。采用随机相近似（random phase approximation）分析玻色子密度涨落，并考察其作为有效费米-费米吸引媒介的行为随相互作用的调节。

Result: 玻色子密度涨落在相互作用适当调节时可变为长程，导致超导温度呈参数化增强 $T_c \\propto g$，显著优于 BCS 情形的 $T_c \\propto \\exp(-1/g)$。稀薄玻色气体密度成为该增强效应的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该机制在理论上可实现超导温度的参数化提升。作者进一步讨论了将这一设想在电子-激子混合物（electron-exciton mixtures）中实现所面临的概念性困难。

Abstract: We couple a metal to an uncondensed bose gas with repulsive bose-fermi interactions in two dimensions. Within the random phase approximation, we show that the density fluctuations of the bosons, which mediate the effective fermion-fermion attraction, become long ranged on appropriate tuning of interactions. This leads to parametrically enhanced superconducting temperatures $T_c \propto g$ as opposed to $T_c \propto \exp(-1/g)$ for the BCS case. The dilute bose gas density acts as the primary bottleneck to the enhancement. We discuss the conceptual difficulties of implementing this idea in electron-exciton mixtures.

</details>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Spectral Crossings Diagnose Multiply Quantized Vortex Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02234)
*Jongheon Baek,Hiroyuki Tajima,Gihwan Nam*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在双组分 Haldane 球（Haldane-sphere）模型中，通过拉曼场（Raman field）耦合研究电荷-r 涡旋（charge-r vortex）在弱对称破缺后保持为融合零模还是分裂为 r 个近邻单位电荷零点的问题。研究表明，缺陷支路穿过零能处的失谐（detuning）$\\tau_c$ 对融合零满足 $|\\tau_c|\\sim N^{-(r+1)}$，而对分辨的单位零点为 $N^{-2}$，从而以谱学方式区分两者并可推断分裂团簇尺度 d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱对称破缺后，电荷-r 涡旋可保持为一个融合的 order-r 零模，或分裂为 r 个近邻单位电荷零点，而两种构型具有相同的总缠绕数（total winding number）和相同的最低朗道能级（lowest-Landau-level）投影暗态数目，因此轨道角动量与总缠绕数均无法区分其局部几何。

Method: 构建由拉曼场耦合的双组分 Haldane 球模型，对单极磁通（monopole flux）N 进行分析；采用 Feshbach 约化（Feshbach reduction）为完整多带哈密顿量（multiband Hamiltonian）推导渐近规律，并在单极-谐振子基（monopole-harmonic basis）中进行数值计算。

Result: 缺陷支路穿过零能处的失谐满足 $|\\tau_c|\\sim N^{-(r+1)}$（融合零）与 $N^{-2}$（分辨的单位零点）；数值计算在 r=1,2,3 时证实了融合涡旋的标度规律；对直径为 d 的部分分裂团簇，当 d 与磁长度（magnetic length）$\\ell_B$ 可比时标度发生交叉（crossover）。

Conclusion: 由失谐标度指数可区分融合零与分裂单位零点；当分裂团簇直径 d 与磁长度 $\\ell_B$ 可比时标度交叉，使 d 可在低于直接成像空间分辨率的条件下通过谱学推断。

Abstract: A charge-$r$ coupling vortex can remain as one fused order-$r$ zero or split into $r$ nearby unit-charge zeros after weak symmetry breaking. The two configurations have the same total winding and the same number of dark states in the lowest-Landau-level projection, so neither the imposed orbital angular momentum nor the total winding number distinguishes their local geometry. We study this distinction in a two-component Haldane-sphere model coupled by a Raman field. For monopole flux $N$, the detuning $τ_{\rm c}$ at which a defect branch crosses zero energy scales as $|τ_{\rm c}|\sim N^{-(r+1)}$ for a fused zero and $N^{-2}$ for resolved unit zeros. A Feshbach reduction establishes these asymptotic laws for the full multiband Hamiltonian, and numerical calculations in a monopole-harmonic basis confirm the fused-vortex scaling for $r=1,2,3$. For a partially split cluster of diameter $d$, the scaling crosses over when $d$ is comparable to the magnetic length $\ell_B$, allowing $d$ to be inferred spectroscopically even below the spatial resolution of direct imaging.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Rank-One HCIZ Integrals and Multiplicative Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02892)
*Benoît Collins,Nicolas Delporte,Manasa Nagatsu,Reiko Toriumi*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究大维数秩一 HCIZ 积分中谱平均与对数的相互作用：对 $(BA)^*(BA)\otimes\mathbf{1}_N$ 的积分，先取对数再平均得到自由乘法卷积，先平均再取对数得到经典乘法卷积，二者在 $N^2$ 尺度上给出两种极限描述。文章同时给出退火积分与 $(A^*A)\otimes(B^*B)$ 积分的精确有限维等式，并构造反例表明对 $k\geq3$ 个因子的类似等式即使渐近也可能失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清 HCIZ 积分中"谱平均（spectral averaging）"与"对数"两种操作的次序效应，即自由概率论（free probability theory）中的自由乘法卷积（free multiplicative convolution）与经典乘法卷积（classical multiplicative convolution）如何从同一类积分在不同极限尺度下涌现。同时考察该等价关系能否推广到多因子张量积情形。

Method: 设定 $A,B$ 为独立双酉不变矩阵（bi-unitarily invariant matrices），其确定奇异值一致有界，且其 Gram 矩阵的经验谱测度（empirical spectral measures）弱收敛。针对 $(BA)^*(BA)\otimes\mathbf{1}_N$ 在小实参数下以 $N^2$ 速度分析秩一 HCIZ 积分的两种极限描述，并证明其与 $(A^*A)\otimes(B^*B)$ 对应积分之间的精确有限维等式；进而对 $k\geq3$ 个因子的张量积，分别取全局 Haar 向量（速度 $N^k$）与乘积态向量（速度 $N$）进行比较。

Result: 对小实参数，退火积分（annealed integral）取对数后给出自由乘法卷积，而先平均后取对数给出经典乘法卷积，两者速度均为 $N^2$；退火积分与 $(A^*A)\otimes(B^*B)$ 积分之间存在精确有限维等式。但对每个固定因子数 $k\geq3$，构造的反例表明该类似等式即使渐近也可能失效。对任意固定 $k$，全局 Haar 向量（速度 $N^k$）与乘积态向量（速度 $N$）在相应假设下分别给出经典与自由乘法卷积。此外，文中导出了 $S$-变换（$S$-transform）的已知乘法性（multiplicativity）。

Conclusion: HCIZ 积分中平均与取对数的次序决定了极限对象是自由乘法卷积还是经典乘法卷积，且两种描述可在同一 $N^2$ 尺度上并存。两因子情形的精确有限维等式不能自然推广至 $k\geq3$ 个因子；多因子张量积中的经典/自由卷积则分别由全局 Haar 向量与乘积态向量在速度 $N^k$ 与 $N$ 下实现。结果同时给出 $S$-变换乘法性的一个新导出途径。

Abstract: We study how spectral averaging interacts with the logarithm in large-dimensional rank-one Harish-Chandra--Itzykson--Zuber (HCIZ) integrals. For independent bi-unitarily invariant matrices $A,B$ with uniformly bounded deterministic singular values and weakly convergent empirical spectral measures of their Gram matrices, the integral for $(BA)^*(BA)\otimes\mathbf{1}_N$ has two limiting descriptions at speed $N^2$ for small real parameters: averaging its logarithm gives free multiplicative convolution, whereas taking the logarithm after averaging gives classical multiplicative convolution. We also prove an exact finite-dimensional equality between this annealed integral and the one for $(A^*A)\otimes(B^*B)$. For every fixed number of factors $k\geq3$, we construct counterexamples showing that the analogous equality can fail even asymptotically.
  For tensor products with any fixed number $k$ of factors, global Haar vectors at speed $N^k$ and product-state vectors at speed $N$ yield classical and free multiplicative convolution, respectively, for small real parameters under the corresponding hypotheses.
  We also derive the known multiplicativity of the $S$-transform.

</details>


### [143] [Fractal dimension of critical Gaussian free field sign clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03703)
*Alexander Drewitz,Alexis Prévost,Pierre-François Rodriguez*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文研究低维（低于平均场机制）瞬态加权图上高斯自由场（Gaussian free field）的度量图符号簇（metric graph sign clusters），证明多种宏观簇定义（直径、体积、容量泛函）在宏观尺度上等价；据此在 εZ^d（d=3,4,5）临界簇的小网格尺度极限中，验证 Werner 猜想：极限集的 Hausdorff 与 Minkowski 维数均为 1+d/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低于平均场机制的低维瞬态图上，宏观符号簇（macroscopic sign clusters）的刻画尚缺乏统一框架：直径、体积、容量等不同泛函定义的宏观簇是否一致并不清楚，而这直接关系到临界簇尺度极限（scaling limit）的几何性质与维数猜想。该研究旨在建立宏观等价性并回答 Werner 关于极限集维数的猜想。

Method: 研究对象为一大类瞬态加权图 G 上高斯自由场的度量图符号簇，聚焦足够低维、即低于平均场机制的情形，证明基于直径、体积或容量泛函的多种宏观簇定义在宏观意义下相互等价；并将结论应用于 εZ^d（d=3,4,5）临界簇当 ε↓0 时的小网格尺度极限，结合环汤（loop soup）图像与两点穿越估计（two-point crossing estimates）进行分析。

Result: 证明宏观等价性成立，并推出低维下宏观簇可由所含大环（large loops）在环汤图像中刻画，与高维典型行为形成对比。对 εZ^d（d=3,4,5）的每个子列尺度极限，证明其为具正 Brownian 容量（Brownian capacity）的集合，且 Hausdorff 维数与 Minkowski 维数均等于 1+d/2，验证了 Werner 的猜想；同时得到极限中的两点穿越估计，并证明每个极限环汤簇是其包含的宏观环尺度极限之并的闭包。

Conclusion: 维数的确定并非源自对极限对象（如 SLE_6）的直接计算，而是来自离散模型本身区别于平面 Bernoulli 渗流（planar Bernoulli percolation）等模型的独特性质，凸显低维离散结构对极限几何的决定性作用。

Abstract: We study metric graph sign clusters of the Gaussian free field on a large class of transient weighted graphs $G$ that are sufficiently low-dimensional, i.e. below the mean-field regime, and prove a macroscopic equivalence property: several natural ways to define macroscopic clusters - involving any of diameter, volume, or capacity functionals - are in fact the same. As a corollary, macroscopic clusters are characterized in low dimensions as those containing large loops in the corresponding loop soup picture, in contrast with the typical behavior in high dimensions. We then apply these results to the small-mesh scaling limit of critical clusters on $\varepsilon\mathbb{Z}^d$, $d=3,4,5$, as $\varepsilon \downarrow 0$. We prove that every subsequential scaling limit consists of sets with positive Brownian capacity whose Hausdorff and Minkowski dimensions are both equal to $1+\tfrac d2$, as conjectured by Werner. We also derive two-point crossing estimates in the limit, and show that each limiting loop soup cluster is the closure of the union of the scaling limits of the macroscopic loops it contains. Remarkably, unlike with planar Bernoulli percolation for instance, the determination of the Hausdorff dimension does not emerge from direct calculations involving a limiting object (such as $\text{SLE}_6$), but rather from the distinctive features of the discrete model itself.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Structural crossover of complex networks: bridging degree correlation and fractality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02883)
*Shogo Mizutaka,Jun Yamamoto,Kousuke Yakubo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究探讨无标度随机网络（scale-free random networks）中长程度关联（long-range degree correlations）与分形性（fractality）的关系，通过在渗流阈值（percolation threshold）处推导依赖度的球体积表达式，预测了一种结构交叉（structural crossover）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是厘清无标度随机网络中长程度关联与分形结构之间的关联，这一问题在临界网络几何刻画中具有基础意义。

Method: 在巨连通分量（giant component）涌现的渗流阈值处分析度关联，推导以度为 k 的根节点为中心、距离 l 内的平均节点数，即球体积（ball volume）\(\tilde{\nu}_k(l)\)，并借助临界分支过程（critical branching processes）进行解释。

Result: 所得表达式预测了依赖度的结构交叉：当 \(l\ll k^{1/(d_f-1)}\) 时，\(\tilde{\nu}_k(l)\sim kl\)；当 \(l\gg k^{1/(d_f-1)}\) 时，过渡为全局分形标度 \(\tilde{\nu}_k(l)\sim l^{d_f}\)，其中 \(d_f\) 为临界无标度随机网络的分形维数（fractal dimension）。该交叉亦在经验分形网络（empirical fractal networks）中被观测到。

Conclusion: 长程度关联与分形性在临界无标度随机网络中通过度依赖的结构交叉相联系，该交叉可由临界分支过程自然解释，并在实际分形网络中具现。

Abstract: We investigate the relationship between long-range degree correlations and fractality in scale-free random networks. By analyzing degree correlations in scale-free random networks at the percolation threshold, at which a giant component emerges, we derive the ball volume $\tildeν_k(l)$, defined as the average number of nodes within distance $l$ from a root node with degree $k$. The resulting expression predicts a degree-dependent structural crossover. For $l\ll k^{1/(\df-1)}$, the ball volume grows as $\tildeν_k(l)\sim kl$, whereas for $l\gg k^{1/(\df-1)}$, it crosses over to the global fractal scaling $\tildeν_k(l)\sim l^{\df}$. Here, $\df$ is the fractal dimension of critical scale-free random networks. We further show that the structural crossover can be naturally understood in terms of critical branching processes and is also observed in empirical fractal networks.

</details>


### [145] [Probing fluctuating hydrodynamics through current fluctuations in hard rod gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03180)
*Tirthankar Mondal,Indranil Mukherjee,Anupam Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维硬棒气体（hard-rod gas）平衡态下积分电流的全计数统计（full counting statistics），比较弹道宏观涨落理论（BMFT）与基于关联布朗运动近似的扩散涨落流体力学理论（CBFHD）两种涨落流体力学框架，并与微观动力学数值模拟对照。结果表明BMFT的前几阶累积量与速率函数与模拟高度吻合，而CBFHD不能准确刻画大尺度电流涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望检验近期提出的两种涨落流体力学框架在描述硬棒气体电流涨落时的适用性与准确性。核心问题是：这些宏观理论能否与底层微观动力学在全计数统计层面定量一致，以及不同框架之间预测有何差异。

Method: 采用两种涨落流体力学方法：弹道宏观涨落理论（Ballistic Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory, BMFT）与基于硬棒准粒子关联布朗运动近似的扩散涨落流体力学理论（CBFHD）。在BMFT中推导弹道标度下的大偏差形式及其显式大偏差函数（适用于点粒子坐标下可分解的任意非均匀初始态）并获得电流累积量；在CBFHD中显式纳入随机噪声与耗散项分析电流统计。结果与微观硬棒动力学的直接数值模拟及一组关联布朗运动模型进行对照验证。

Result: BMFT预测电流分布在弹道标度下满足大偏差原理，并给出显式大偏差函数与相应电流累积量；CBFHD框架下电流分布同样服从大偏差原理。对照显示BMFT对前几阶累积量与速率函数的预测与微观硬棒动力学数值模拟高度一致，而CBFHD的预测不一致；通过一组关联布朗运动独立验证了CBFHD的预测。

Conclusion: CBFHD无法准确刻画硬棒气体的大尺度电流涨落；BMFT则至少在全计数统计语境下与微观动力学定量吻合。研究支持在硬棒气体电流涨落问题中采用BMFT而非CBFHD，并凸显不同涨落流体力学框架在描述大尺度涨落上的适用性差异。

Abstract: We study the full counting statistics of the integrated current in a one-dimensional hard-rod gas in equilibrium, employing two recently proposed fluctuating hydrodynamic frameworks: (i) Ballistic Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (BMFT) and (ii) diffusive fluctuating hydrodynamic theory based on correlated Brownian motion approximation of hard-rod quasiparticles (CBFHD). We investigate how the predictions of these two approaches compare with each other and with the underlying microscopic dynamics. We show within BMFT that the current distribution admits a large-deviation form under ballistic scaling, and we derive an explicit expression for the large-deviation function, valid for an arbitrary inhomogeneous initial state factorized in point-particle coordinates. The corresponding current cumulants are also obtained. We subsequently analyze the current statistics within the CBFHD framework, which explicitly incorporates stochastic noise and dissipative terms, and demonstrate that the current distribution likewise obeys a large-deviation principle in this approach. We find that, while BMFT predictions for the first few cumulants and the rate function are in excellent agreement with direct numerical simulations of the microscopic hard-rod dynamics, CBFHD prediction is not. We further verify the predictions of the CBFHD independently using a collection of correlated Brownian motions, which provides an approximate description of the dynamics of hard-rod quasiparticles. Our findings indicate that CBFHD does not accurately capture large-scale current fluctuations in the hard-rod gas, whereas BMFT yields predictions in quantitative agreement with the microscopic dynamics, at least in the context of full counting statistics.

</details>


### [146] [Functional renormalization group in an external field: constant field versus constant order parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03365)
*Julia von Rothkirch,Andreas Rückriegel,Peter Kopietz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作将固定外场与固定共轭序参量两类边界条件纳入泛函重整化群框架，比较了φ^4理论中相应流方程，并探讨弹性对Ising临界性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部场作用下系统的重整化群流可通过固定场或固定共轭序参量两种方式导出，但如何在泛函重整化群方法中实现这些边界条件尚需明确。

Method: 在泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group）框架内实现上述两类边界条件，针对φ^4理论推导并比较相应的流方程（flow equations）。

Result: 在Ising不动点附近，与相关三点顶点（three-point vertex）线性化流相关的部分标度变量与标度指数（scaling exponents）依赖于所选边界条件；同时讨论了弹性（elasticity）对Ising临界性的影响，发现在恒定应力下进行重整化群时，恒应变情形下得到的重整化Ising不动点与球面不动点（spherical fixed point）被移至参数空间中物理上不可及的区域。

Conclusion: 边界条件的选取会影响临近Ising不动点的标度性质；弹性耦合下，不同力学边界条件（恒应变与恒应力）导致不动点在参数空间中的可达性发生改变。

Abstract: Renormalization group (RG) flows for systems subject to an external field can be derived either by fixing the field or by fixing the conjugate order parameter. We show how to implement these boundary conditions within the functional renormalization group approach. We derive and compare the corresponding flow equations for $φ^4$ theory and show that some scaling variables and scaling exponents associated with the linearized flow of the relevant three-point vertex in the vicinity of the Ising fixed point depend on the boundary conditions. We also discuss the effect of elasticity on Ising criticality. In this case two of the fixed points (namely the renormalized Ising fixed point and the spherical fixed point) obtained for constant strain are shifted to physically inaccessible regimes of parameter space when we perform the RG at constant stress.

</details>

Uchida,Y. Uchiyama,S. Ueda,K. Ueno,Y. Uesaka,D. Vasileuskaya,E. Velicheva,M. L. Wong,T. S. Wong,C. Wu,T. Xing,Y. Xu,C. Yamada,H. Yamaguchi,M. Yamanaka,Y. Yang,W. Yao,B. Yeo,H. Yoshida,M. Yoshida,M. Yoshii,D. Yoshikawa,T. Yoshioka,Y. Yuan,Yu. V. Yudin,N. Yussup,I. Yusuff,J. Zhang,Y. Zhang,Z. Zhang,Y. Zhou,K. Zuber*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: COMET Phase-α（COMET 第一阶段）于 2023 年完成首次束流运行，验证了低动量负缪子（low-momentum negative muon）经传输螺线管（Transport Solenoid, TS）输送至实验区，并以 Range Counter 测量其停止缪子数量、动量谱与横向束剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该实验是 J-PARC 上 COMET 缪子-电子转换（muon-to-electron conversion）项目的初始束流运行阶段，目的在于调试脉冲化慢引出 8 GeV 质子束与缪子束线，并从实验上证明低动量负缪子能被输送至实验区。

Method: 使用专用探测器 Range Counter，通过探测缪子在铜吸收体（copper absorber）中停止后发射的延迟电子来鉴别停止的负缪子；由测得的衰变时间谱（decay-time spectra）重建停止缪子数、相应动量谱与横向束剖面，并与基于 Geant4 的模拟进行对比。

Result: 观察到停止缪子成分，证明慢速负缪子已经由 TS 输送至实验区；重建动量谱显示束流集中于低动量区，横向剖面不均匀，与弯曲螺线管磁场（curved solenoidal magnetic field）中的电荷漂移定性一致。Geant4 模拟可复现对降能片厚度与探测器位置的整体依赖关系，但实测产额系统性偏高约 30%。

Conclusion: 该结果为 COMET 中低动量负缪子输运提供了首个实验基准（experimental benchmark），并为后续束流表征与产生研究奠定基础。

Abstract: The COMET Phase-$α$ experiment, the initial beam-operation stage of the COMET muon-to-electron conversion programme at J-PARC, took place in 2023 and marked the first beam operation at the COMET facility. It served to commission the delivery of a bunched, slow-extracted 8 GeV proton beam and the muon beamline. Its objective was to demonstrate experimentally that low-momentum negative muons could be transported to the experimental area through the Transport Solenoid (TS). We report a measurement of the transported muon beam using the Range Counter, a dedicated detector that identifies stopped negative muons by detecting delayed electrons emitted after the muons stop in a copper absorber. From the measured decay-time spectra, we reconstruct the number of stopped muons, the corresponding momentum spectrum, and the transverse beam profile. The observation of the stopped-muon component demonstrates that slow negative muons were transported through the TS to the experimental area. The reconstructed momentum spectrum indicates that the transported beam is concentrated in the low-momentum region. The non-uniform transverse profile is qualitatively consistent with charge drift in the curved solenoidal magnetic field. A Geant4-based simulation reproduces the overall dependence on degrader thickness and detector position; the measured yields are systematically higher by roughly 30%. These results provide the first experimental benchmark of low-momentum negative-muon transport in COMET and establish the basis for future beam characterisation and production studies.

</details>


### [148] [Entanglement-sensitive observables in $e^+e^- \to τ^+τ^-$ at STCF: a detector-level feasibility study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03024)
*Chentao Bao,Xi Tao,Hai Chen,Lailin Xu,Xiaorong Zhou,Mingyi Liu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究基于STCF上$\sqrt{s}=7$ GeV的$e^+e^-\to\tau^+\tau^-$过程，利用$\rho\rho$与$\pi\rho$衰变道开展探测器级模拟分析，评估通过$\tau$衰变产物测量$τ^+τ^-$量子纠缠（quantum entanglement）与非经典自旋关联的可行性，并给出符合度（concurrence）与Bell型变量的预期精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\tau^+\tau^-$产生中的量子纠缠为相对论量子系统中的非经典自旋关联提供了直接探针，而$\tau$衰变产物可充当自旋分析器（spin analyzer）。研究旨在检验在STCF实验条件下于探测器层面实施此类测量的可行性。

Method: 对信号与主要本底过程采用完整STCF探测器与重建框架进行模拟；针对$\rho\rho$与$\pi\rho$两个衰变道设计专门的重建与事例筛选流程；由重建后的衰变运动学推断$τ^+τ^-$自旋密度矩阵（spin-density matrix），并在选定的基准区域计算符合度$C[\rho]$与Bell敏感量$m_{12}[C]$。

Result: 整体信号效率约为$4.5\%$（$\rho\rho$）与$4.8\%$（$\pi\rho$），纯度分别约为$87.6\%$与$93.2\%$。在$|\cos\theta|<0.2$且积分亮度为$1~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$的条件下，$C[\rho]$的相对精度在两个道中均约为$2.2\%$；$m_{12}[C]$的相对精度约为$1.4\%$（$\rho\rho$）与$1.5\%$（$\pi\rho$），已包含统计与探测器分辨不确定度。

Conclusion: 重建后的$m_{12}[C]$在$\rho\rho$道中与参考阈值的分离更大，表明该拓扑对Bell型自旋关联尤为敏感。结果表明在STCF上于强子$\tau$衰变中探测量子纠缠与非经典自旋关联在探测器层面是可行的。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement in $τ^+τ^-$ production provides a direct probe of non-classical spin correlations in a relativistic quantum system, with the $τ$ decay products serving as spin analyzers. We investigate the detector-level feasibility of such measurements in $e^+e^-\toτ^+τ^-$ at the Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF) at $\sqrt{s}=7$ GeV, using the $ρρ$ and $πρ$ decay channels. Signal and dominant background processes are simulated with the full STCF detector and reconstruction framework. Dedicated reconstruction and event-selection procedures yield overall signal efficiencies of approximately $4.5\%$ and $4.8\%$, with corresponding purities of about $87.6\%$ and $93.2\%$ for the $ρρ$ and $πρ$ channels, respectively. From the reconstructed decay kinematics, the $τ^+τ^-$ spin-density matrix is inferred and the concurrence $C[ρ]$ together with the Bell-sensitive quantity $m_{12}[C]$ are evaluated in a selected fiducial region. For the benchmark requirement $|\cosθ|<0.2$ and an integrated luminosity of $1~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, the projected relative precision on $C[ρ]$ is about $2.2\%$ in both channels, while that on $m_{12}[C]$ is approximately $1.4\%$ for $ρρ$ and $1.5\%$ for $πρ$, including statistical and detector-resolution uncertainties. The reconstructed $m_{12}[C]$ shows a larger separation from its reference threshold in the $ρρ$ channel, making this topology particularly sensitive to Bell-type spin correlations. These results demonstrate the detector-level feasibility of probing quantum entanglement and non-classical spin correlations in hadronic $τ$ decays at STCF.

</details>

Girgus,P. Govindaraj,M. Posiadala-Zezula,Y. S. Prabhu,S. B. Boyd,R. Edwards,D. Hadley,M. O'Flaherty,B. Richards,A. Ali,B. Jamieson,C. Bronner,D. Horiguchi,A. Minamino,Y. Sasaki*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Super-Kamiokande 在 SK-Gd 阶段通过钆（gadolinium）中子捕获提高对低能电子反中微子的探测灵敏度，基于 411.52 天有效曝光量的反应堆反中微子数据，以约 6σ 显著性确认反应堆反中微子信号，并测量太阳中微子振荡参数，与太阳中微子数据联合拟合得到更精确的 θ12 与 Δm21²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2020 年 Super-Kamiokande 进入 SK-Gd 阶段，借由探测溶于探测器水中的钆（gadolinium）所捕获的中子，提升对低能电子反中微子（electron antineutrino）的灵敏度，从而开展反应堆反中微子数据的振荡分析。

Method: 使用 SK-Gd 数据对反应堆电子反中微子（reactor electron antineutrino）进行分析，总有效曝光量为 411.52 live days，并在扣除偶然、地球中微子（geoneutrino）与散裂（spallation）本底后，对观测的瞬发能谱与月事件率进行检验。

Result: 观测的瞬发能谱与月事件率在扣除本底后与振荡后的反应堆反中微子预期一致；无反应堆假设被否定，信号显著性约为 6σ。测得振荡参数 sin²θ12 = 0.500±0.155、Δm21² = (9.08_{-0.54}^{+0.55})×10^{-5} eV²；与 Super-Kamiokande 太阳中微子测量结果在 1.9σ 水平上统计相容。

Conclusion: Super-Kamiokande 中太阳与反应堆数据的联合拟合给出最佳拟合值 sin²θ12 = 0.332_{-0.026}^{+0.028}、Δm21² = (8.94_{-0.47}^{+0.46})×10^{-5} eV²。

Abstract: In 2020, Super-Kamiokande entered the SK-Gd phase, improving its sensitivity to low-energy electron antineutrinos through the detection of neutrons captured on gadolinium dissolved in the detector water. An analysis of reactor electron antineutrino data has been carried out using data from SK-Gd, with a total exposure of 411.52 live days. The observed prompt energy spectra and monthly event rates are consistent with the oscillated reactor antineutrino prediction after accounting for accidental, geoneutrino, and spallation backgrounds. The no-reactor hypothesis is disfavored with a reactor-antineutrino signal significance of approximately $6σ$. The oscillation parameters are measured to be $\sin^2θ_{12} = 0.500\pm0.155$ and $Δm^2_{21} = (9.08_{-0.54}^{+0.55}) \times 10^{-5} \mathrm{eV}^2$. A comparison with Super-Kamiokande solar neutrino measurements shows a statistical compatibility between the two datasets at the $1.9σ$ level. A combined solar and reactor fit in Super-Kamiokande yields best-fit values of $\sin^2θ_{12} = 0.332_{-0.026}^{+0.028}$ and $Δm^2_{21} = (8.94_{-0.47}^{+0.46}) \times 10^{-5} \mathrm{eV}^2$.

</details>


### [150] [Track fitting as a language translation problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03339)
*Deepak Samuel,Christy Elsa Koshy*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该探索性研究将粒子径迹重建（particle tracking）重新表述为语言翻译问题，使用仅解码器（decoder-only）的 Transformer 架构与自定义分词器（tokenizer），把探测器语言翻译为物理语言，并在合成数据与真实宇宙缪子（cosmic-muon）数据上验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粒子物理分析依赖收集单个探测器击中（detector hits）、重组为径迹并拟合模型函数，流程相对繁琐。鉴于大语言模型（LLMs）在理解语言语法与语义方面的能力，作者希望探索能否借用这一范式改进径迹重建。

Method: 将粒子径迹重建概念化为语言翻译问题，把探测器语言翻译为物理语言；为此设计了一个仅解码器（decoder-only）架构并配备自定义分词器（tokenizer），随后在合成数据集上评估径迹参数精度与多径迹事件表达能力，并在真实宇宙缪子数据上测试。

Result: 在合成数据集上，模型在径迹参数精度方面比简单回归技术高一个数量级，与随机抽样一致（RANSAC）方法相当；多径迹事件识别准确率达98%。真实宇宙缪子事件分析中，天顶角（zenith angle）分布与预期吻合良好。

Conclusion: 结果表明紧凑语言模型具有竞争力，并天然具备表达多径迹事件的能力；但要理解该技术的全部潜力，仍需针对复杂几何结构开展进一步研究。

Abstract: Particle physics analysis depends on information retrieved from particle tracks in a detector. Traditionally, this involves collecting individual detector hits, recombining them to form tracks, and fitting them to a model function. Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in comprehending the grammar and semantics of languages. Our exploratory study leverages transformers to conceptualise particle tracking as a language translation problem, wherein the language of detectors is translated into the language of physics. A simple decoder-only architecture with a custom tokenizer was designed for this task, and the behaviour of the model in this atypical setting provided interesting insights. We evaluated the advantages of this formulation in terms of track-parameter accuracy, and the ease of representing multi-track events, and finally tested the model on real cosmic-muon data. On synthetic datasets, the model outperformed a simple regression technique by an order of magnitude in track-parameter accuracy while being on par with the random sample consensus (RANSAC) method. The model also identified multi-track events with an accuracy of 98\%. The analysis of real cosmic-muon events showed an excellent agreement of the zenith angle distribution with expectations. Our results suggest that a compact language model shows competitive performance and a natural ability to represent multi-track events. Further studies with complex geometries are required to understand the full potential of this technique.

</details>


### [151] [Search for pair-produced vector-like top quarks decaying into Lorentz-boosted top quarks and scalar bosons in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03489)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS 实验在 13 TeV 质子-质子对撞数据中搜寻成对产生的矢量类顶夸克（vector-like top quark, T），其衰变产物为标量玻色子（scalar boson）φ 与标准模型（standard model, SM）顶夸克；未观测到显著超出，给出截面与分支比乘积的 95% 置信水平上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量类夸克（vector-like quark）是超出标准模型物理的常见预言粒子，由 T 衰变引入新标量玻色子 φ 的通道可解释双光子（photon pair）末态，此前需通过数据检验其存在性。该搜索针对顶夸克高倍增（boosted）拓扑，扩展对 T 与 φ 质量参数的覆盖。

Method: 基于 CMS 探测器在 CERN LHC 于 2016–2018 年采集的 √s = 13 TeV 质子-质子对撞数据，积分亮度（integrated luminosity）为 138 fb⁻¹。选取含源自标量玻色子衰变的光子对、并伴随 SM 顶夸克对全强子（all-hadronic）衰变的事例，针对高倍顶夸克信号将顶夸克衰变产物重建为大半径喷注（large-radius jet）。对一组 T 与 φ 质量点进行扫描。

Result: 观测数据与预期本底（background）无显著偏离。最大局域超出（local excess）显著性为 2.7 个标准差，考虑多重比较效应（look-elsewhere effect）后降至 1.5 个标准差。

Conclusion: 对 T T̄ 产生截面与分支比（branching fraction）乘积设定 95% 置信水平（confidence level）上限。

Abstract: A search for pair-produced vector-like top quarks, T, decaying into a new scalar boson $φ$ and a standard model (SM) top quark is presented. The search targets events containing a photon pair originating from the decay of one of the scalar bosons, accompanied by all-hadronic decays of the SM top quark pair. The analysis is optimized for boosted top quark signatures in which the decay products of the top quarks are reconstructed as large-radius jets. The search is based on proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, collected by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC during 2016$-$2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The search is performed for an array of T and $φ$ masses. No significant deviation from the predicted background is observed. The largest local excess has a significance of 2.7 standard deviations, reduced to 1.5 standard deviations after accounting for the look-elsewhere effect. Upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the product of $\mathrm{T\bar{T}}$ production cross section and the branching fraction.

</details>

### [152] [Is DESI Seeing Dynamical Dark Energy, or a Cosmic Glitch in Gravity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40176)
*Robin Y. Wen,Lukas Tobias Hergt,Niayesh Afshordi,Douglas Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillation, BAO）与宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）联合数据已开始轻微倾向于偏离标准宇宙学模型（ΛCDM）。相较通常的两参数动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy）框架，一个单参数"引力宇宙缺陷（cosmic glitch in gravity）"模型——其宇宙学引力耦合区别于牛顿常数，类似 Hořava 引力——拟合效果至少相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合 BAO 与 CMB 数据对 ΛCDM 的偏离迹象通常被解释为两参数动力学暗能量，但该框架并非唯一或最简解释；因此有必要考察更少参数的替代模型能否同样甚至更好地拟合观测。

Method: 采用单参数"引力宇宙缺陷"模型，其中宇宙学引力耦合（cosmological gravitational coupling）不同于牛顿常数（Newton's constant），形式类比 Hořava 引力；并使用 Planck、ACT、SPT 及 DESI DR2 数据对模型进行拟合与检验，附加 CMB 透镜（CMB lensing）与超新星（supernovae）数据以评估稳健性。

Result: 由 Planck+ACT+SPT 与 DESI DR2 得到 G_cosmo/G_N = 0.9920 ± 0.0025，即以 3.3σ 的显著性偏好较弱的宇宙学引力；该结果在加入 CMB 透镜和超新星数据后依然稳健。此外，仅用 CMB 拟合的缺陷模型能够预测 DESI 距离，而 w0waCDM 则不能。

Conclusion: 单参数引力宇宙缺陷模型在拟合质量上不劣于两参数动力学暗能量解释，且具备更强的预测能力（由 CMB 预测 DESI 距离），表明其可能是当前数据所偏好的更简宇宙学描述之一。

Abstract: Combined baryon acoustic oscillation and cosmic microwave background data have begun to mildly favor departures from $Λ\mathrm{CDM}$. This is usually framed as 2-parameter dynamical dark energy. However, a 1-parameter "cosmic glitch in gravity" model, whose cosmological gravitational coupling differs from Newton's constant as in Hořava gravity, fits at least as well. $\textit{Planck}$+ACT+SPT and DESI~DR2 give $G_\mathrm{cosmo}/G_\mathrm{N}=0.9920\pm0.0025$, a $3.3\,σ$ preference for weaker cosmological gravity, robust to adding CMB lensing and supernovae. Fitted to the CMB alone, the glitch predicts DESI distances; $w_0w_a$CDM does not.

</details>


### [153] [Distinguishing the origin of cosmic birefringence: dark energy, dark matter, and neutrino asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02667)
*Lu Yin,Eiichiro Komatsu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文计算了由中微子不对称电流（neutrino-asymmetry current）产生的宇宙双折射（cosmic birefringence）所导致的 EB 功率谱（EB power spectrum），并在统一的 Chern–Simons 框架下与轴子暗能量（axion dark energy）及轴子暗物质（axion dark matter）进行对比，指出其低多极矩抑制特征可区分不同物理起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一偏振源（polarized source）的净旋转角难以揭示宇宙双折射的物理起源，而不同红移 z 的源可用于区分不同机制。大尺度与小尺度上宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）的 E、B 模式偏振奇偶敏感关联分别探测 z≃10 与 z≃1100 的源，因此 EB 功率谱的形状对双折射源的红移演化敏感。

Method: 在统一的 Chern–Simons 框架下计算由中微子不对称电流产生的 EB 功率谱，并与轴子暗能量和轴子暗物质情形进行比较。

Result: 中微子不对称导致的 EB 功率谱在低多极矩 ℓ≲20 处受到抑制，与暗物质情形类似，而暗能量情形无此抑制。复合时期（recombination）中微子密度的演化会改变高多极矩处 EB 功率谱的形状。

Conclusion: 包括 LiteBIRD 在内的大尺度偏振测量可区分晚期暗能量双折射与由轴子暗物质或中微子不对称在更高红移产生的双折射；而地面实验（如 Simons Observatory）可利用高多极矩处的特征区分中微子与轴子暗物质解释。

Abstract: Cosmic birefringence, the isotropic rotation of the plane of linear polarization of photons from sources at cosmological distances, can be generated by different parity-violating mechanisms. While the net rotation angle of a single polarized source carries little information about its physical origin, sources at various redshifts $z$ can be used to distinguish between different origins. Parity-sensitive correlations of the $E$- and $B$-mode polarization fields of the cosmic microwave background measured on large and small angular scales probe sources at $z\simeq 10$ and $1100$, respectively. Thus, the detailed shape of the $EB$ power spectrum is sensitive to the redshift evolution of the birefringence source. In this paper, we calculate the $EB$ power spectrum due to a neutrino-asymmetry current, and compare it to axion dark energy and dark matter using a common Chern--Simons framework. We find that the $EB$ power spectrum due to a neutrino asymmetry is suppressed at low multipoles, $\ell\lesssim 20$, similar to the dark matter case. This can be clearly distinguished from the dark energy case, which has no such suppression. Large-scale polarization measurements, including \textit{LiteBIRD}, can therefore distinguish late-time dark energy birefringence from that generated at higher redshifts by axion dark matter or neutrino asymmetry. We also find that the evolution of the neutrino density during recombination alters the shape of the $EB$ power spectrum at high multipoles. This signature can be used to distinguish between the neutrino and axion dark matter interpretations, using ground-based experiments such as the Simons Observatory.

</details>

### [154] [Fracture under body forces: An accessible test and analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03404)
*Farhad Kamarei,Sarah Bueno De Castro,Jeffery R. Roesler,Oscar Lopez-Pamies*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简易台式（benchtop）自重力断裂（self-weight fracture）实验方法，利用带向下 V 形缺口（V-notch）的悬臂梁（cantilever beam）在自重作用下实现裂纹萌生与扩展，从而绕开离心机（centrifuge）等大型设施，并在 3D 打印砂浆上验证了近期提出的断裂理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型结构（如混凝土坝失效、冰川崩解）的断裂由自重主导，但因尺度巨大，难以直接观测裂纹萌生与扩展位置；既有实验多依赖离心力模拟自重，需专用离心设备，成本与门槛较高。

Method: 设计一种仅受自重的悬臂梁试件，内含朝下的 V 形缺口；通过合理选择缺口几何、位置及梁尺寸，使裂纹在缺口尖端萌生并以可测量方式扩展。以可 3D 打印的砂浆（3D-printable mortar）演示该自重力断裂测试，并采用 2026 年新提出的断裂理论分析结果。

Result: 该台式测试成功驱动裂纹在缺口尖端萌生并贯穿试件，且测量结果可由所引断裂理论描述；研究结果首次为该理论在体积力（body forces）驱动断裂情形下提供了验证。

Conclusion: 无需离心设施的自重力断裂实验可行，为自重主导断裂研究提供简便途径，并首次验证了相关断裂理论在体积力驱动情形下的适用性。

Abstract: Fracture in large-scale structures --- from failing concrete dams to calving glaciers --- is dominated by self-weight. Yet, owing to their immense scale, directly studying when and where cracks nucleate and propagate in these systems under gravity is unfeasible. To date, experimental investigations have largely relied on mimicking self-weight in scaled-down specimens via centrifugal forces, necessitating specialized centrifuge facilities. In this Letter, we introduce a simple benchtop test that bypasses the need for this infrastructure. The method utilizes a cantilever beam containing a downward-pointing V-notch, loaded solely by its self-weight. Through appropriate selection of the notch geometry and placement and the beam dimensions, the setup forces a crack to nucleate at the notch tip and propagate through the specimen in a measurable manner. We demonstrate the utility of this self-weight fracture test on a 3D-printable mortar and analyze the results using the fracture theory recently introduced in (\citeauthor{LPKFG2026}, 2026). \emph{Inter alia}, the findings provide a first validation of the theory for fracture driven by body forces.

</details>
